### Debt map — which increment retires what
Boarding sick-bay `LIGHT·PLACEHOLDER` count + `sb-note` (`boarding/houses/[houseId]/today/page.tsx:106,155–157,435–470`) → **INCR-28** (becomes a real derived read of open admissions; ⚠️ **the `"· sick-bay not subtracted"` gloss at L135 STAYS — it is the OQ5 ruling, not a placeholder**; HM sees count + name, NEVER condition) · boarding "Sickbay referral log" link → route at 25, link flips at 28 · **SC-12 auto-suggest** (app-layer, NEVER a trigger) → **INCR-28**, insert-if-absent DRAFT only, never UPDATE a non-DRAFT row, MATRON gains NO `WASSCE_SETUP_ROLES` · WASSCE setup §4.2/§4.6 banners asserting live sickbay wiring (`wassce/setup/page.tsx:451–470,509–520`) → made real or made honest at 28 · ward/bed/admit-time → 21+22 · clinician/visiting-doctor → 22 · matron + N&MC no. → 21+22 · treatment/drug → 24 · visit history → 22 (24h) + 27 (30-day) · "Hospital ward updates" → 25 · WASSCE deep-dive §7 Sickbay cell → 28 · parent-portal sickbay/Hospital/NHIS cards → **NOT 4.4** (D8).
**Recommend MOVING to backlog:** F3 discipline-pause / WASSCE-window absence exemption (Decision 6) is **boarding × WASSCE with NO sickbay dependency** — parked here by association only. Kofi call.

### Risks
1. **🔴 Third RLS boundary (INCR-23).** D2's per-staff grants must be DB-enforced (BUILD_STACK Decision 5: *"RLS on Postgres enforces this; the application layer cannot bypass it"*) → a `staff_grant_scope` RESTRICTIVE family on a new `app.current_staff_user` GUC mirroring the shipped 19a `parent_scope`/`parent_deny` (`db/sql/policies.sql:258–451`) + a verifier cloned from `scripts/verify-parent-boundary.ts`. The single most novel item in 4.4. App-layer-only grants contradict the constitution AND the surface copy — do not take that shortcut without a Kofi override.
   ✅ **Good news:** `parent_deny` is catalog-driven, so **every new sickbay table is parent-denied with zero edits** — the correct default for all of them (D8).
2. **🔴 The attendance-M path loses data if built naively.** (a) `uniq_attendance_student_day` + `onConflictDoUpdate` overwrite → a matron's 09:14 MEDICAL is silently replaced by a teacher's 10:00 save (D4 guard fixes at the root). (b) `saveAttendance` refuses writes into a CLOSED term — the sickbay writer must respect that too. (c) **`attendance_record.class_id` is NOT NULL**, and the 240 synthetic F3 WASSCE candidates (`db/seed/wassce.ts:222`) have **no `classId`/house/residency** — so **Y. Aidoo, the canonical cross-module demo case, cannot receive an attendance row at all** and can never appear in the boarding sick-bay count. Fix the seed or honestly skip classless students — **decide before INCR-22, not during INCR-28's demo.**
   (d) Lucy B-item: the surface's **`5 periods · all classes` is impossible** — `uniq_attendance_student_day` means per-period attendance does not exist. Omit or re-express as a day mark.
3. **🟠 "Every read is audited" is a volume/perf trap.** `lib/db/audit.ts` logs mutations only, but 8 of the 11 audit rows on the register are View/Export. Naive per-read logging = write amplification on a hot path + an audit table outgrowing the data. Recommend: audit **care-plan detail opens only**, never the list view, deduped one row per (actor × student × day). Kofi/Dex.
4. **🟠 Medical PII leaks by PROXIMITY, not just by field** (Lucy, sharp): the bed tile prints `SCD` beside a name; the setup stock row reads `Hydroxyurea 500mg · for Adwoa Mensa` — **a diagnosis leaking through an inventory table**. Sarah must gate adjacency, not just column lists. The deliberate non-disclosure copy (*"medical detail withheld"*, *"'M' not 'A'"*, *"Diagnosis stays inside the sickbay module"*) **is the product — preserve verbatim.**
5. **🟠 Boarding in-House conflict (NEW, Lucy):** OQ5 ruled sick-bay is NOT subtracted from in-House (sickbay is on-site) — but a **referred-out** student genuinely IS off-campus. Sickbay-admitted ≠ referred-out. Kofi must split the two before INCR-28.
6. **🟠 5 prod-pastes of the most sensitive data yet.** A missed paste leaks medical PII across schools. Sarah gates paste parity per increment; module close re-runs `db/sql/verify-prod-rls.sql`.
7. **🟡 Mode C is ~half the market.** ~49% of public SHS have no sickbay. `REFERRAL_ONLY` must be first-class from INCR-21 (referrals + chronic + notifications, no beds/rounds/admissions) — empty-by-design, not broken. Quinn AC at 21, not discovered at 25.
8. **🟡 Scope ceilings (Decision 12):** 24 stock items + reorder points, nothing more. Not a pharmacy, not an EMR. Vitals timeline is a log, not a monitor. No diagnosis vocabulary beyond `chronic_condition_enum`.
9. **🟡 Seed drift blocks the demo (Lucy: 15 fabricated items).** Surfaces name **Kufuor/Nkrumah** Houses (seeded: Aggrey·Guggisberg·Fraser·Slessor·Kingsley·Aryee); **Adwoa Mensa** (the SCD anchor on 4 of 5 surfaces) doesn't exist (seed has *Abena Mensah*); **two Aidoos** — `ASK-23-0007 Yaw` (MALE, DAY) vs `SHS-2023-0817 Yaa` (FEMALE, ON_MEDICAL); the referral log prints `SHS-2023-0817`, so the sickbay case is the **WASSCE** Aidoo. Also **4 different Slessor housemasters**, 4 student-code formats, **3 contradictory round schedules** (which proves rounds must be config-driven). Kofi/Lucy to settle before INCR-28.
10. **🟡 `student_health_record.studentId` carries a GLOBAL `.unique()`** (`db/schema/students.ts:250`) not the tenant-scoped idiom — harmless with UUIDs, but it is *why* chronic conditions need their own table (one health record per student; a student can carry two chronic conditions). Wells note.

### INCR-21 · Kofi rulings + AC (2026-07-21) · Lucy map: `docs/senior/sickbay-setup-surface-map.md` — F0 spine, migration **0056**
**Scope:** `sickbay-setup` §1 (mode · clinical staff · health prefects) + §2 (beds · hours/rounds) + §5 policy anchors (static). §3 stock→INCR-24, §4 hospitals→INCR-25 — **absent entirely, no shell/badge/anchor target.** NO visits, NO patients, NO clinical PII beyond the matron's N&MC number. **No split** (smaller than INCR-8 which shipped whole); splitting would cost a 2nd prod paste of the same table family.
- **R1 naming** — module-wide singular `sickbay_*`: `sickbay_settings`/`sickbay_bed`/`sickbay_schedule_slot` (repo idiom over BUILD_STACK's mixed plurals; unprefixed `referrals`/`drug_stock` would collide with billing/pharmacy).
- **R2 one sickbay per school** — `sickbay_settings.school_id NOT NULL UNIQUE` (the `boarding_settings` singleton idiom); **NO child carries `sickbay_id`** (it would be a derived duplicate of `school_id` + a 2nd composite FK on every table; no boarding table carries a `boarding_settings_id`).
- **R3/R4 mode is an AFFORDANCE filter, never a data filter** — one schema, all three modes, no row deleted/hidden/migrated on switch (BUILD_STACK L588 verbatim). **A and B are capability-IDENTICAL** (differ in editorial copy only — do NOT gate anything on A-vs-B in 4.4). **C (REFERRAL_ONLY) disables:** beds, isolation, admissions, schedule slots, medication rounds, visiting doctor, standing orders, drug stock. **C KEEPS:** visit record (disposition REFER/DISCHARGE, never ADMIT), chronic register, referrals, parent notifications, health prefects, policy anchors.
- **R5 Mode C renders empty-by-design with the reason NAMED** — §2 absent from the DOM entirely; ONE explanatory panel (no on-site beds/rounds; prefects are first response; cases route to referral hospitals). **Never `0/0`, never `—`, never a disabled control, never a PLACEHOLDER badge.** §1 promotes the prefect card ABOVE clinical staff (in C the prefects ARE the front line). Risk 7 discharged at 21, not discovered at 25.
- **R6 mode changes are lossless + reversible** — B→C retains every bed/slot row (unread, not deleted); C→B returns identical bed numbers. **No `frozen_at`** — a sickbay is live operational config (do NOT copy the WASSCE freeze). Guard landing at 22: reject →REFERRAL_ONLY while an admission is open.
- **R7 bed-as-a-row, own table, NOT `boarding_bunk`** — 3 independent reasons: bunks are dormitory/House-scoped (a sickbay bed has no House); `students.current_bunk_id` is one-per-student but a patient occupies her dorm bunk AND a sickbay bed simultaneously (reuse would vacate her dorm bunk and silently corrupt the boarding in-House count); sickbay occupancy is hours-scale and belongs to an admission.
- **R8 `bed_number smallint`, `UNIQUE(school_id,bed_number)`, STABLE FOR LIFE** — retiring bed 4 never renumbers 5→4 (a visit record saying "bed 3" must still mean that bed); next = `max+1`. **R9 `is_isolation` boolean; the two pools NEVER merge** (surface: *"The two numbers can't pool"*) — a full general pool does not overflow into isolation.
- **R10 bed counts DERIVED from rows — NO `capacity_beds`/`isolation_beds` scalars** (BUILD_STACK carries both models; a stored count that can disagree with the rows is the STPSHS-matrix failure in miniature). **R11 capacity edit = target reconcile, never delete** — decrease deactivates highest-numbered UNOCCUPIED beds; if unreachable without deactivating an occupied bed the WHOLE save is rejected, named error, no partial application.
- **R13 canonical slot set = setup §02's 7 rows VERBATIM** — the setup surface IS the config spine, so `today`'s 17:00 round and the chronic register's 13:00 column are demo drift and LOSE. **The canonical medication rounds are THREE: 06:30 / 12:30 / 21:00** (INCR-24 fires off exactly these; the 4th is dropped, not seeded). Descriptions are STORED (*"when the matron is on leave the description is the handoff document"*).
- **R14 day applicability = `days_of_week` ISO set + `runs_on_holidays` — REJECT reusing `boardingDayTypeEnum`** (it answers "what rhythm is today", not "which days does this slot run", and literally cannot express `Thursdays` or `Every day · 365` — forcing it would silently drop the visiting doctor's weekday). One pure formatter reproduces all 5 surface labels; **never store the label beside the set.**
- **R15 `sickbay_slot_kind` enum** (`MEDICATION_ROUND|CLINIC|DOCTOR_VISIT|ON_CALL`) — else INCR-24 must string-match labels to find rounds.
- **R16 anchor semantics** — exactly ONE per school (partial unique index, the `uniq_mock_exam_predictor` idiom); kind MUST be MEDICATION_ROUND; **time IS editable** (05:45 is still anchored); cannot be deleted/deactivated/re-kinded/un-anchored and none can be promoted; **must be ≤ every other medication round's start** (else "morning round" sorts after evening and INCR-24's ordering is nonsense). A/B must have one; C has none.
- **R17 NO `operating_hours_json`/`visiting_doctor_schedule_json`** — both describe facts the slot rows already hold relationally; two mechanisms for one fact is exactly how the 3 contradictory schedules got into the surfaces.
- **R18/R19 authz — `SICKBAY_ROLES=[ADMIN,HEADMASTER,MATRON]` (read), `SICKBAY_CONFIG_WRITE_ROLES=[ADMIN,HEADMASTER]` (write §1/§2).** **The MATRON READS but CANNOT WRITE §1/§2** — not mode, not beds, not schedule (she must see her own hours + bed inventory). §3 flips to `[ADMIN,MATRON]` at INCR-24. Grounding: the surface's sidebar footer is the Headmaster on §1/§2/§4/§5 and switches to the Matron on §3 alone — *intentional and load-bearing*. HOUSEMASTER is NOT a member (HM reach is grant-scoped at INCR-23, never role-scoped). Footer renders the ACTING user, never a hardcoded name.
- **R20 Assistant Matron / School Nurse = the same `MATRON` role**, distinguished only by which pointer holds them (`matron_user_id`→"Senior Matron", `assistant_matron_user_id`→"Assistant Matron"). No seniority column, no `sickbay_staff` table, no new role. Both nullable; both must hold MATRON in that school — validated at the APP layer, never a cross-table DB trigger (the boarding "Kofi trap J3" precedent).
- **R21 the visiting doctor is NOT a system user** — `visiting_doctor_name`/`_affiliation` text + the DOCTOR_VISIT slot. No `ref_user`, no `role_assignment`, no invite, no login. An account for a 3-hour-a-week external clinician creates a tenant identity, a password, an audit actor and a leaving problem, for a name on a card. His clinical artefacts are attributed at 22/23 as *recorded external actors* (name+affiliation stamped, authored by the transcribing matron).
- **R22 N&MC = 2 columns on `staff_profile`** beside the shipped `ntc_licence_*` — **reject the generalised `licence_body` refactor**: a teacher-turned-matron holds BOTH licences and one generalised triple cannot hold two. Sarah note: an N&MC number is a **public statutory-register credential, NOT medical PII**; it is the only new "clinical" datum in 0056.
- **R23 health-prefect roster = DERIVED READ of `boarding_bunk.prefect_role='SICKBAY'`** — **reject BUILD_STACK's `school_health_prefect_student_ids JSONB`: an id array is un-FK-able, so it cannot carry the composite `(school_id,id)` tenant FK the schema rests on → a foreign-school student id could be written in with nothing to stop it. A tenant-isolation hole for a display list.** 6 marked bunks (one per House) yields the surface's *"6 students · covers all 6 houses"* with real referential integrity + zero new storage. **OMIT** seniority/training-date/rotation (no backing). Zero marked bunks → honest empty state pointing at Boarding. ⚠️ **Supersedes Lucy N30b** (she proposed a roster table); Kofi rules derive-now, and escalates the day-student gap as E3.
- **R24 frozen contract `lib/sickbay/{config,defaults}.ts`** in the `getExeatPolicy`/`getScheduleTemplate` idiom — server-only reader + pure DB-free unit-tested defaults. `getSickbayConfig` / `getScheduleSlots` / `getRoundSchedule(→MEDICATION_ROUND subset, anchor first)`. **FROZEN:** every type/field name, the 3 getter signatures, both enum value sets, `bedNumber` as bed identity, R16's anchor semantics. **LIVE:** mode, staff pointers, doctor, bed rows, slot times/labels/days/staffing. `capabilities` is DERIVED from mode by a pure function — **never stored, never hand-set.**
- **R25 a missing config row coalesces to `REFERRAL_ONLY` + `configured:false`** — never null, never fabricating capacity. Defaulting to FULL-with-8-beds would assert clinical capacity a school never declared; REFERRAL_ONLY is both the safe default and the statistical one (~49%).
- **R26 policy anchors are FROZEN CONSTANTS, not `notes_json`** — identical static editorial for every school; a per-school free-text JSON column invites per-tenant drift in a regulatory citation (LI 683, 1971).
- **R27 OMIT (no placeholder, no zero, no stub):** `Currently occupied 1/8 · Adwoa Mensa · bed 3 · since 09:14` → **omit the whole tile** (no admission table exists; even `0/8` is a hardcoded assertion about an entity the system cannot see) · `Avg. weekly load 2.4` → omit (needs a semester of history; `0.0` asserts a *measured* zero) · staff status pills `On shift`/`Off · back 18:00` → omit (no shift roster), render a static role pill instead · `11 years here` → omit · prefect seniority/training/rotation → omit · §3/§4/the 3-tier notification table/`Save & acknowledge` → **absent entirely** · sub-nav shows **only `Setup`** (omit-not-fake applies to navigation). ⚠️ Doctor pill `Tomorrow` MAY render (derivable from the DOCTOR_VISIT `daysOfWeek`).
- **R28 the mode percentages (~16/~30/~59%) ship VERBATIM as static copy** — they do NOT sum to 105%: the third is a % of the ~49% *without* a sickbay, a different denominator (matches BUILD_STACK L588). Never computed, never stored.
- **Forward rulings:** **R29** sickbay-admitted ≠ referred-out — OQ5's "sick-bay NOT subtracted from in-House" STANDS (on-site) and its gloss stays; a **referred-out** student IS subtracted, same bucket as an exeat. Two states, two treatments (binds 25/28). **R30** `5 periods · all classes` → re-express as a single day mark (`uniq_attendance_student_day`; don't escalate to per-period attendance for a copy string) (binds 22). **R31** F3 discipline-pause/WASSCE-window exemption → **move to backlog** (boarding × WASSCE, no sickbay dependency) — confirmed.
**AC (Quinn) A1–A8 · B1–B7 · C1–C9 · D1–D7 · E1–E6 · F1–F6 · G1–G3.** Highlights: A2 (FULL≡FIRST_AID capabilities) · A5 (no row → REFERRAL_ONLY/configured:false, no throw, no null) · A6 (B→C→B returns identical rows) · A7 (Mode C: §2 absent from DOM, no 0/—/disabled/placeholder) · B4 (retire bed 4 → next is 9) · B6 (`sickbay_bed_tenant_uk` authored in 0056 though nothing references it until 0057) · C3 (2nd anchor fails the partial unique) · C5 (anchor time editable, but rejected if later than another round) · C7 (`Thursdays` — a value `boarding_day_type` cannot express, proving non-reuse) · C8 (22:00→06:00 midnight wrap ACCEPTED) · D4 (doctor has NO ref_user/role_assignment/invite) · D7 (zero marked bunks → honest empty, not a count of 6) · E2/E3 (MATRON read-only incl. direct POST) · E5 (FORCE+tenant_isolation+auto parent_deny, verify don't assume) · F2 (the omit-strings appear NOWHERE in the rendered surface) · G1 (**from-empty `db:migrate` replay is a manual gate step** — no CI does it) · G2 (0056 = exactly 2 enums + 3 tables + 2 columns, nothing clinical).
**🔶 OWNER-ESCALATE:** **E1** Mode-C staff-card title (no surface renders Mode C) — recommend `School Health Coordinator · SHEP`, same `matron_user_id` pointer + MATRON role; **non-blocking, defaults to the recommendation.** **E2** six BUILD_STACK amendments (see below). **E3** the R23 derivation means a **DAY STUDENT cannot be a health prefect** — fine for Asankrangwa (720 boarders/480 day) and the boarding-prefect tradition, but possibly wrong for a **day-heavy Mode-C school, which is exactly where prefects ARE the entire clinical front line.** Recommend ship the derivation now; if day students must be eligible that is a small `sickbay_health_prefect` table at a later increment, NOT a change to 0056.
### INCR-21 · Build + gate outcomes (PR #172 — awaiting owner merge) — ⚠️ prod deploy: hand-paste `prod-paste-0056-sickbay-spine.sql`
- **Commits:** Wells schema/migration/RLS `a4e7e90` · Claude Code impl `b7796cd` · gate rework `138fb4a` · Dex D1/D2 `a67db6e`. Migration **0056** = exactly 2 enums + 3 tables + 2 columns (AC G2). Files: `db/schema/sickbay.ts`, `db/sql/{policies.sql,prod-paste-0056-sickbay-spine.sql}`, `lib/sickbay/{defaults.ts(+test),config.ts}`, `lib/actions/sickbay-config.ts`, `db/seed/sickbay.ts`, `app/(app)/senior/sickbay/setup/page.tsx`, `components/sickbay/setup-console.tsx`, `lib/access.ts`, `scripts/rls-test.ts`.
- **Gates ALL GREEN:** Quinn **GREEN (sustained)** — AC A1–G3; **14 mutants run, all killed** after the rework; anchor ordering proven closed on **four** distinct paths through real server actions; A6 losslessness proven live *including a Mode-C staff save*; copy compared **character-exact** (`===`, not regex) against the surface HTML; 536 tests. Dex **APPROVE** — frozen contract matches R24, client/server seam **STRUCTURAL** (`defaults.ts` is import-free so it cannot regress into a data module; `config.ts` is `server-only`), portability clean (no trigger, no vendor primitive), D2 truth table walked across all 3 branches. Sarah **CLEAR-TO-MERGE** — attacker probe run as the **non-superuser `omnischools_app` role**: unset GUC / forged school id / claimed-parent session / cross-tenant INSERT / **row-relocation `UPDATE ... SET school_id=<foreign>`** all rejected (the `WITH CHECK` means a school cannot *export* a row either); `authorizeWrite()` is the first statement of all 6 mutations; no IDOR (slot ids re-resolved server-side + explicit predicate + RLS = 3 layers; bed ids never client-supplied); no new dep, no secret, no `sql.raw`.
- **🔴 3 gate BLOCKERS found + closed (`138fb4a`):** (1) **a Mode-C school could NEVER finish setup** — save enabled only on a mode *change*, but a fresh school already coalesces to `REFERRAL_ONLY` → button permanently disabled, `configuredAt` never written, `configured:false` forever for **~49% of the market**, while the page rendered "Mode C · selected" with a gold dot on a school that declared *nothing* (inverting the exact distinction R25 created `configured` to preserve). Found **independently by both gates** (Dex REQUIRED-B == Quinn MINOR-2). (2) **anchor-ordering enforced on 1 path of 4** — R16 states a property of the SET but the check fired only when the edited slot WAS the anchor; a non-anchor round could be moved earlier, or the anchor moved past an *inactive* round which was then toggled on (Quinn found a 4th: *edit* a parked round earlier while off, then toggle on). Replaced by one pure set-level `validateRoundOrdering(slotsAfterChange)` called from **both** writers. (3) **`getRoundSchedule` returned INACTIVE rounds** — a round the headmaster switched off would still fire at INCR-24; fixed now because changing a frozen getter's semantics later is a cross-increment break.
- **🔴 Dex D1/D2 (2nd round) → FIXED (`a67db6e`):** **D1** the doctor fields' absent-means-unchanged semantic was half-applied — `matronUserId`/`assistantMatronUserId` still treated absence as CLEAR while the schema declared all four `.nullish()`, so a POST of just `{visitingDoctorName}` would wipe both matron pointers (unreachable from today's UI, but the first partial editor added in 22–28 hits it). Both moved to `.nullable()` → absence **rejected at the trust boundary**. **D2** the audit `after` had become a *patch* while `before` stayed a *snapshot*, so an auditor could not distinguish "doctor unchanged" from "doctor cleared" — live on every Mode-C staff save. DB write stays a patch; `recordAudit` now gets the effective snapshot (AC E4 restored).
- **🟢 Tooling fix (Quinn):** `db:rls-test` used **exact** seeded counts, so every legitimate seed addition (INCR-21's 2 MATRON assignments) turned it permanently RED — at which point a real isolation regression hides among standing failures. Now `MIN_OWN_ROWS`/`assertAtLeast` (a **liveness floor**); the two directions that actually prove isolation (foreign school id, unset GUC) stay **exact-zero**. Proven to still go RED by widening `tenant_isolation` to `USING(true)` on `sickbay_bed`, then restored.
- **Honest omissions (omit-not-fake):** `Currently occupied` + `Avg. weekly load` tiles omitted **entirely** — no admission/visit table exists, so even `0 / 8` asserts something about an entity the system cannot see (a *false zero*, distinct from a known-zero). Staff shift pills, `11 years here`, prefect seniority/training/rotation omitted (no backing). §3/§4/the 3-tier notification table absent entirely — no shell, no badge, no anchor target. Sub-nav shows only `Setup`.
- **🟠 INCR-24 obligations (recorded, deliberately NOT fixed — they only bite once 0059's medication tables carry `(school_id, slot_id)`):** (a) **`resetScheduleSlots` is a hard DELETE** that changes every slot id → must become a reconcile/update-in-place, or a "Reset to defaults" cascades away medication-administration rows against the append-only MAR rule. (b) **`validateRoundOrdering` TOCTOU** — the slot set is read *outside* the write tx (`sickbay-config.ts:307,365`), so two concurrent admins can both validate against pre-change state and commit a violating set; fix is a `FOR UPDATE` read inside `withSchool`. (c) a future **slot-create** action must refuse a second anchor (`validateRoundOrdering` takes `rounds.find(isAnchor)`; the partial unique index is the only guard today). (d) the B5 reject branch stays unit-test-only until INCR-22 supplies real `occupiedBedIds` — grep `planBedReconcile(config.beds, parsed.data, [])`.
- **🟠 Sarah advisories for INCR-22–28 (none block 21):** (1) **the data-minimisation argument EXPIRES at INCR-23** — today every reader holds `SICKBAY_ROLES` so prop-trimming is hygiene, but 23 introduces grant-scoped HOUSEMASTER reach into health data and "the reader is privileged anyway" becomes false; make the Mode-C gating pattern (`page.tsx:78-88`) the rule before the first clinical prop crosses. (2) **`holdsMatronRole` is the ONLY tenancy guard on the matron pointers** — `ref_user` is global so the DB cannot stop a foreign user id; every future clinical actor pointer (attending clinician, prescriber) needs the same explicit app-layer check, and where the target is a *tenant* row use a composite `(school_id,id)` FK instead. (3) `days_of_week` has no DB CHECK — fine while `updateScheduleSlot` is the only writer; a bulk import/template-apply path must route through `SlotSchema` or add a CHECK. (4) `AUTH_DEV_BYPASS` must stay false in prod or the audit actor on the product's most sensitive module is untrustworthy. (5) `getHealthPrefects` returns raw student UUIDs as React keys — harmless now, a cross-reference handle if that component is reused under a lower read gate.
- **🟡 Non-blocking:** `configuredAt` is overwritten on every mode save, so the column no longer answers "first configured when" (`configured` boolean is unaffected) — use `coalesce(configured_at, now())` if the first-time answer matters. `validateRoundOrdering` compares times ignoring `daysOfWeek` (a Sunday-only 05:00 round is rejected against a weekday 06:30 anchor) — conservative and consistent with `roundSchedule`'s global ordering; INCR-24 may want per-day. Mode-C `form` staleness is prevented only by an *accidental* remount (the card-order swap changes React's index-based reconciliation) — if someone keys those children or drops the swap, a C→B switch could render an empty doctor input over a stored doctor (non-destructive by construction since absent ≠ cleared, but the protection is incidental).
- **Quinn's ruling on the compensating control:** no DOM test stack. The save rule is a pure `canSaveMode()` with 5 asserted cases; selection state / gold dot / payload contents were proven from the **served HTML + flight payload** (the real build serving real props — better than jsdom); the residual gap is 3 non-branching wiring lines whose failure mode is now a *no-op* rather than data loss. **Condition to revisit:** the first client component in this module holding state that can diverge from its server props and be written back (optimistic update, multi-step wizard).

## INCR-21 ✅ MERGED (PR #172, `a4e7e90`+`b7796cd`+`138fb4a`+`a67db6e`) — Sickbay F0 spine · migration 0056 · ⚠️ prod-paste `prod-paste-0056-sickbay-spine.sql` RUN by owner 2026-07-21

### INCR-22 · Kofi rulings R32–R67 + AC (2026-07-21) · Lucy map: `docs/senior/sickbay-visit-surface-map.md` — THE TRUNK, size XL, **SPLITS 22a/22b/22c**, migration **0057**
**Scope:** `visit-record` §01 (identity + vitals timeline) §02 (assessment + consult) §04 (disposition + criteria) + `today` §01 (queue + bed board). Tables: `sickbay_visit`, `sickbay_vital_reading`, `sickbay_admission`, `sickbay_doctor_consult`.

**🔴 R67 SPLIT — three gated slices, ONE milestone branch, ONE migration, ONE prod paste** (the seam is where no migration crosses, so it costs the owner zero extra hand-pastes — the objection that kept INCR-21 whole does not apply):
| Slice | Contents | Migration |
|---|---|---|
| **22a** | 0057 (2 enums + 4 tables) + RLS/policies/prod-paste, `lib/sickbay/{visits,vitals}.ts`, the visit write path (create·begin·assess·dispose·admit·discharge·void·consult), visit-record §01/§02/§04, R56 mode guard, R59 real `occupiedBedIds` | **0057** |
| **22b** | `lib/attendance/mark.ts` extraction + the D4 guard + the **R49b SMS/audit fix**, `lib/sickbay/medical-hold.ts`, push+pull, R50 label, R51 correction fix, the R53 seed fix, the §04 attendance line | none |
| **22c** | `today` §01 (live strip, admitted block, queue, bed board) — pure derived reads. **+ today §03 "Recent visits · 24h"** (Lucy's boundary gap: assigned to NO increment on the board yet it is the ONLY route into a visit record; needs only 0057) | none |
🔴 **22a and 22b MUST reach main TOGETHER.** 22a alone ships an admission that does not excuse attendance — a student in a bed gets marked ABSENT by her teacher and her mother gets an SMS saying so. That interim state must never exist in production. 22c may merge separately. 22b/22c branch **independently off the milestone head, never stacked** (orphan-PR rule).

**A · lifecycle:** **R32** NO status column — state derived from 4 timestamps (`presented_at`/`started_at`/`disposition`+`disposition_at`/`voided_at` + the admission's `discharged_at`) via a pure `visitState()` in `lib/sickbay/visits.ts`; a stored enum can disagree with its own timestamps (R10 again). **R33** queue = not voided, not started, no disposition, presented today; the wait clock stops at **Begin visit**, not at assessment. **R34** disposition preconditions: `started_at` set · complaint non-empty · attending holds MATRON (`holdsMatronRole` reused) · **`working_impression` required for ADMIT and REFER only** — a routine discharge is Tier-1 non-event and forcing prose on a 40-second dressing change produces garbage, not a record. **R35** ADMIT + admission row are ONE transaction. **R36** disposition IMMUTABLE at 22 (ADMIT→REFER escalation → INCR-25 as a referral *event*, never a mutation). **R37** nothing hard-deleted; a visit is VOIDABLE **only while open** (`disposition IS NULL`) — an open visit on the wrong student is an *active attendance-coercion source*. **R38** recorded external actors (Sick Bay Prefect at intake, visiting doctor) are **TEXT, never identity pointers** — an FK to the prefect student would place one student's identity as an *actor* inside another student's clinical record.
**B · authz:** **R39** clinical WRITE = `[MATRON]` only (not ADMIN, not HEADMASTER — he reads but must never author an impression). **R40** clinical READ at 22 = **`SICKBAY_CLINICAL_READ_ROLES = [HEADMASTER, MATRON]`; ADMIN keeps module access and gets NO clinical detail** — INCR-23 EXTENDS this gate (`role ∈ READ || hasGrant(actor, student)`) rather than replacing it. ⚠️ **Lucy Q2 independently found the same thing and rates it build-blocking:** reusing the shipped `SICKBAY_ROLES` (which includes ADMIN) as the read gate hands the proprietor/IT account every impression, vital and complaint — contradicting owner D2. ADMIN's props must be **server-side trimmed**, not conditionally rendered (Sarah INCR-21 advisory 1 lands here). **R41** the HOUSEMASTER boundary is a **field-limited FUNCTION SIGNATURE**, not a grant, and lives at INCR-28: a separate reader returning `{studentId, studentName, admittedAt}` — structurally incapable of returning a complaint. Do NOT build a general `getAdmissions()` that 28 can reuse. **R42** D2 sub-ruling ANSWERED: the acute visit record is NOT tiered at 22 (mental-health content is unclassifiable free text); at **23** the HEADMASTER default does **NOT** extend to `MENTAL_HEALTH` chronic records (grant-only) — a longitudinal psychiatric label is not operational information for a headmaster.
**C · `working_impression` NOT `diagnosis` (R43):** the string `diagnos` appears in **no** column/enum/type/zod-key/label/route anything 0057 ships (grep-testable). Only structured clinical vocabulary in all of 4.4 is `chronic_condition_enum` (7 values, INCR-23). At 22 every clinical assertion is free text. No ICD/SNOMED/code-list/picker. `red_flags_screened` stays **free text** — structuring it into a checkable screen is an EMR feature. `escalation_triggers` is **inert**: stored, never evaluated.
**D · vitals (R44/R45):** 5 measures + pain, fixed units (**no `unit` column** — a second fact that can disagree), all nullable with ≥1 required, plausibility bounds in zod as typo guards (temp 25–45, sys 50–260, dia 30–160, pulse 20–250, SpO₂ 50–100, pain 0–10). **Append-only: no update, no delete, no void, no `updated_at`.** **ZERO derived alerting at 22** — cell colours/pills are presentation from a pure DB-free helper; trend deltas are arithmetic; anything that *acts* on a value is surveillance → INCR-27.
**E · 🔴 THE ATTENDANCE HOOK (22b) — writes into shipped Basic-tier code:** **R46** fires on **ADMIT and REFER, NEVER on DISCHARGE** — the 20-minute headache gets no `M` (every attendance claim on every surface attaches to an *admission*; a routine visit is not an attendance event, and it must not become a row every class teacher can read). **R47** ONE mark per student per day (R30 discharged — no per-period attendance), civil date Africa/Accra, **today only, never backdated**. **R48** multi-day needs **NO scheduler**: **PUSH** at disposition writes today (upgrading an already-taken `ABSENT`), **PULL** = the shared writer coerces on **INSERT only** when the student is under an open medical hold — so day 2/3/4 are marked the moment the teacher takes the register. One predicate `medicalHoldStudentIds()` = open admission covering the date **OR** open visit presented that date (which correctly handles the 07:30 clinic visit still open at the 08:00 register). INCR-25 extends the same function with the open-referral arm and no caller changes. **R49** the shared writer is `lib/attendance/mark.ts`, **`server-only` and emphatically NOT `"use server"`** (every export of a `"use server"` module is a remotely callable endpoint). (a) the downgrade guard is a **DB `setWhere`** on the existing `onConflictDoUpdate` — caller-agnostic by construction. (b) **🔴 R49b LIVE DEFECT:** `saveAttendance` derives `absent` (→ parent SMS → audit `after.absent`) from `d.entries`, the teacher's **input**. With the guard in place the row write is correctly blocked **and the mother still gets "Adwoa was marked absent" while Adwoa is on bed 3 in the school's own sickbay** — the exact harm this module exists to prevent, shipped by a guard that "worked". Fix: `.returning()` on each upsert; SMS + audit computed from **effective** statuses. (c) the **closed-term check MOVES INTO** the shared writer; the **edit-window lock STAYS** in `saveAttendance` (a teacher-register concept — a matron admitting at 14:00 into a 30-hour-old register must not be blocked), safe only because of R47. **R50** `reason_code='SICKBAY'` is **NOT teacher-pickable** (a teacher who could select it could forge a clinical assertion) — add `SICKBAY → "In sickbay"` to the label map only. **Location, never condition** — a teacher who does not know stops chasing a child who cannot come. **R51** the `M` is **NEVER reverted** by the sickbay — not on discharge, not on error; correction is the existing co-signed `attendance_correction` flow only. Voiding cannot touch attendance **by construction** (void is legal only while `disposition IS NULL`, and only ADMIT/REFER write). ⚠️ `decideCorrection` must **clear `reason_code`** when approving a change away from MEDICAL, else the row reads incoherently; and because coercion is INSERT-only, **the co-signed correction always wins**. **R52** closed term → clinical write COMMITS, mark SKIPPED, reason `CLOSED_TERM`. **R53 🔴 CLASSLESS:** skip the mark, **never block the visit** (a sick child is treated first), **never go silent** (honest line on the disposition card + `attendance_mark_skipped` audit row). `class_id` stays **NOT NULL** — relaxing a shipped Basic-tier constraint for a seed defect is backwards. **It IS a seed defect and it is fixed in 22b**: `db/seed/wassce.ts:223–237` creates 246 candidates with no class/house/residency (measured: non-WASSCE **28/28** classed, WASSCE **0/246**), so not one can receive an attendance row and Y. Aidoo cannot demo the headline integration; ~15 lines reusing the `wassce-mock.ts:133` class-upsert idiom, **scoped to the `SHS-2023-*` marker rows only**. **R54** the attendance write is **BEST-EFFORT and OUTSIDE the clinical transaction** — the visit is the medico-legal record and must survive an attendance subsystem having a bad day. The one place atomicity is deliberately rejected.
**F · mode:** **R55** Mode C ADMIT refused at the **SERVER**, not hidden in the UI; bed board absent from the DOM. **R56** R6's deferred guard lands — reject →REFERRAL_ONLY while ANY admission is open, named error in the R11 grammar (open *visits* do not block; only beds-in-use do). **R57** `admission.is_isolation` **MUST equal** `bed.is_isolation` — isolation is a property of the CASE, so no judgment call and no overflow either way. **R58** three exclusivity invariants by **PARTIAL UNIQUE INDEX, not app check** (app checks lose the concurrent double-admit race): one open admission per bed · per student · one open visit per student. **R59** `occupiedBedIds` stops being `[]` → the INCR-21 B5 reject path becomes reachable and gets a real **integration** test.
**G · doctor consult (R60):** separate artefact; `clinician_name` **copied onto the row** (a locum/ER registrar is not the settings value, and the record must keep who actually said it); it is **hearsay with attribution** (render "recorded by {matron}"); **it cannot be a co-sign or a gate** — an unauthenticated external actor cannot be an authorisation subject (forward: INCR-24's `Doctor-ordered` MAR tag records **provenance, never permission**); append-only, corrected by a second row.
**H · omit-not-fake (R61/R62/R63/R65):** OMITTED entirely — SCD protocol banner + `View care plan` · `Sickle cell · HbSS` chip · **the queue's `No chronic flag` column (a false NEGATIVE — it asserts *safety* about a register the system cannot see; worse than a false zero)** · the 4-row discharge checklist + `3 of 4 met` (per-condition templates → 23) · `NHIS · active` · `bed S-12-B` · §03 meds entirely · §05 entirely (notification log, HM card, follow-up, cross-links, immutable-close) · today §01 HM strip · cluster-watch tile · active-referrals tile · **the triage `Routine` pill (R62 — a single-valued enum nobody sets is wrong the first time it matters; a hardcoded pill is an *unassessed clinical-urgency assertion*)** · both print actions. **R63** discharge criteria = **free text** + `expected_discharge_at` + required `overnight_plan` (the "no silent overnight stays" rule preserved at 22; the parent half is 26). **R65** ONE honest attendance line in the §04 disposition card, naming a **day** never periods, and it is where the R52/R53 skip warning lives.
**I · R64 four more BUILD_STACK amendments** (continuing INCR-21's six): (7) `vitals_json` → `sickbay_vital_reading` rows (a JSON blob cannot carry per-reading `taken_by_user_id`, cannot be time-indexed, cannot be append-only). (8) **drop `sickbay_visits.discharged_at`** — superseded by `disposition_at` + the admission's `discharged_at`; two end-stamps for one fact can disagree. (9) `disposition_enum`→`sickbay_disposition`, new `sickbay_consult_mode` (module prefix, R1). (10) `parent_notified_at`/`tier_fired` are **NOT in 0057** — a `tier_fired` column with no chain to fire it is a stub (authored at 0060, built at 26). (11) **no generated `visit_ref`** — it encodes 3 facts already on the row in a format the surfaces contradict four ways; a pure formatter if a printed header needs it.
**⚖️ Kofi ⟂ Lucy divergences — KOFI RULES (spec authority), reasons recorded:** (1) **trigger** — Lucy read "admission only" off the surfaces; **R46 adds REFER** (a student at Asankrangwa Govt. Hospital is definitively not in class and definitively not unauthorised-absent — precisely what the 5th status exists for). The surface simply never draws a referral. (2) **multi-day** — Lucy proposed *backfill-on-discharge*; **R48's push+pull wins** because backfill leaves the register showing wrong data *during* the admission and only corrects it at the end. (3) **0057 size** — Lucy mapped 6 tables + 3 enums (separate assessment + criterion tables); **Kofi's 4 tables + 2 enums wins** (assessment folds into the visit row as columns; the structured criterion instance needs per-condition templates → INCR-23, R63).
**🔒 Lucy's 11 ADJACENCY leaks (A1–A11) — rendering decisions no column list catches; all binding:** **A1** the bed tile printing `SCD` beside a name → **condition removed, duration only** (the map's central ruling). **A2** the live tile `A. Mensa · bed 3 · SCD crisis` → name+bed for ONE patient, **no names at all above one**. **A4** the SCD banner leaks **twice — by name AND by drug** (`hydroxyurea` ⇒ sickle cell). **A6** the queue printing the complaint (`Menstrual cramps`) beside a name **on a bench-side screen** (independent reason the `Print day sheet` action is omitted). **A7 🔴 `attendance_record.note` is the module's ONLY outbound leak path into the Basic tier** → write `null` or a fixed non-clinical string, **never** the complaint or impression. **A11** physical shoulder-surfing is the whole justification — recorded so a future implementer does not "restore" the condition line as a helpful touch.
**AC (Quinn):** **H1–H18** (the attendance hook — the highest-value tests in the module; H6 is the R49b SMS regression, H8 the no-scheduler multi-day proof, H11 classless-must-not-throw, H14 the co-signed correction winning, H17 the full shipped attendance suite passing untouched, H18 zero active students classless after seed) · **V1–V12** lifecycle · **Z1–Z5** authz (Z2: ADMIN's **flight payload** carries no clinical field) · **W1–W4** working-impression ceiling · **T1–T5** vitals · **M1–M8** mode · **D1–D5** consult · **O1–O4** omit-not-fake (O4: `===` character-exact copy comparison, the INCR-21 method) · **S1–S4** structural (**S2: the from-empty replay must confirm `sickbay_visit_tenant_uk` is emitted BEFORE the three composite FKs targeting it — the 0033 hazard INSIDE a single migration**).
**🔶 OWNER-ESCALATE (all defaulted, none blocking):** **E4** triage vocabulary → omit at 22. **E5 🔔 a communication REGRESSION the owner should see coming:** today a student in a sickbay bed is marked ABSENT and her mother gets *"Adwoa was marked absent"*; after 22b she is marked Medical and her mother gets **nothing**, because D8 keeps parents out of sickbay until the Tier-2 chain at **INCR-26**. Default **accept** (silence beats a false accusation) — flagged so it is a decision, not a surprise. **E6** ADMIN cannot read a visit record until INCR-23; for a single-admin school the account that pays for the system cannot open sickbay clinical records for one increment. Default accept — the honest escape is that the *role* carries clinical read, so an admin who really runs the sickbay assigns themselves MATRON. **E7** confirming R42 (mental-health grant-only at 23) since D2 was an owner decision.
**Also found:** shipped-copy defect `PROGRAMME_ABBR.GENERAL_SCIENCE = "GS"` contradicts all three surfaces (`SCI`) — one-line fix that also corrects the already-merged INCR-21 setup surface. `.cluster-note` is used but never defined in the visit-record file (take it from `schoolup-sickbay-setup.html:191`).

### INCR-22a+22b · Build + gate outcomes (PR #174 — awaiting owner merge) — ⚠️ prod deploy: paste `0056` **then** `0057`
- **Commits:** Wells 0057 `0e0d1ad` · 22a visit path `03e0cf1` · 22b attendance hook `95ed619` · gate rework `968d937` · tx un-nesting `e08c042`. **22a+22b merge TOGETHER** (22a alone = an admission that doesn't excuse attendance). **22c still to come.**
- **Gates ALL GREEN:** Quinn **GREEN** (H1–H18 + V/Z/W/T/M/D/O/S; **H5/H6/H8/H14 mutation-proven** — reverting the R49b fix turns 4 checks red *and emits a real SMS*; Z2 proven from **served HTML with a MATRON positive control**, 9/9 tokens vs ADMIN 0/9, so the negative isn't vacuous; closed 2 gaps the shipped harness left — H8's discharge-closes-the-hold and M5's live Mode-C ADMIT refusal; 617 tests). Dex **APPROVE** (dependency direction **correct and confined**; client/server seam **structural**; portability clean; mutation-killed 3 guards itself). Sarah **CLEAR-TO-MERGE** (**12-vector attacker probe as the non-superuser `omnischools_app`** with a positive control: unset GUC · forged school id · claimed-parent session · cross-tenant INSERT ×4 · **row relocation in BOTH directions** · cross-tenant DELETE · foreign-parent composite FK — all refused; `tenant_isolation` carries `WITH CHECK` as well as `USING`, so a school cannot *export* a row either).
- **🔴 The R49b live defect — found by Kofi, fixed and mutation-proven:** `saveAttendance` derived the absent list (→ **parent SMS** → audit) from `d.entries`, the teacher's **input**. With the D4 guard in place the row write is correctly blocked **and the mother of an admitted student still gets "Adwoa was marked absent" while Adwoa is on bed 3 in the school's own sickbay** — the exact harm the module exists to prevent, shipped by a guard that "worked". Now derived from `.returning()` (effective statuses).
- **🔴 Gate BLOCKERS found + closed (`968d937`):** **R1** a *"Referral note (optional)"* textarea was collected → parsed → written into the audit `after`, but **no column stored it** — a matron's referral reasoning invisible forever, on the increment whose premise is "the visit IS the medico-legal record". Closed by **deletion** (Refer is now one-click; the zod key removed so a crafted POST can't smuggle it into `audit_log` either; no column ⇒ no second prod paste; the surface never drew it — 22a invented it). **R2** the H6 block went red **1-in-14** on an unchanged tree. Root-caused a **real UTC-midnight rollover** in the verifier (`civilDate()` is UTC, host is BST → the civil day turns at 01:00 local; the script pinned `today` at module load while the actions compute at call time), **reproduced deterministically** with a new fake-clock harness (red at 2500/2700/2800/3200 ms leads, green at 2600/4000) and fixed (pin once at `main()`, wait out a rollover under 60s, assert the day didn't turn). Shipped as `pnpm db:verify-sickbay-attendance-rollover`.
- **🟠 STANDING WATCH ITEM — the 1-in-14 observation is NOT fully explained.** Dex disproved the rollover as *its* cause: the failing run's H5 stamp was `2026-07-21T23:32:23Z` — **28 minutes** before UTC midnight, not the ~3s window the mechanism needs — and under a rollover **H1 fails before H6 can** (H1/H4/H7 and both H5 checks were green in that log). The audit counters `{absent:1, marked:1, heldMedical:1}` were verbatim tool output yet are arithmetically unreachable for either save in that sequence. **Why it does not block:** the harm path no longer depends on any verifier — the downgrade guard is a **DB `setWhere`** (caller-agnostic by construction) and the writer set is pinned repo-wide by a mutation-killed test. **The discriminator:** if the H6 cluster reddens again **with `pinned == now`**, the rollover is exonerated and it becomes a genuine writer-side investigation. Do not close this without that evidence.
- **Structural guards added (all mutation-killed):** the set of files writing `attendanceRecords` is pinned to exactly `mark.ts` + `decideCorrection` (+ a raw-SQL-name check) · `medical-hold.ts` is asserted to import **nothing** from `lib/attendance/` — the acyclicity is load-bearing because the reverse edge already exists (`visit-reads.ts` imports `civilDate`/`closedTermLabel`) and **INCR-25 is aimed at extending that exact function** · the `diagnos` ceiling now sweeps the 22b files + `db/schema/sickbay.ts` + `0057_*.sql` + the prod paste, and `stripComments` handles trailing `//`/`--` (before this delta a `diagnosisCode: text(...) // …` walked straight through — Dex verified) · `KNOWN_APP_ROLES` validates `AUTH_DEV_ROLES` with a loud throw.
- **🟢 Post-gate fix (`e08c042`, Dex):** `auditSkip` opened its own `withSchool` **inside** the still-open one — holding a pool connection while acquiring another (postgres.js default max 10), the classic exhaustion-deadlock shape, and about to become the idiom for 23–28. The skip reason is now **returned out of the callback and audited after the tx resolves**: smaller diff, same guarantee (nothing is written in either skip branch, so there is no tx to preserve, and the audit stays outside so a failing insert can't downgrade a NAMED reason to `FAILED`).
- **🟠 Sarah's advisories for INCR-23–28:** (1) **the clinical read gate lives at the CALL SITE, not in the read.** Correct today (one call site, gate above the fetch — so ADMIN's clinical query is never *issued*). At 23 the rule becomes **per-row** (`role ∈ READ || hasGrant(actor, student)`) which RLS cannot express, arriving exactly when her INCR-21 "every reader is privileged anyway" assumption dies. **Move the gate into the read function (or a `requireClinicalRead(schoolId, studentId)` seam) at 23, before there are three call sites** — the write path already models this with `authorizeClinicalWrite()`. (2) `attending_user_id`'s app-only tenancy guard (global `ref_user`, so the DB cannot check it — her INCR-21 advisory 2 landing exactly as predicted) **replicates** at 24's prescriber/standing-order pointers: extract one `assertSchoolClinician()` rather than copy-paste `holdsMatronRole`. (3) `referralOnlyGuard`/`planBedReconcile` surface bed numbers + occupancy to ADMIN/HEADMASTER config actions — occupancy only, unavoidable for the invariant; **at 23 the temptation to name the patient in that error must be refused**. (4) the writer-set pin is identifier-shaped — an aliased import evades it (the raw-SQL check still covers the back door).
- **🟠 22c obligations:** **22c must NOT reuse `getVisitRecord`** — it returns complaints/impressions/vitals/consults, so per-row use on the board is simultaneously an N×9 round-trip **and** a Lucy-A6 adjacency leak (a complaint beside a name on a bench-side screen). 22c needs its own **field-limited reader** in the R41 signature shape. Also: 22c must absorb or re-point `/senior/sickbay/visits/new` (authored by 22a as the write path's only entry since the queue is 22c) so it does not survive as a second, unmapped route that bypasses the queue's guards.
- **🟠 Still deferred to INCR-24** (all only bite once 0059's medication tables carry `(school_id, slot_id)`): `resetScheduleSlots` hard DELETE · the `validateRoundOrdering` TOCTOU · a slot-create action must refuse a second anchor · `admitPatient` reads `getSickbayConfig` outside the tx (same TOCTOU shape) · `medical-hold.ts`'s non-sargable `::date` casts + a missing `(school_id, student_id, admitted_at)` index (it runs on **every register save at every Senior school forever**).
- **🎫 Out-of-scope defect spun off to its own ticket** (Quinn verified pre-existing by replaying at `f2a6439`, before any INCR-22 code): `pnpm db:verify-score-ledger` fails 3 assertions on already-merged Module 4.1 code — **a score batch containing an out-of-range cell commits its valid rows anyway** (a silent partial write on the ledger feeding WASSCE projections and report cards), and `setLedgerPath` accepts the unbuilt Path B. Same "standing red hides real regressions" pattern as the RLS-test drift fixed at INCR-21.

## INCR-22a+22b ✅ MERGED (PR #174, `0e0d1ad`+`03e0cf1`+`95ed619`+`968d937`+`e08c042`) — sickbay visit + attendance-M hook · migration 0057 · ⚠️ prod-paste `0056` then `0057` RUN by owner 2026-07-22

### INCR-22c · Kofi rulings R68–R90 + AC (2026-07-22) · Lucy map: `docs/senior/sickbay-today-surface-map.md` — the Today board · **NO migration, NO prod-paste, NO split**
**Scope:** `today` §01 (crumb/h1/lede · live strip · admitted block · queue · bed board) + **§03 "Recent visits · 24h"** — built as a SECTION of `/senior/sickbay/today`, not a second route (R75; `TODAY_PATH` is already every shipped 22a write's revalidation target — the route was pre-agreed in code). §02 rounds→24, §04 referrals→25, §05 outbreak→27: **absent entirely**. Everything derives from 0056/0057; Q1 rides the `sickbay_visit_presented_idx (school_id, presented_at)` authored in 0057 with a comment anticipating exactly this read.
- **R68 the reader — `lib/sickbay/board-reads.ts`, `server-only`, ONE entry** `getSickbayBoard(schoolId, roles, now)`. **O(1) round trips is part of the contract** — ~4–6 selects, independent of row count; any per-row query fails the AC whatever it returns. **`now` is a PARAMETER, never `new Date()` inside the reader or a child component** — pinned once in `page.tsx` and threaded (grounded in the 22a R2 blocker: a clock read at module load vs call time went red 1-in-14 and cost a day; three components each calling `new Date()` can render a queue whose "oldest wait" predates its own oldest row).
- **R69/R87 the field ceiling — four DISJOINT row types, and the ceiling is now ONE sentence:** *the only clinical string anywhere in the board reader is `presenting_complaint`, on the queue projection only.* `SickbayQueueRow` {visitId, studentName, formLabel, houseName, studentCode, presentedAt, **complaint**} · `SickbayWardPatient` {admissionId, visitId, bedNumber, isIsolation, studentName, formLabel, houseName, studentCode, admittedAt, admittedByName, expectedDischargeAt, latestVital{…}, firstPainScore} · `SickbayBedTile` {bedNumber, isIsolation, occupant: null | {studentName, formLabel, houseName, admittedAt}} · `SickbayRecentVisitRow` {visitId, presentedAt, studentName, formLabel, houseName, disposition, dispositionAt}. Every type except the queue is **structurally incapable** of carrying a clinical assertion — A1 stops being a vigilance item.
- **R70 three guards, none of them a comment:** (1) **runtime key-set pin** — `Object.keys(row).sort()` equals a frozen list per type (TS interfaces erase at runtime; the returned object does not; mutation-killable). (2) **one SELECT per type** — no board type may be produced by mapping over another (`beds = ward.map(...)` is banned; that is how a clinical field arrives as a "harmless" spread). (3) **served-HTML token test with a POSITIVE control** (Quinn's Z2 method).
- **🔴 R87 (SUPERSEDES the narrative clause of R69/R85) — `.ab-line`'s clinical prose comes OFF the board.** `SickbayWardPatient` loses `workingImpression`/`hydrationStatus`/`plan`: not selected, not returned, not rendered. **Lucy's A13 wins.** Kofi's own words: *"I deleted three characters (`SCD`) from the bed tile and kept `Mild vaso-occlusive pain crisis · … Hydroxyurea continued` eight lines above it, on the same screen, for the same readers. **A rule that survives only where the string is short is not a rule.**"* Three grounds: (a) his **own R76 test applied consistently** — the A6 exception is bought by *operational necessity*, and narrative prose supports no decision the record one click away doesn't; (b) it is an **A4 leak by construction** — free-text `plan`/`hydrationStatus` are exactly where a matron types drug names, and `hydroxyurea` ⇒ sickle cell, so `.ab-line` is the omitted SCD banner restored in user-typed form on the landing page; (c) **A8 justified it on the role gate, and the role gate is not the threat** — it decides who logs in, not who is standing behind the matron at a bench-side screen (A11). *"A8 answered the wrong question and I ratified it."* **Replacement (slot + dashed border kept):** `Expected discharge **{t}**` · `· **reassessment overdue**` (gold, when passed and `discharged_at` null) · trailing `Open record →`. The line **always renders** (supersedes R85's "no assessment ⇒ no line"). **`.ab-vitals` SURVIVES** — Lucy's A14: the glance-check on a patient ten metres away is a genuine patient-safety instrument, and removing it would push the matron to open full records *more* often, which is worse for exposure overall. The pain trend survives in the Pain tile's own sub-line, where the surface put it.
- **R88 the gate STANDS unamended.** Still `SICKBAY_CLINICAL_READ_ROLES = [HEADMASTER, MATRON]`; ADMIN gets `ClinicalRestricted` and the board is **never fetched**. The page still carries the queue `complaint` (Class 1, Class 4 when reproductive — `Menstrual cramps`) and the Class-3 vitals grid. **Pre-emptively refused:** "the narrative is gone, so give ADMIN a counts-only board" — that is the third read tier R80 rejected; R41 reserves the field-limited tier for INCR-28. ⚠️ **`SickbayBedTile` is now very close to what 28's housemaster reader needs — that is a TRAP, not a convenience.** 28 authors its own narrower reader and must not import from `board-reads.ts`.
- **R81 Sarah's advisory TAKEN for the board** — the gate moves INTO the read: `getSickbayBoard` returns `null` for a non-clinical reader, role check as the **first statement before any query is issued** (preserving the property that makes Z2 strong). At 23 the per-row rule (`role ∈ READ || hasGrant`) is added *inside*, where roles are already in scope. **`getVisitRecord` keeps its call-site gate at 22c** — its `null` already means *not of this school* → `notFound()`; overloading it with *forbidden* turns ADMIN's honest panel into a 404 and deletes the E6 escape. Converting it needs a discriminated result and 23 has the second input (grants) to justify the shape. Quinn pins it meanwhile: **every file importing a clinical reader must also reference `SICKBAY_CLINICAL_READ_ROLES`** (the mutation-killed writer-set-pin idiom).
- **🔴 R76 §03's complaint column comes OFF — corroborated INDEPENDENTLY by Lucy (A12).** A6 buys the complaint on the *queue* with a triage necessity a **closed** visit does not have; nothing on a retrospective log is being decided, and the matron needs to *find* a row, which time+name+form+House+pill does. Lucy's added evidence: three of the surface's six strings in that cell aren't complaints but impressions/readings/condition names (`malaria RDT negative`, `sickle cell pain crisis`, `asthma chronic`, `pain 7/10`) — **six legible at once vs the queue's three**. `.v-action` also dropped (A12b — per-row drug re-identification, and half-unbacked anyway). ⚠️ **Kofi flags this as the ruling most likely to be "helpfully" reverted by a future implementer who sees an empty-looking log — say A11 in the PR.**
- **R75 §03 window = ROLLING 24h from `now` on `presented_at`, NOT the civil date.** The log's job is shift handover, and a civil-date window is **empty at 06:00** — exactly when the incoming matron needs last night's 19:40 referral. One timestamp, one predicate, sargable. **R75b:** the window is NOT widened to catch stale open visits; instead 22c enriches the shipped open-visit collision error (`"This student already has an open sickbay visit."`) to the R11 grammar — name the day, link the record — because now that a board exists there is nowhere to go looking. **R77** disposition pills `Discharged {HH:MM}` / `Admitted` / `Referred` + AUTHORED neutral **`Open`**; `ON_WARD_DISCHARGED` still reads `Admitted` (R36 makes the disposition immutable; the bed board 200px above answers "still on a bed" authoritatively). Open visits ARE included (an IN_PROGRESS visit appears nowhere else on the board). Order `presented_at` desc. **R78** voided visits excluded from §03 and from every counter — a log row naming a student who was never seen is a false clinical adjacency, and a counter including it over-reports care delivered.
- **R71/R72/R73/R74 §01:** bed tile = `Bed 03` · `A. Mensa` · `F1 · Slessor` · `05h 31m`, condition slot **kept but re-toned** `text-terra`→`text-navy-3` (a duration is not an alarm) · queue = 4 columns, complaint is the single named exception, `Begin visit` writes `started_at` **then** navigates (a nav-only button would leave R33's wait clock running on a student being treated) and renders only for writers while the row still renders for a HEADMASTER (affordance filter, never a data filter) · **A2 one-name rule:** 1 admission → `**{I}. {Surname}** · bed {n}`; **>1 → `{n} on the ward`, NO names at all**; 0 → no meta · **counter honesty:** `0 / 8 beds` is a **known** zero and renders (R27's false zero was `0/8` when no admission table existed), but with zero active beds render `0` with **no `/ M` denominator** (a capacity the school never configured is the false-zero shape again) · empty queue renders `0` with **no meta at all** (`avg wait 0 min` asserts a measured wait for a patient who doesn't exist) · **partition invariant: the breakdown terms SUM to the total.**
- **R89 (AMENDS R71) — NO Mode-C panel on the board.** Kofi: *"I reached rung 2 of the ladder (reuse the shipped `MODE_C_CAPACITY_PANEL`) without asking rung 1."* **Setup is a decision surface** — the panel is new information when an admin is choosing a mode. **The board is a daily operational surface** — a permanent panel explaining the school has no beds is noise by week two, and an explanatory panel where a card was is a soft placeholder. Mode C is not bare (2-tile strip + queue promoted to full width + the 24h log). **Empty by design means empty.** What replaces the bed card: **nothing.** ✅ **ADOPTED — the `configured === false` notice** (one dashed panel above the strip, every reader): `Sickbay not set up yet — declare your school's mode in **Sickbay setup**.` This is R25 landing correctly — a coalesced `REFERRAL_ONLY` is NOT a declared Mode C, and without it a FULL-mode school that never ran setup sees no bed board and concludes the product doesn't do beds. **Orthogonal to mode**, renders whatever the coalesced mode says, disappears when setup is saved.
- **R90 the surface's own numbers and weekday are WRONG — derive both.** Tile 3's `5 · 3 discharged · 1 admitted · 1 awaiting` **contradicts §03's own table** (4 discharges) and excludes the three students standing in its own queue; the correct demo value is **8 = 4 discharged · 1 admitted · 3 awaiting**. First live proof that a *drawn* number loses to a *derived* one (spec wins on logic, HTML wins on presentation) — and exactly what R74's partition invariant exists to catch. **`Wed 14 May 2026` is a THURSDAY (Lucy F7)** — the h1 date derives `EEE d MMM yyyy` from the pinned `now`, and **the string must not appear in any expected-copy fixture** or O4's character-exact method would enshrine the error.
- **R82/R83/R84 routes & nav:** `/senior/sickbay/visits/new` is **KEPT and re-pointed** (it bypasses nothing — the queue doesn't create visits and every guard lives in `createVisit`); the board head's `New visit` links to it, its crumb becomes `Sickbay · Today · New visit`, and `safeRevalidate(TODAY_PATH)` is **already shipped** — verify, don't re-author. **`Admit patient` is OMITTED from the board head** — an admission is a *disposition* of an in-progress visit (R35) requiring `started_at` + complaint + impression (R34), so a top-level button is either a second write path skipping the preconditions or `New visit` with a misleading label: **omit-not-fake covers affordances, not just data.** Sidebar `Sickbay` re-points to `/senior/sickbay/today` (**row count unchanged**, no sub-nav); crumb convention `Sickbay`→setup (module root), `Today`→the board; `ClinicalRestricted` gains an optional `label` and keeps its setup link so a restricted ADMIN is never stranded.
- **R85 admitted block:** one per open admission ordered by `bedNumber`; class renamed `.adwoa-block`→`.admitted-block` (a CSS class named after a fabricated demo patient does not ship); `formLabel()` everywhere (the surface's `F1 General Arts` vs `F1 SCI` is drift); chronic tag absent; `.ab-vitals` ×5 use the **visit record's** vocabulary (`Temp · BP · HR · SpO₂ · Pain`) and the same `vitalSeverity()`; **a metric absent from the latest reading renders BLANK — never `—`, never `0`** (a dash in a vitals grid reads as "measured and normal" to a nurse).
- **AC:** **B1–B6** reader (B1 round-trips ≤6 and flat as the queue grows to 30 · B3 frozen key-sets, mutation-killable · **B4 is the headline: `ZZTOKENCOMP` exactly once, `ZZTOKENIMP` ZERO times anywhere including the flight payload, with the visit record as positive control — but see ⚠️ below for WHICH check carries the payload half** · B6 the SQL selects none of the five narrative columns) · **Q1–Q7** queue · **P1–P7** bed board (P3 Mode C: no bed markup **and no explanatory panel** · P4 zero beds → the setup link, `0 / 0` nowhere · P6/P7 new) · **N1–N7** counters (N5: tile 3 reads **8**, terms sum, the literal `5` is not an expected value anywhere) · **L1–L9** §03 (L4 the complaint token absent while name/form/House present · L9 the enriched collision error) · **G1–G5** gate (G1 ADMIN → 200 + `ClinicalRestricted` + **zero** names/beds/complaints/impressions/counts, positive control · G2 **no SQL issued** for ADMIN · G5 repo-wide clinical-reader pin) · **R1–R4** routes/nav · **A-block** (overdue/expected/null-stamp branches; vitals grid always, clinical prose never) · **O** omit register `===` character-exact, and **no fixture contains `Wed 14 May 2026`**.
- **⚠️ B4 CORRECTION (Quinn MINOR-1, post-gate) — "including the flight payload" is carried by B3 + B6, NOT by the served-HTML check.** Quinn proved it: add `workingImpression` to the ward projection **without rendering it** and **B3** (the runtime key-set pin) and **B6** (`JSON.stringify(board)` over the reader's own return) both go RED, while the served-HTML **B4 stays GREEN**. The reason is structural — this page's only client component is `<BeginVisitButton visitId={…} />`, so the *only* prop that crosses the client boundary is a uuid; **server-component props never serialise into the flight payload at all**, so an unrendered leak is invisible to a check that greps the served document. The served check is still real (it catches a leak that *renders*) and it **becomes load-bearing the moment any clinical prop crosses a client boundary** — **INCR-23's per-row grants are the likely trigger**: the instant a row's clinical field is handed to a `"use client"` component, the flight payload starts carrying it and B4's served half is the only check that sees it. Until then, the payload guarantee is B3 + B6.
- **🔶 OWNER-ESCALATE (defaulted, none blocking):** **E8** the sidebar now lands ADMIN on `/today` where they see `ClinicalRestricted` — for a single-admin school that is the paying account opening Sickbay to a wall. Default accept (same escape as E6: clinical read travels with the MATRON role); the one-line variant is a role-conditional href sending ADMIN to setup. **E9** `Admit patient` gone from the board head (R83). **E10** authored copy for sign-off: `Open` · `No visits in the last 24 hours.` · `reassessment overdue` · `{n} on the ward` · `Sickbay not set up yet — declare your school's mode in Sickbay setup.` · `Expected discharge {t}` · `Open record →` · the enriched collision error.
