## INCR-23 · Chronic register — the third RLS boundary

### Interposed: PR #176 security hotfix (✅ MERGED, off `main`, folded into `senior-feat`)
A live PII leak found while mapping this increment, unrelated to sickbay but directly upstream of it. `requireSchool()` authenticated and resolved a school but did **no role check**, and 62 of the 82 `app/(app)` pages are gated by nothing else; accepting a PARENT invite creates a real `role_assignment`, so a claimed parent could read any student's blood group, allergies, conditions, medications and emergency contact — and `updateStudent` would have let them **overwrite** it. `requireSchool` is now staff-only by default with one greppable opt-in (`allowNonStaff`, used solely by the INCR-19b parent statement PDF). **Why it matters here:** the 19a parent boundary did not catch it because staff pages read under `withSchool`, leaving `app.current_parent_user` unset so `parent_deny`'s permit-by-default clause (`pu IS NULL OR …`) let the row through. **That is exactly the polarity hazard R112 inverts** — and it is now a demonstrated one, not a theoretical one.
- Also shipped: a **guard-ordering invariant** (`lib/auth/app-shell-guard.test.ts`) — every `page.tsx` under `app/(app)` and `route.ts` under `app/api` that opens a tenant transaction must await a `require*` guard first; machine-to-machine exemptions are an explicit list and must each prove a fail-closed secret check before reading. Gate lesson recorded for every future guard test: **assert the CONSEQUENCE, not only the CONDITION** (Quinn kept the pinned `if`, deleted its `redirect`, and all 668 tests stayed green while the leak reopened) and **read comment-stripped source** (Dex found a guard name matching inside prose — a false PASS, the dangerous direction).
- 🟠 **Open, and it collides with this increment's premise:** every staff role currently reads *and edits* `student_health_record` (blood group, allergies, conditions, medications) with **no read audit**. INCR-23 cannot honestly call the chronic register "admin-private" while that page shows overlapping fields to any teacher. Ticketed, not fixed here. Also open: `isFinanceOnly(["BURSAR","PARENT"])` is `false`, so a bursar who accepts a parent invite escapes finance confinement.

### Owner decisions — ANSWERED (these close E15–E19 and the D-series; build to them)
- **D1 clinical narrative → STORE IT.** Owner consciously accepts special-category health data under Ghana's DPA 2012.
- **D2 clinical read → MATRON + HEADMASTER default; ADMIN grant-only.** (Unchanged; R116 carves `MENTAL_HEALTH` out of the Headmaster's half.)
- **D3 NHIS → IN**, shape unsettled → **INCR-25**. Confirms R127's omission at 23 (storing it now pre-commits the wrong shape; the card in the referral log is the *mother's*).
- **D4 attendance-M → a teacher save cannot downgrade a sickbay-written `MEDICAL`;** correction only via co-signed `attendance_correction`. (Shipped in 22b as a DB-level `setWhere` guard.)
- **D5.1 retention → 7 years post-exit, configurable per school in settings; STORE THE SETTING, BUILD NO PURGE AT 23, SOFTEN THE UI CLAIM.** This is Kofi's E15 recommended default with one addition: the setting is real and school-configurable now, the machinery is not. **Applies to all three objects** — `sickbay_chronic_read` rows, chronic entries after exit, and 🔴 `audit_log` rows carrying care-plan before/after snapshots (R103 makes the audit log a clinical record store; the owner had never been asked about that table). The save-bar copy keeps its append-only half **verbatim** and drops the 7-year assertion until a purge job exists — asserting a retention policy the repo cannot perform is omit-not-fake in reverse.
- **D5.2 controlled-substance register → IN SCOPE, brief.** INCR-24 (dispensing/administration), not 23 — 23 stores a prescription *schedule*, never an administration.
- **D5.3 N&MC witness → MANDATORY FOR CONTROLLED SUBSTANCES ONLY**, with a documented single-signature override. The witness must be **a real user in this school**, never free text. INCR-24.
- **E18 / R116 → CONFIRMED grant-only, invisible by default.** The Headmaster sees 5 of 6 with **no signal a sixth exists**. Baked into `chronic_entry_ids` and the prod paste, so it had to be ruled before 23a — it now is.
- **E16 → adopt the recommendation** (default access = MATRON + HEADMASTER; copy corrected from four roles to two; School Nurse folds into `MATRON`; Deputy Head (Welfare) reachable by grant). **E17 → omit all three bulk exports**, rule them as a named later slice; `Print dorm copy` still ships at 23b. **E19 → refuse the `Senior prefect` grant row.** **E20** authored copy accepted.

### Kofi rulings R91–R127 (2026-07-22) · Lucy map: `docs/senior/sickbay-chronic-register-surface-map.md`
**Split: 23a / 23b, ONE migration (0058), ONE prod paste, one milestone branch.** The seam is *the day a non-clinical reader can see health data*. **23a** = 0058 + the `staff_grant_scope` boundary + `withStaffScope` + the verifier + §01/§02/§03 + entry/med writes + the read-audit write + the debts to 22 + the `resetScheduleSlots` reconcile fix. **23b** = §04 grants + `grantAccess`/`revokeAccess` + the widened route + the scoped projections + `Print dorm copy`. Unlike 22a/22b, **23a alone is a safe, honest, shippable state** — it is exactly D2's default with no grant issuable. 23b branches independently off the milestone head, **never stacked** (orphan-PR rule).

**A · the register's model** — **R91** `sickbay_chronic_entry`, one row per (student × condition), NOT columns on `student_health_record` (whose `student_id` is globally `.unique()`, so SCD **and** asthma is inexpressible); the shipped table stays the 1:1 bio baseline, not migrated, not dual-written. **R92** module-wide `sickbay_` prefix. **R93** 0058 = 2 enums + 4 tables, nothing else. **R94** `chronic_condition` = BUILD_STACK L346 verbatim (7 values); the no-EMR ceiling is *enforced by* `OTHER` + free-text detail, never a migration; R43's `diagnos` sweep extends to every 23 file. ⚠️ named ceiling: `MENTAL_HEALTH` is a **security discriminator** (R116), so the carve-out is only as good as the matron's classification — the entry form must state the consequence at the point of choice. **R95** status is THREE values (`STABLE|MONITOR|ACTIVE_CRISIS`); **`REFERRAL_MANAGED` is NOT a status** — as an enum value it forces a referral-managed student *in crisis* to choose, and crisis wins, silently erasing "we do not treat this on-site" at the one moment it matters. Consequence (the R90 shape again): the drawn counts are wrong; derived they are `All 6 · crisis 1 · monitor 2 · stable 3`, with `Referral-managed` a **second-axis overlay pill, not a partition term**. **R96** `on_site_treatable` + `referral_managed` are independent NOT NULL booleans; `MENTAL_HEALTH ⇒ (false,true)` is a **DB CHECK** (product policy, not per-school judgement; J3 forbids *cross-table* triggers, not a single-row CHECK). **R97** NO protocol-step table — `emergency_protocol` is ONE text column in the matron's own words; the terracotta frame is kept, the five numbered cards become her paragraphs; **do not write a prose parser to reconstruct the numbering.**
- **🔴 R98 · THE HEADLINE — the dorm-side card is a SEPARATELY AUTHORED artefact, not a redaction of the care plan.** The surface proves it: protocol step 4 lists *priapism (boys)* and *O₂ sat <95%*; the dorm card says *"severe pain · fever · breathlessness · chest pain"*. Different strings for different readers, not one string truncated. So the entry carries **HM-tier columns** (`triggers`, `red_flags`, `first_action`) alongside the clinical tier. **Why it is load-bearing: it deletes the entire class of redaction bug.** There is nothing to redact, so a scope is a fixed pinnable key-set (R70's runtime key-set pin generalises straight on) rather than a substring judgement made at render time.
- **R99** `sickbay_chronic_med` = one row per (entry × drug × slot | PRN); `dose_label` is text; CHECK `is_prn XOR slot_id`; the grid is a **pivot** (presentation). Rejected: a `slot_doses jsonb` map (R64(7) — INCR-24's round query becomes a full scan) and a third `_dose` table. 🔴 `slot_id` FK is **RESTRICT, not SET NULL** — SET NULL silently orphans a dose from its round, i.e. a student quietly stops being dosed. **R100 🔴 the `resetScheduleSlots` obligation MOVES FORWARD from 24 to 23a** — it is a hard DELETE that changes every slot id, so "Reset to defaults" would cascade against (or, under RESTRICT, hard-fail) every chronic dose in the school. The TOCTOU and slot-create anchor guard stay at 24. **R101** med-grid columns render from `getRoundSchedule()`; the surface's `13:00 Lunch` is demo drift and loses. **R102** `on_site_treatable = false ⇒ zero med rows`, app-layer in the writer (J3). **R103** versioning is a **counter + the shipped audit log** — no version table, no superseded rows; `v4` is a number. ⚠️ this makes `audit_log` a clinical record store (see D5.1). **R104** discharge criteria = one free-text column + a **prefill** into the shipped visit field; the structured checklist and `3 of 4 met` stay omitted (R63 closed with a smaller answer).

**B · grants** — **R105** a grant is per **ENTRY**, not per student (a grant on "Adwoa's plan" silently widening to a future `MENTAL_HEALTH` entry is exactly what R116 exists to prevent). **R106** the grantee is a `ref_user`, NOT NULL; **a STUDENT or PARENT may never hold one** (the `Senior prefect` demo row is refused — R38 already ruled one student's identity as an *actor* inside another's clinical record); a non-user gets the **printed** dorm card, which is the doctrine already. Reuses shipped `isStaff()`. **R107** grants are explicit rows a matron created — **auto-EXPIRE yes, auto-GRANT no.** The note panel's *"grants auto-transfer to the new HM"* **loses**: six students' medical records landing on a man the day he changes job, with no matron decision and no audit event, is the boundary this increment exists to build. The *"auto-expired"* half **wins**, made real by one nullable `house_id`: live iff `houses.hm_user_id = grantee AND student.house_id = grant.house_id`. One column, no new mechanism. **R108** scope enum is THREE values (`FULL_PLAN|PARTIAL|DIRECTIVE`); the surface's nine labels are not nine scopes (`Removed` is `revoked_at IS NOT NULL`; six others return the same projection and differ only in the matron's words). `REFERRAL_ONLY` → **`DIRECTIVE`** (collides with `sickbay_mode_enum.REFERRAL_ONLY` *and* `referral_managed`) — BUILD_STACK amendment #11. **🔴 R109** `DIRECTIVE` reads exactly ONE field and it lives **on the grant row** (`directive_note`, CHECK-enforced): the grantee sees student name + that sentence and **nothing from the entry at all** — not the condition, not the pill, not the colour. The narrowest tier is therefore *structurally incapable* of leaking. Three matron-authored strings live on a grant (`scope_label`, `reason`, `directive_note`) — do not collapse them. **R110** append-only: revoke never deletes; a scope change is revoke + re-grant, never an UPDATE. **R111** grant/revoke is **MATRON-only** — not HEADMASTER, not ADMIN (R39's split repeats: the Head reads but never authors).

**C · the third boundary (the novel work)** — **🔴 R112 DB-enforced, RESTRICTIVE, on `app.current_staff_user`, and DENY-BY-DEFAULT — the one clause that does NOT mirror 19a.** One `SECURITY DEFINER STABLE` function `chronic_entry_ids(school, su)` in `policies.sql` (never the migration) unioning (a) default clinical readers (`MATRON` → all; `HEADMASTER` → all except `MENTAL_HEALTH`) and (b) every entry with a live grant to `su`. Four `AS RESTRICTIVE FOR ALL TO public` policies named `staff_grant_scope`. **Polarity:** `USING (bypass OR (su IS NOT NULL AND <rule>))` versus the parent family's permit-by-default `pu IS NULL OR <rule>` — safe there because those tables' default audience *is* all staff; **the chronic tables have no default audience.** Once the route is widened (R117), `su IS NULL ⇒ permit` means one forgotten seam hands a HOUSEMASTER the whole register; under deny-by-default the same bug yields an empty page. **PR #176 is the proof this is the right call** — that leak was permit-by-default meeting an unset GUC. Seam: `withStaffScope(schoolId, userId, fn)` wrapping **reads and writes**. Verifier: `scripts/verify-sickbay-grant-boundary.ts` cloned from the parent one, every probe as `omnischools_app`. `parent_deny` is catalog-driven so all four tables are parent-denied free — **verify, don't assume.**
- **R113 🔴 the reader's row filter uses the SAME function the policy uses — REQUIRED, not belt-and-braces.** A naive `EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sickbay_chronic_grant …)` **breaks**: RLS applies to tables in the reader's own subquery and the grant table is clinical-reader-only (R122), so a grantee's `EXISTS` returns false and he sees nothing. One predicate, two enforcement points, zero divergence. Never over-fetch-then-filter-in-TS; never a per-row `hasGrant` (R68's N+1).
- **R114** expiry/revocation evaluated server-side, per request, in the transaction, against the DB's `now()` — never a session claim, never middleware. R68's pinned `now` governs **rendering** only. **R115** the coarse role check stays but changes meaning: first statement is `isStaff(roles)` (non-staff → `null`, zero SQL), then **exactly one bounded query** returning zero rows for an unentitled staffer. **🔴 R116** the Headmaster's default read does NOT extend to `MENTAL_HEALTH` — structural, inside `chronic_entry_ids`, so his SQL cannot return the row whatever the reader does. Decisive ground: **he is the school's disciplinary authority**, and a psychiatric history in his default read is the exact adjacency that makes an adolescent not disclose — a register nobody discloses to is worse than no register. **Counts are reader-dependent and R74's partition invariant is evaluated per reader.** **🔴 R117 THE ROUTE — decided, not discovered:** `SICKBAY_ROLES` is **NOT** widened (that would hand HOUSEMASTER the setup page and the today board — names, beds, complaints no grant covers); the register gets its own wider gate (`requireSchool()` + `isStaff()` + `schoolType !== 'BASIC'`) and **the reader's gate is the only clinical boundary**. A separate grantee route would make a URL a membership oracle. **R118** the detail route returns `notFound()` **indistinguishably** for "no such student" and "no entry you may see" — membership of the register is itself medical information; no forbidden panel, no distinguishable timing, no different copy. **R119** `getSickbayBoard(schoolId, actor, now)` with `actor: {userId, roles}` — the field is `userId` not `id` (Sarah's ADV-2 proved an ambiguous identity field name is what lets a tier collapse silently). **R120** the client boundary takes scalars or a separately-pinned view type, **never a reader row** — ⚠️ this is the increment where 22c's B4 served-HTML half becomes load-bearing.

**D · read-audit** — **R121** detail opens only, deduped per (actor × entry × civil day), in its **own** table `sickbay_chronic_read` with `UNIQUE(...)` + `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`: one insert, no read-before-write, no race. Rejected `actionType='viewed'` on `audit_log` (dedupe becomes SELECT-then-INSERT needing a five-column index on a hot shared table — Risk 3's *"an audit table outgrowing the data"*), and decisively: a separate table can be aged out on its own retention schedule. Fires on the detail route and the dorm-card print; **never** on the list, marker, counters, tiles or a revalidation. **The matron's own opens ARE audited.** §04's trail is the union of this table and `audit_log`, rendered as one list. **🔴 R122** the audit row stores IDs and a scope — **never a condition string** (the surface's `viewed Esi Antwi · diabetic protocol` is a leak by proxy on the very screen meant for oversight); and **§04 is clinical-reader-only** — a grantee must never learn who *else* knows.

**E · what 23 owes 22** — **R123** the queue's chronic column returns as a **neutral boolean marker** `Care plan on file`, rendered only when the actor may read ≥1 active entry, **nothing at all** otherwise; R61's false negative (`No chronic flag` asserting *safety* about a register the reader may not fully see) is retired. `SickbayQueueRow` gains exactly ONE key, `hasCarePlan: boolean` — structurally incapable of naming a condition; the other three row types gain **nothing**. The marker is computed from the entries **the actor may read**, so a Headmaster gets none for a mental-health-only student (a marker he cannot act on is an invitation to ask *"what plan?"*, which is the disclosure). **R124** the visit record's header gains the readable condition chip(s) + a working `View care plan →`; the SCD protocol **banner** stays omitted (A4: it leaks twice, by name *and* by drug — `hydroxyurea ⇒ sickle cell`; a link leaks neither). **R125** §01 `Last visit` renders timestamp + relative age only — the reason fragment is omitted (R76/A12 refused this on a *retrospective* log; here it would sit in the same row as a named condition pill, strictly worse) and is unbacked anyway.

**F · adjacency, ruled fresh** — **R126 the register's threat model is the GRANTEE, not the shoulder.** On `today` the reader was always a clinician and the risk was who stood behind her; here the reader may be a sports master and the risk is *what he is handed*. So the discipline inverts: **full clinical detail on §02 is correct and is the surface's purpose** for a `FULL_PLAN` reader, and the whole discipline lives in the per-scope column projection (R98 makes that a fixed key-set). **22c's rulings are NOT re-litigated from this surface.** Never leaking back: no condition/family/pill/drug/membership on `today` beyond R123's boolean · **a grantee never sees the §01 list** (his register contains only his granted entries — falls out of R113 but is an AC in its own right, because "show him the list, gate the detail" is the obvious wrong shortcut) · drug names never leave §02 · no grant metadata to grantees · `attendance_record.note` stays `null` · preserve verbatim *"Do not display in dorm common area — medical privacy applies."* and *"Class teachers see 'M' not 'A'."*

**G · omit-not-fake at 23 (R127)** — absent entirely, no shell/badge/anchor/inert button: **all NHIS** (D3 stands, shape at 25) · **`Open VLC case` + the DMHU visit-log card** (VLC unbuilt; ship `referral_managed` + external-care text columns and no link; `VLC Case 2024-VLC-0047` is a matron-typed string inside `external_pastoral_home`, not a join) · **`Export PDF` / `Export grant log` / `Export 30-day log`** (the highest-value exfiltration path in the product, no ruling behind it — E17) · `Deputy Head (Welfare)` / `School Nurse` as default readers (neither role exists) · the `Senior prefect` grant row · the `13:00` column · the `Last visit` reason fragment · the five numbered protocol cards *as structure*. Sub-nav is `Today · Chronic register · Setup` only. **The demo six must be REAL** — seeded against seeded students and the six seeded Houses (`Aggrey·Guggisberg·Fraser·Slessor·Kingsley·Aryee`; `Kufuor`/`Nkrumah` are drift), and the seeded `Abena Mensah` is renamed **`Adwoa Mensa`** (one string, and it makes the canonical cross-surface demo case real on four surfaces at once).

### AC for Quinn (INCR-23)
- **X1–X16 + X-MUT · the boundary verifier**, mechanism cloned from `verify-parent-boundary.ts` (fixtures as superuser in a **rolled-back** tx; every probe after `set local role omnischools_app`). **X1 is the clause that differs from `parent_scope`** — school GUC set, staff GUC unset ⇒ **0 rows on all four tables**, and it must go RED if the polarity is flipped to `su IS NULL OR …`. X3 Headmaster reads exactly the non-`MENTAL_HEALTH` entries (positive control: the SCD entry and its meds visible). X7 expiry evaluated in the same statement. X9 house-tied grant dies when the student moves House. X10/X11 a grantee cannot extend or self-issue a grant. X12 row relocation refused in **both** directions. X14 **drift guard**: every table in `CHRONIC_TABLES` has both a `staff_grant_scope` and a `parent_deny` policy in `pg_policy`, and the list equals the `sickbay_chronic_*` set exported from `db/schema/sickbay.ts` so a fifth table at 24–28 cannot escape silently. X15 `withoutTenantScope` is the **only** escape. **X-MUT:** rewriting the function to ignore `su` turns X3/X5/X6/X7/X8/X9 red; dropping the `MENTAL_HEALTH` clause turns **X3 alone** red while X5/X6 stay green — the asymmetry proves the carve-out is live independently of the grant arm (the 22c ADV-1 proof shape).
- **E1–E9 entry model** (E3 the CHECK proven with raw SQL, not only through the action · E6 slot ids **stable across a reset**, proven by comparing before/after · E9 the `diagnos` sweep).
- **S1–S6 scope projection** — S2 `PARTIAL` proven by `Object.keys()` that the clinical columns are **absent, not empty**, with the matron's read of the same row as positive control; S3 `DIRECTIVE` frozen key-set (no condition value, label, pill class or colour token); S5 byte-identical `notFound()`; **S6 adding a clinical column to the `PARTIAL` projection without rendering it turns the runtime key-set pin RED** (the allowlist generalises where a denylist would not).
- **G1–G5 grants** (G2 proven through a direct POST, not only the UI). **A1–A6 read-audit** (A2 zero rows across a full 30-entry render; A3 concurrency handled by the UNIQUE, not a check; A5 the matron's own opens audited). **R1–R5 route & reader** (R2 `SICKBAY_ROLES` unchanged, grep-pinned, with the full 21/22 suites passing untouched · R3 **statement**-level flatness 6→30 · R5 the clinical-gate **expression** pin, RED when the gate is deleted). **D1–D5 debts to 22.** **O1–O5 structural** (O1 the from-empty replay with `*_tenant_uk` before the composite FKs — manual, this repo has no CI).

The three CHECKs (`MENTAL_HEALTH ⇒ NOT on_site_treatable AND referral_managed` · `is_prn XOR slot_id` · `DIRECTIVE ⇒ directive_note NOT NULL`). 6. ⚠️ the grant partial-unique **cannot** use `now()` (not immutable) — fall back to `WHERE revoked_at IS NULL`, expiry in the reader. 7. `slot_id` composite FK RESTRICT (this is what forces R100 into 23a). 8. `read_on` as `date`, Africa/Accra civil date from `civilDate()`. 9. `prod-paste-0058-sickbay-chronic.sql` = DDL + the four policies + the function, **and** the catalog-driven `parent_deny` loop re-run (or the four new tables are parent-readable on prod until someone runs `db:policies`).

### INCR-23a · Wells — migration 0058 + the third boundary (commit `bfae974`, pushed to `senior-feat`)
Files: `db/schema/sickbay.ts` · `db/schema/_enums.ts` · `db/migrations/0058_windy_silver_sable.sql` · `db/sql/policies.sql` · **`db/sql/prod-paste-0058-sickbay-chronic.sql`** · `lib/db/rls.ts` (`withStaffScope`).

**OQ1 answered — two of the nine corrected the spec:**
1. **Grant scope IS an enum** (`sickbay_grant_scope`, module-prefixed) — what BUILD_STACK L350 already declares, the idiom of all 8 shipped 4.4 enums, and it hands the implementer a real TS union (a `text`+CHECK column infers as `string`). **So 0058 is 3 enums + 4 tables, not R93's 2 + 4** — the only count that moved, and OQ1 #1 delegated it.
2. Tenant UKs inline in `CREATE TABLE`; all four tables emitted before any `ADD FOREIGN KEY`. Verified by **reading the emitted SQL**, not by exit code. Three composite FKs consume the entry UK in this same file — the 0033 hazard, closed.
3. **🔴 CORRECTED — the correlated form is a real O(n²) cliff, proven by EXPLAIN both ways.** Passing `school_id` **from the row** gives a plain `(SubPlan 1)`, re-executed per row, each execution scanning the entry table whose own policy re-runs the predicate per entry. Passing the **GUC constant** gives `(hashed SubPlan 2)` — built once, probed per row. So every policy passes `NULLIF(current_setting('app.current_school',true),'')::uuid`, never `school_id`. Equivalent because `tenant_isolation` already forces `school_id =` that GUC on any row that can survive. **A deliberate divergence from `parent_student_ids(school_id, pu)`, which is correlated today** — worth revisiting there.
4. `app.bypass_rls` arm byte-identical to `tenant_isolation`'s and verified: a bypass-shaped session reads 2/1/3 with both other GUCs empty. `db:policies`, `db:rls-test`, `verify-parent-boundary` (89 deny tables, zero leaks) all green.
5. All three CHECKs ship and were proven **with raw SQL, not through an action**, rejecting both directions with positive controls.
6. **🔴 CORRECTED — the recommended `WHERE revoked_at IS NULL` fallback is WRONG, and no live-grant unique shipped.** An **expired grant is not a revoked one**, so a July dead-but-unrevoked row would reject August's lawful re-grant with a unique violation, and the only workaround is stamping `revoked_at` on something that merely expired — **a lie inside a clinical audit trail** (R110). It also buys nothing: duplicate live grants are semantically idempotent (both matron-issued, no escalation), and the reader must resolve a *set* of live grants regardless (an expired `FULL_PLAN` beside a live `DIRECTIVE` must collapse to `DIRECTIVE` whatever an index says). ⚠️ **23b owes the precedence rule** — which scope wins when two live grants exist on one entry (Wells recommends widest-wins; with Kofi).
7. `slot_id` composite FK RESTRICT **+ an index on `(school_id, slot_id)`** — Postgres does not index FKs, and an unindexed RESTRICT target means a seq scan of every prescription in the school on every schedule edit. **R100 is now a HARD BLOCKER, not a nice-to-have: `resetScheduleSlots` hard-DELETEs slot rows, so after 0058 "Reset to defaults" ERRORS for any school with a med schedule.** Must become a reconcile in this same release.
8. `read_on date NOT NULL` from the shipped `civilDate()`; it is the dedupe key and a timestamp cannot be one.
9. Prod paste ends with the verbatim catalog-driven `parent_deny` loop + a 🔴 note on skipping it.

**Deviations from Kofi's spec, each forced and each reported:**
- **🔴 THREE `SECURITY DEFINER` functions, not one.** SECURITY DEFINER does **not** exempt you from `FORCE` RLS, so a single `chronic_entry_ids` called from the entry table's own policy reads that table → re-enters the policy → calls itself → **infinite recursion at runtime**. The acyclic fix: `chronic_clinical_role(school,su) → text` (must be DEFINER — it joins the **global `ref_role`**, which has bare ENABLE and no policy, so a non-owner session reads zero rows and an inline join would silently evaluate to "no role" for the very session it authorises) → `chronic_entry_readable(school,su,entry,student,cond) → boolean`, **the** predicate, taking id/student/condition as *arguments* precisely so it never reads the entry table → `chronic_entry_ids(school,su) → SETOF uuid`, its projection, and the signature R113 requires the reader to call. **X-MUT still discriminates as specified.**
- **🔴 One place the DB is coarser than R116, cycle-forced.** The grant policy cannot read the entry table (`entry → grant → entry`), so its rule is "a default clinical reader OR your own grant row" — and a HEADMASTER is a default clinical reader. **⚠️ Wells characterised the residue as harmless ("no student, no name, no condition"); that is wrong** — the grant row carries `scope_label`, `reason` and `directive_note`, all matron-authored free text, and R109 makes `directive_note` deliberately clinical-adjacent. A Headmaster barred from a `MENTAL_HEALTH` entry can read a grant row against it, **learn a sixth entry exists** (which R116 forbids) and read prose that may name the condition. **Open with Kofi (product) and Sarah (security).**
- **The grantee CAN read his own grant row — required, not a courtesy.** `chronic_entry_readable` reads the grant table to evaluate entitlement, so a blanket clinical-only rule there makes **every grant self-defeating**. Own-row-only is the shipped `student_guardian`/`parent_scope` idiom, and R122's "never learn who *else* knows" is preserved.
- **Asymmetric USING / WITH CHECK on the entry table — mandatory.** A `WITH CHECK` is evaluated on a row that does not exist yet, so any "an entry you may read" write rule is FALSE for every INSERT and **no matron could ever create a care plan**. Write rules are actor-shaped (`chronic_clinical_role IS NOT NULL`), which also stops a FULL_PLAN grantee editing the plan or injecting a dose. On `sickbay_chronic_read` the asymmetry inverts (everyone WITH-CHECKs their own row in, only clinical readers USING-read the trail) — which makes the trail append-only against a grantee. ⚠️ **the audit insert must not use `.returning()`** (RETURNING needs SELECT).
- **Three extra `staff_grant_delete` policies (FOR DELETE, RESTRICTIVE).** DELETE is authorised by USING alone, so without them a FULL_PLAN grantee could **delete the care plan he was shown**, and any grantee could delete his own grant row and **erase the evidence he ever had access**.
- **`OTHER` exempt from the per-condition partial unique** — it is R94's escape hatch, so capping a student at one `OTHER` row caps the register (coeliac *and* hypertension is legitimate).
- Five of Lucy's N17 columns not shipped (unruled by Kofi, each replaceable): `goal_signal`, `scope_limits`, `protocol_title`, and `superseded_at`/`superseded_by` (R103 kills those two outright).

**Verified on dev as `omnischools_app` via `SET LOCAL ROLE`, rolled back:** school GUC set + **staff GUC unset → 0/0/0/0** (flip the polarity to `su IS NULL OR` and it becomes 2/1/3) · MATRON 2/1/3 · **HEADMASTER 1 entry, `SICKLE_CELL` only** · FULL_PLAN grantee 1/1/1-own-grant/0-audit · self-issue refused, expiry-extension refused, all three DELETEs 0 rows, entry UPDATE refused, **matron positive control passes all four** · house-tied grant 1 → 0 on House move → 1 on return · expired DIRECTIVE grantee 0 · matron of A scoped to B 0 · bypass 2/1/3. `tsc` clean, 668/668.

**⚠️ `db:push` is unusable on this dev DB** — it demands truncating `boarding_infractions` to add a pre-existing missing `boarding_infractions_tenant_uk` (unrelated drift, destroys 10 rows). 0058 was applied via `docker psql --single-transaction`, then `db:policies`, then the prod paste replayed over the top to prove idempotency (catalog unchanged: 15 policies / 15 FKs / 4 uniques / 3 CHECKs). **That drift needs its own ticket.**

**🔴 PROD DEPLOY REQUIRED — `db/sql/prod-paste-0058-sickbay-chronic.sql`**, one idempotent paste: enums → 4 tables → FKs → indexes → ENABLE/FORCE/`tenant_isolation` → the 3 helpers → 4 `staff_grant_scope` + 3 `staff_grant_delete` → **the `parent_deny` loop re-run**. Then `db/sql/verify-prod-rls.sql` (Query 1 zero rows; tenant_tables +4; parent_denied +4) and the header's `pg_policy` query — expect exactly 15 rows. **Skipping the paste does not leak a bed count: R117 opens the route to all staff and the RLS is the only clinical boundary, so it hands a school's adolescent mental-health register to whoever logs in next.**

**Still owed before 23a can ship:** R100 `resetScheduleSlots` reconcile (now hard-fails) · `scripts/verify-sickbay-grant-boundary.ts` (X1–X16 — Wells's probes were throwaway scratchpad SQL, not a repo file) · the reader must call `chronic_entry_ids` (a hand-written `EXISTS` on the grant table fails closed and confusingly) · 23b's multiple-live-grant precedence · §04 must INNER JOIN the entry.

### INCR-23a · 0058 gate corrections — ✅ **applied (`882b079`) and Sarah RE-VERIFIED CLEAR** (prod-shaped)
Wells applied all seven items (`882b079`, pushed to `senior-feat`). **Kofi's primary R129 shape worked in full on PG16 — the fallback was not needed.** Sarah re-gated every door under **prod-shaped ownership** (`probe_owner NOSUPERUSER`, FORCE intact, in a rolled-back tx — because on dev the helpers are superuser-owned and their bodies bypass RLS, proving nothing), each pass with a non-vacuous negative control:
- **HIGH-1 SHUT** — HEADMASTER INSERT grant/entry/med → RLS violation; UPDATE grant → violation; DELETE → 0. Negative control: revert the `WITH CHECK` to `IS NOT NULL` → HM INSERT **succeeds**, so `= 'MATRON'` is load-bearing. MATRON positive control writes all three.
- **R128/R129 SHUT** — HM sees **0** grants on the MH entry (cannot even enumerate it). **CASCADE proven LIVE, not on trust:** MATRON flips an ASTHMA entry to `MENTAL_HEALTH` → the entry's generated `hm_restricted` f→t **and the live grant's bit cascades f→t** → HM's grant count on it drops to 0. **Lying stamp refused** — even a MATRON inserting/updating a grant with `hm_restricted=false` on an MH entry → FK violation `sickbay_chronic_grant_entry_hm_fk`. Negative control: drop `AND NOT hm_restricted` → HM sees the MH grant.
- **MEDIUM-1 SHUT** — grantee forging an audit row attributed to the HEADMASTER → RLS violation; his own row inserts but he reads the trail back as **0** (writes into a log he cannot see); MATRON `DELETE`/`UPDATE` on `_read` → 0 rows (bypass still purges, for D5.1 retention).
- **MEDIUM-2 SHUT** — `pg_temp` shadow of `role_assignment`/`ref_role` → helper returns the real role (NULL), not MATRON. Negative control: recreate with `search_path = public` only → shadow promotes. `parent_student_ids` carries the same fix (`prod-paste-0055`).
- **MEDIUM-4 SHUT** — `verify-prod-rls.sql` check D fires (`no staff_grant_scope — every staff member reads this table`) when a `staff_grant_scope` is dropped, while A/B/C stay silent; clean catalog → Query 1 zero rows, 17 chronic policies (entry 4 / med 4 / grant 4 / read 5).
- **No new hole from the fix:** the generated column adds no write path and blocks no legitimate one; `UNIQUE (school_id, id, hm_restricted)` is a strict superset of the already-unique `(school_id, id)` so it can't reject a valid row; `search_path` change introduced no `public`-resolution regression; the HM read arm is exact (reads non-restricted grants, not restricted, writes neither). **The three-function graph stayed acyclic under FORCE.**
- **Cross-file parity confirmed:** `policies.sql` and `prod-paste-0058` carry all four fixes identically in executable DDL (independently diffed byte-for-byte on the `staff_grant` blocks).

Hold both until the 23a reader PR is ready so a single deploy covers the increment.

### INCR-23a · Kofi R128–R131 + Sarah's security read on `bfae974` — 🔴 the ORIGINAL gate (findings above now ✅ resolved by `882b079`)

**🔴🔴 CRITICAL — `assignStaffRole` (and nine siblings) have NO role gate. Live on `main`. Paused 23a to hotfix it.**
Every exported mutator in `lib/actions/staff.ts` — `addStaff`, `importStaff`, `saveStaffProfile`, `updateStaff`, `deleteStaff`, `deleteStaffBulk`, **`assignStaffRole`**, `removeStaffRole`, `saveStaffCompensation` — is gated by `requireSchool()` alone, which **since PR #176 means "authenticated + is staff" and nothing more**. `ADMIN` is in `STAFF_ROLES` (`lib/staff-roles.ts:9`) and `components/staff/staff-row.tsx:128` renders the role editor for every row. **So any teacher, librarian or sports master can open `/staff`, find their own row and assign themselves `ADMIN` — three clicks, no crafted request.** Sarah found it as self-promotion to `MATRON` (which defeats this increment's entire boundary, since `chronic_clinical_role` reads `role_assignment`); the wider truth is full administrative takeover of the tenant, plus bulk staff deletion and write access to payroll via `saveStaffCompensation`.
**Root cause is the same seam as #176, one layer up:** #176 correctly made `requireSchool()` mean *is staff*; a **role-management** surface needs *is an administrator*, and nothing supplies it. The escalation is audit-logged (`recordAudit … "Role assigned"`), so it is detectable after the fact — that is the only mitigation present. **The third boundary is keyed on a table its own subjects can write.** Hotfix off `main`, then resume.

**Kofi R128 — the grant-row residue is a VIOLATION of R116/E18, not residue.** Wells's *"no student, no name, no condition"* is true only of the column list read literally. Three grounds: (1) **existence** — a grant row carries `entry_id`, so a Headmaster whose `chronic_entry_ids` returns 5, holding a grant row whose `entry_id` is in none of them, has been handed the signal *by construction and can count them*; that is the literal negation of the sentence the owner ratified in E18. (2) **Free-text clinical payload** — `chronic_grant_directive_needs_note` *forces* `directive_note` non-null for every DIRECTIVE grant and R109 defines it as the grantee's entire operational view of the child, so the column cannot be assumed condition-free; `reason` is by definition the matron's justification. **This is R122's family** — R122 refused a condition string on a row already carrying `entry_id + actor + scope`, and there is no principled reading under which a stored enum is a disclosure but a stored sentence about the same entry is not. (3) **The adjacency R116 was ruled FOR** — `grantee_user_id + house_id + granted_at` tells the disciplinary authority which HM was recently briefed about a boy in his House; the entry is not needed. Scope: **only** the grant table has the hole; `_med` and `_read` gate on `entry_id IN chronic_entry_ids(...)` and are correct as shipped.
- **Kofi R129 — close it with an FK-pinned policy BIT, not the condition, not MATRON-only, not an app-layer strip.** The cycle is real and stands (any grant policy reading the entry table recurses; Postgres does not guarantee OR short-circuit across policy arms, so the loop is a planner decision, not a guarantee). **Required outcome:** a HEADMASTER selecting from `sickbay_chronic_grant` gets **zero rows** whose entry his default read excludes, evaluated **inside the RLS predicate**, with **no read of the entry table**, and the stored discriminator must be **incapable of diverging** without a loud failure. Recommended shape: `sickbay_chronic_entry.hm_restricted boolean GENERATED ALWAYS AS (condition = 'MENTAL_HEALTH') STORED` + `UNIQUE (school_id, id, hm_restricted)`; `sickbay_chronic_grant.hm_restricted boolean NOT NULL` with the entry FK becoming composite `(school_id, entry_id, hm_restricted)` **`ON UPDATE CASCADE`**. **Why not a plain insert-time boolean: it fails OPEN on re-classification** (a grant stamped `false` against an entry later corrected to `MENTAL_HEALTH` stays Headmaster-visible) — the dangerous direction, and R94's own ⚠️ concedes re-classification happens. Under CASCADE the DB propagates the flip and a wrong stamp is an **FK violation at INSERT, not a silent leak** — which answers R10 squarely (R10 forbids a stored value that *can* disagree). Named for the policy fact, not the diagnosis, so the string `MENTAL_HEALTH` never lands on a grant row. Fallback if Postgres refuses the shape: plain NOT NULL boolean **plus** `condition` immutable after insert (mis-classification corrected by `active = false` + a new entry, which the R91 partial unique already permits and which correctly drops every stale grant). **Timing:** the grant table is empty until 23b's `grantAccess`, so no live exposure — **fold the DDL into 0058 while the PR is open**; if 0058 had already merged and been pasted it becomes 0059 and a hard blocker on 23b.
- **Kofi R130 — §04's reader does not change; its STATUS does.** The INNER JOIN to the entry **stays** (R113's "one predicate, two enforcement points"; it also supplies the student and pill §04 renders), but it stops being the sole enforcement of R116 and becomes defence-in-depth. **New AC:** deleting the reader's INNER JOIN must still return zero grant rows for a HEADMASTER on a granted `MENTAL_HEALTH` entry, **and** flipping the policy arm back to `IS NOT NULL` must turn that probe RED while X3 stays green — two mutations, two independent enforcement points. Add to X14: every `sickbay_chronic_*` `staff_grant_scope` USING must gate on `chronic_entry_ids(...)` **or** carry the `hm_restricted` arm, so a fifth table at 24–28 cannot inherit the coarse rule by copy-paste.
- **Kofi R131 — widest-wins, confirmed, four bindings.** No live-grant unique (Wells's reasoning adopted verbatim — forcing `revoked_at` on a merely-expired grant is a false statement in a clinical audit trail, dispositive on its own). Widest-wins because every live grant is a MATRON's explicit authorisation and narrowest-wins makes her most recent act a silent no-op. (1) the set is **live** grants only, per R114's DB `now()` in the same statement; (2) total order `FULL_PLAN > PARTIAL > DIRECTIVE` and the projection is **the winner's key-set, never a union** (a merged key-set is a fourth unpinned scope and defeats S6); (3) the read-audit records the winning scope at the **first** audited open of the day and must **not** become an upsert (`DO UPDATE` needs SELECT/UPDATE on a table a grantee cannot read — the same reason `.returning()` is banned); (4) **§04 lists every live grant and the effective scope, not just the winner** — otherwise a matron revokes the DIRECTIVE row, access persists via a live FULL_PLAN, and the surface has lied about the consequence of her own action.

**🔴 Sarah HIGH-1 — the write rules are wider than the ruling, and Kofi found it independently.** All three `WITH CHECK`s and the `staff_grant_delete` loop test `chronic_clinical_role(...) IS NOT NULL` = *MATRON or HEADMASTER*, where **R39** says clinical write is MATRON-only and **R111** says grant/revoke is MATRON-only. `USING` is the read predicate; **an INSERT never touches `USING`**. ✅ Sarah verified the exploit as `omnischools_app`: as HEADMASTER, `select count(*) … MENTAL_HEALTH` → 0, then one `INSERT INTO sickbay_chronic_grant … select … FROM sickbay_chronic_grant WHERE scope='DIRECTIVE'` naming himself → then reads the entry, `condition_detail`, `emergency_protocol` and **Sertraline**. **He obtained the `entry_id` from the grant table itself — so R128's residue is not decoration, it is the enumeration step of this escalation.** Same root cause also lets a HEADMASTER author/edit care plans and doses (R39 bypass) and un-revoke, extend, re-target or **delete** any grant (R110/R111 bypass — the append-only trail is app-layer-only against him). **Fix: one token, four places, in BOTH `policies.sql` and the prod paste** — `chronic_clinical_role(...) = 'MATRON'`. Nothing legitimate breaks (the helper already prefers `'MATRON'` when a user holds both; seeds/ETL run under bypass).
**🔴 Sarah HIGH-2 — the residue confirmed and extended** (see R128). Her cycle-free alternative if the prose must go: move `scope_label`/`reason`/`directive_note` to a child `sickbay_chronic_grant_note` policed by `entry_id IN chronic_entry_ids(...)` — nothing reads that table, so the graph stays acyclic. **Column privileges do NOT work: the app connects as the table owner on prod and column grants do not bind the owner.** With HIGH-1 fixed the residue drops to a bounded read-side prose leak and is no longer an escalation.
**🟠 Sarah MEDIUM-1 — the read audit is forgeable AND suppressible.** ✅ Verified: `sickbay_chronic_read`'s `WITH CHECK` never checks `actor_user_id` is the session, so a FULL_PLAN grantee inserted a row **attributed to the HEADMASTER** (and `actor_user_id` FKs the **global** `ref_user`, so the forged actor need not even be in this school). He cannot read the trail back — **he writes into an oversight log he cannot see**, the worst asymmetry for §04's credibility. Second: **`scope` is not in the dedupe key**, so a grantee can pre-insert tomorrow's row with the narrowest scope and the genuine `FULL_PLAN` insert silently no-ops, permanently understating what he was shown. Fix: `AND actor_user_id = <the staff GUC>` in `WITH CHECK`; plus restrictive `FOR UPDATE`/`FOR DELETE USING (bypass)` so a **matron cannot delete the audit of her own opens** — the one thing §04 exists to prevent. ✅ The `.returning()` footgun **fails CLOSED, loudly** (RLS violation aborts the tx) — the residual risk is an implementer wrapping it in `try/catch`, which serves the read **unaudited**.

Postgres searches the temp schema first for **relations** when `pg_temp` is not named explicitly. Precondition is arbitrary session SQL — defence-in-depth — **but RLS is precisely the layer that must survive an injection.** ✅ Fix verified: `SET search_path = public, pg_temp` on all three helpers **and on the shipped `parent_student_ids`, which has the same flaw.**

RLS is row-level and cannot mask columns (already stated at `policies.sql:344-346`). Not fixable at the DB layer without column policies, so **this must be the loudest line in the 23b handoff: S2/S3's key-set pins are load-bearing security controls, not tidiness**, and `select *` or spreading a reader row into a client component is a direct disclosure of exactly the tier R109 exists to prevent.
**🟠 Sarah MEDIUM-4 — `verify-prod-rls.sql` cannot see the third boundary.** It checks tenant isolation, the parent boundary and global tables. Add a **check D** in the file's own doctrine: every `sickbay_chronic_%` table must carry a **restrictive** `staff_grant_scope`, and entry/med/grant a `staff_grant_delete`. That is the durable version of X14 and the only thing that catches a fifth chronic table at 24–28 escaping on prod.

It terminates because the grant policy reads only `role_assignment`; **`ref_role`'s bare ENABLE (no FORCE) is what keeps `chronic_clinical_role` correct on prod**, so a future FORCE on `ref_role` would silently make every clinical reader a nobody. Standing invariant: any policy added to `students`/`house`/`role_assignment`/`ref_role`/`sickbay_chronic_grant` that reads a `sickbay_chronic_*` table closes the cycle — **23b is the near-term temptation and does not need one.** **🟡 L3** helper EXECUTE is PUBLIC (PostgREST RPC on Supabase) — returns nothing without the GUCs, so hardening not a hole; REVOKE anyway. **🟡 L4** the bypass arm is byte-identical and not a wider door, but on these tables it satisfies both families — **treat a chronic table inside `withoutTenantScope` as an automatic review trigger** (it reads every school and writes no audit row). **🟡 L5** `withStaffScope` hygiene correct — every `set_config` is `is_local = true`, `userId` bound as a parameter; a `withSchool` nested inside takes a different pooled connection with no staff GUC, which under this polarity yields an empty page, not a leak. **🔴 L6 — one query to run on prod, once: `select rolbypassrls from pg_roles where rolname = current_user;`** The repo asserts Supabase's `postgres` lacks BYPASSRLS (`policies.sql:9-11`) and every FORCE-RLS guarantee depends on it; **it has never been confirmed. If it is true, none of 0055–0058 is enforced on prod.**

## INCR-23b · Chronic register — grant issuance · Kofi R132–R140 build spec (2026-07-25)
**No new migration — CONFIRMED.** Every column shipped in 0058 (`scope`/`scope_label`/`reason`/`directive_note`/`house_id`/`hm_restricted` on the grant; `scope` on `_read`; the `staff_grant_scope`+`staff_grant_delete` families; the widened route R117). 23b is app-layer only: `grantAccess`/`revokeAccess`, the per-scope reader projections, §04, `Print dorm copy`. A reviewer proposing a column is a spec violation — kick it back.

**🔴 TWO RECONCILIATIONS — build to the RULINGS, not Lucy's map (which predates R108/R105):**
1. The third scope is **`DIRECTIVE`, not `ALERT_ONLY`** (Lucy §7.3 superseded). Shipped enum = `FULL_PLAN|PARTIAL|DIRECTIVE`. No ALERT_ONLY, no fixed "care plan on file" sentence-as-scope — DIRECTIVE reads one matron-authored `directive_note` (CHECK-forced non-null).
2. A grant is **per ENTRY, not per student (R105)** — shipped `grant.entry_id` proves it. Lucy §7.2/§8.2's "pick a student" + `grantee_kind` enum are superseded: the form picks a specific **entry**, and the house-tie is the nullable `house_id` (R107), NOT a `grantee_kind` radio. `grantee_user_id` is ALWAYS NOT NULL (a house-tied grant still names the current HM; `house_id` only makes it auto-**expire**, never auto-transfer).

**🔴 R132 — THE THREE FROZEN KEY-SETS (the increment's security crux).** MEDIUM-3 governs: RLS can't mask columns, so the DB hands a grantee the WHOLE entry row; the reader's per-scope projection is the ONLY control. R98 made each scope a fixed runtime-pinnable allow-list (HM tier + clinical tier are separately-authored columns). **Three distinct view types, three distinct `CHRONIC_ROW_KEYS` pins** (runtime-distinguishable, R120/Sarah ADV-2).
- **FULL_PLAN** = the shipped `CHRONIC_ROW_KEYS.entry` **exactly** (all 24 keys) + full `ChronicPlanView` wrapper (identity + guardian + matronName/Phone + roundColumns + anchorDescription). Condition/pill/colour all present — full re-identification, intended (a FULL_PLAN reader is a counsellor / matron-designate / HM holding the epipen).
- **PARTIAL** = **exactly the dorm card (§5.6), nothing else.** New view type `ChronicDormCardView`, key-set (sorted): `condition, conditionLabel, dormMedNote, entryId, firstAction, redFlags, triggers`; wrapper adds `guardianName/Phone, matronName/Phone` (student-grain). **Clinical columns ABSENT not empty (S2):** conditionDetail, baselineStatus, careGoals, emergencyProtocol, dischargeCriteria, all four external*, reviewedByName, coReviewerNote, version, status, onSiteTreatable, referralManaged, hmRestricted — **not in the object at all.** **MEDS/drug names OUT** — the "Daily med" row is a **derived string** `dormMedNote` = `"{anchor.startsAt} round at sickbay (must attend)"` when ≥1 round-scheduled med, else null; the reader computes it server-side and **discards the meds array** (R120). Condition **label + pill/colour IN** (the card names the condition on purpose — that's why the HM walks her to sickbay; bounded by R132.1).
  - **🔴 R132.1 — PARTIAL is VOID on a mental-health entry.** (a) `grantAccess` **refuses `scope=PARTIAL` when the entry `hm_restricted=true`** (form-level); (b) the reader **degrades** any live PARTIAL grant on an `hm_restricted` entry (the ASTHMA→MENTAL_HEALTH reclassification — the grant survives the CASCADE, the payload must not) to a **name-only floor**: `{studentId, studentName, entryId, scope, floorNote:"This student has a care plan on file. Contact the Matron."}` — no condition, no label, no pill, no dorm card. **The reader picks the projection AFTER reading `hm_restricted`, never before.**
- **DIRECTIVE** = **one sentence, nothing from the entry (R109).** New view type `ChronicDirectiveView`, FROZEN key-set (sorted): `directiveNote, entryId, scope` + wrapper `studentId, studentName` only (initials/formLabel/houseName permissible — no condition signal; NEVER any entry field/guardian/phones/roundColumns). `directiveNote` from the **grant row** (never a clinical column read). **Condition/pill/colour: NONE** (not value, label, pill class, colour token, status). The only information is what the matron deliberately wrote — structurally incapable of leaking.
- **R132.2 — the wrapper is scope-aware:** `guardian`/`matronName`/`matronPhone` only when the student's MAX readable scope ≥ PARTIAL; `roundColumns`/`anchorDescription` only when FULL_PLAN. A DIRECTIVE-only reader gets a minimal wrapper. A student with mixed grants (FULL on SCD, DIRECTIVE on asthma) renders each entry at its own winning projection inside one wrapper.

**R133 — Widest-wins reader resolution (R131 restated as the reader contract):** (1) candidate set = LIVE grants only (`revoked_at IS NULL AND (expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at > now())` against the DB's own `now()` in the same statement, R114 + the house-tie liveness) — no "one live grant" unique (an expired-unrevoked row would reject a lawful re-grant; the reader resolves the set at read time). (2) **A default clinical role outranks every grant** — MATRON (all) and HEADMASTER (all-but-MH) → FULL_PLAN with audit `scope=null`. (3) among grants, total order `FULL_PLAN>PARTIAL>DIRECTIVE`, winner's key-set projected **whole, never a union** (a merged key-set is a fourth unpinned scope, defeats S6); expired FULL beside live DIRECTIVE → DIRECTIVE. (4) **exactly ONE projection per (reader × entry).**

**R134 — §04 render (R122/R130/R131.4):** clinical-reader-only (MATRON∪HEADMASTER); **a grantee NEVER reaches §04** (never learns who else knows — `notFound()`). INNER JOINs the entry (R130, defence-in-depth; a HEADMASTER sees no grant rows for MH entries — cannot enumerate a sixth, E18). **Lists EVERY live grant + the per-row effective scope, not just the winner** (else revoking the DIRECTIVE row while a FULL_PLAN is live lies about the consequence); revoked rows stay (append-only) with a neutral `Revoked` pill + disabled action. Audit trail = union of `sickbay_chronic_read` + `audit_log` as one list; event text is a **pure formatter** over `(actionType, actorName, studentName, scope)` — **never stored, never names a condition** (R122). MH-plan audit rows render only to readers who can see that plan (M9). Lede counts over the reader's visible set (`2 default-access roles (Matron, Headmaster)`, not the fabricated 4).

**R135 — grantAccess/revokeAccess:** **MATRON-only (R111)**, first statement, mirroring 23a's `authorizeChronicWrite` (the RLS `WITH CHECK = 'MATRON'` is the backstop). Grantee must be a **staff `ref_user` with a role assignment IN THIS SCHOOL** (R106 — app-layer tenancy check, `ref_user` is global; reuse `isStaff()`). **STUDENT/PARENT refused (E19)** — a non-user gets the printed dorm card. Per ENTRY (R105); PARTIAL refused on `hm_restricted` (R132.1); DIRECTIVE requires `directive_note`. **Append-only (R110)** — revoke stamps `revoked_at`+`revoked_by_user_id`, NEVER a DELETE or a `scope` UPDATE; a scope change is revoke+re-grant. House-tie liveness (R107) — auto-expires on HM/house change, never auto-transfers. Copy (E20): the MH grant-consequence line + the not-a-staff-user refusal + the revoke confirmation, all AUTHORED (see Kofi output).

**R136 — Print dorm copy (C12/C13):** a **print-stylesheet route, not a PDF** (renders the 8 dorm-card rows = the PARTIAL projection). Writes an `audit_log` `exported` row (**intent-to-print** — no web app observes a physical print; distinct from the `_read` dedupe). May print iff winning scope ≥ PARTIAL **and** entry not `hm_restricted`: MATRON, HEADMASTER-on-non-MH, FULL/PARTIAL grantees. **Absent for a MENTAL_HEALTH plan (C13)** and for a DIRECTIVE reader (route refuses).

**R137 — the §01 list AND §04 are clinical-reader-only; a grantee reaches DETAIL only (R126/C2).** The 23a list is board-shaped (condition pills + the drug-name-beside-condition column, six at once) and a grantee's "register" is only his granted entries (not a register). So the **§01 list page and §04 gate on `SICKBAY_CLINICAL_READ_ROLES` at the app layer**; a grantee reaches a specific student's **detail** page by direct link (the visit record's `View care plan →`, R124), projected to his winning scope. The detail route keeps its wider gate (clinical role OR live grant, R117). HEADMASTER keeps his 5-of-6 list.

**R138 — read-audit scope for a grantee open:** one `sickbay_chronic_read` per (actor × entry × civil day), `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`, **no `.returning()`** (grantee has INSERT not SELECT — MEDIUM-1), `actor_user_id` session-bound. `scope` = the winning scope for a grantee; **`null`** for a default clinical role. Recorded at the FIRST audited open of the day; **must not become an upsert** (a wider scope later the same day does not rewrite the morning's row — R131.3).

**R139 — open items resolved + ONE owner flag.** Form fields (R135): grantee staff-picker (this school) · entry (this student's plan) · scope (PARTIAL blocked on MH) · directive_note (req iff DIRECTIVE) · scope_label (opt) · expiry (`<input type=date>` + No expiry) · optional house-tie · reason (required). No `grantee_kind`. **🟡 OWNER FLAG — phone masking (Lucy C11):** the surface shows masked numbers (`0244 ███ 089`); Lucy calls the mask incoherent on a dorm card whose purpose is *who to call*, and recommends **full numbers** to a reader whose scope includes the field (the same reader reads the guardian's number on the student profile anyway). A genuine product judgment (screenshot-safety vs usable artefact); changes `guardianPhone`/`matronPhone` in two key-sets. **Kofi recommends full numbers; DECIDE before Claude Code wires it.** DIRECTIVE carries no phone regardless.

**AC for Quinn — S2/S3/S6 now WIRED (was Quinn M2), + S7–S13 new:**
- **S2** PARTIAL: `Object.keys(dormCardView).sort() === CHRONIC_ROW_KEYS.partial`; clinical cols (conditionDetail/baselineStatus/careGoals/emergencyProtocol/meds/external*) **absent not empty**; matron read of the same entry = full key-set (positive control). **S3** DIRECTIVE: keys === `[directiveNote, entryId, scope]` (+ wrapper studentId/studentName); **no** condition value/label/pill/colour anywhere. **S6 (load-bearing)** — constructing a PARTIAL projection with an extra `conditionDetail` key and asserting `=== CHRONIC_ROW_KEYS.partial` must **FAIL** (allowlist generalises where a denylist won't); pin runs at the reader boundary on the real object, not the TS type.
- **S7** widest-wins (FULL+DIRECTIVE live → FULL; FULL expired + DIRECTIVE live → DIRECTIVE; MATRON+grant → FULL, audit scope=null; exactly one projection per reader×entry). **S8** PARTIAL void on MH (grant refused; reclassified entry → name-only floor, RED if the dorm card returns). **S9** §04 lists ALL live grants (revoke DIRECTIVE → FULL still shown live; HEADMASTER §04 zero rows for a MH entry). **S10** two enforcement points (delete the §04 INNER JOIN → still zero grant rows for HM on a granted MH entry via the `hm_restricted` RLS arm; flip the arm to `IS NOT NULL` → RED while X3 green). **S11** Print absent for MH + DIRECTIVE; a PARTIAL/FULL print writes one `exported` audit row (direct POST). **S12** grant/revoke authz (HEADMASTER/ADMIN refused app+RLS; STUDENT/PARENT/non-school-user refused; revoke stamps `revoked_at`, row survives, no `scope` UPDATE/DELETE; house-tie live→dead→live; via direct POST). **S13** grantee reaches detail only (list + §04 → `notFound()`); grantee open writes `_read` with `scope={winning}` (null for default clinical), deduped, no `.returning()`, session-bound actor.
- **Unchanged:** S5 byte-identical `notFound()`; X1–X16/X-MUT (shipped verifier); `SICKBAY_ROLES` grep-pinned; R3 statement-flat. **Do NOT build an AC on a direct helper call** (Sarah L1 — dev superuser bypasses RLS).

**Files 23b binds:** `lib/sickbay/chronic-copy.ts` (add `ChronicDormCardView`+`ChronicDirectiveView`+the `partial`/`directive` pins) · `lib/sickbay/chronic-reads.ts` (per-scope projection + widest-wins + audit scope) · `lib/actions/sickbay-chronic.ts` (`grantAccess`/`revokeAccess`) · `app/(app)/senior/sickbay/chronic-register/access-grants/` (§04) + a `print` route · `db/schema/sickbay.ts` (read-only, NO change). **The one-line risk: a wrong allow-list on PARTIAL is a child's psychiatric label handed to a sports master; the `Object.keys` pin (S6) is the tripwire — RED before the projection is trusted GREEN.**

**R139 OWNER DECISION (2026-07-25): phone masking → FULL NUMBERS.** Render the complete guardian + matron number to any reader whose scope includes the field (PARTIAL/FULL_PLAN). No masking formatter. Grounds: masking is incoherent on a dorm card whose purpose is *who to call* in a crisis, and the same reader already reads the guardian number on the student profile. DIRECTIVE carries no phone regardless.

### INCR-23b · BUILD COMPLETE — ✅ Quinn GREEN · Dex APPROVE · Sarah CLEAR-TO-MERGE (2026-07-25)
Commits `009c596..6f84a67` on `senior-feat` (app-layer only, **no migration** — 0058 shipped every column). **753 tests, typecheck/lint/build clean.** Files: `lib/sickbay/chronic-copy.ts` (the 3 frozen key-sets + `partialProjection` seal + view types + audit formatter) · `chronic-reads.ts` (per-scope projection, widest-wins, read-audit scope, `getAccessGrants`/`getDormCardForPrint`) · `lib/actions/sickbay-chronic.ts` (`grantAccess`/`revokeAccess`) · `components/sickbay/chronic-grant-form.tsx` + `chronic-revoke-button.tsx` (scalar-only client) · `access-grants/page.tsx` (§04) · `[studentId]/[entryId]/dorm-print/page.tsx` · seed (3 demo grants).

- **The three frozen key-sets (R132) hold** — FULL_PLAN = the 24-key entry; PARTIAL = the 7-key dorm card (clinical columns absent-not-empty, meds → derived `dormMedNote` string); DIRECTIVE = `{directiveNote, entryId, scope}`, nothing entry-derived. Quinn ran the real reader per scope; the S6 pin is RED-capable at the reader boundary; all four projections (incl. FULL/FLOOR) now route through a runtime pin (Dex LOW-1).
- **🔴 The MEDIUM-3 value-safety seal is SEALED and TESTED.** RLS can't mask columns, and the key-set pin is keys-only — so the ONLY control keeping a psychiatric label out of a dorm card on a reclassified (ASTHMA→MENTAL_HEALTH, FK-CASCADE) entry is the `hm_restricted` degrade to a name-only FLOOR. **Quinn MINOR-2: that branch was untested — neutering it left all 751 green.** Extracted to the pure `partialProjection`, given a committed test that asserts no clinical VALUE survives (`JSON.stringify` sweep), and **Sarah + I both mutation-proved it RED** (Quinn's exact `if(false)` mutation now reds the R132.1 test). The reader reads the ENTRY's fresh `hmRestricted`, never the grant's stale copy; the detail route is a pure server-component tree, so no clinical value crosses the client boundary for a PARTIAL/DIRECTIVE/FLOOR reader.
- **Grant/revoke** MATRON-only (app + the settled DB `WITH CHECK='MATRON'` backstop), HEADMASTER/ADMIN/STUDENT/PARENT/non-school-user refused, cross-tenant impossible, **append-only** (revoke stamps `revoked_at`, no DELETE, no `scope` UPDATE — both negatives test-pinned). §04 clinical-reader-only (a grantee → `notFound()`; a HEADMASTER sees zero grant rows for an MH entry — E18), audit a pure formatter that never names a condition, `reason` nulled server-side for non-matron. Read-audit `onConflictDoNothing`, no `.returning()`, session-bound actor, scope=winning/null. Print absent for MH + DIRECTIVE, writes an intent-to-print row.
- **Gate fixes folded in (commit `6f84a67`):** MINOR-2 seal + test; LOW-1 (FULL/FLOOR pinned); LOW-2 (revoke surfaces a refused action instead of silently reappearing); MINOR-1 (authored E20 copy wired — `GRANT_ONLY_MATRON` refusal + `GRANT_MH_CONSEQUENCE` shown to the matron at the point of choice). The §04 `reason` matron-only render kept (Quinn+Dex: nulled server-side, nothing crosses the wire; empty HM column is cosmetic).

A scope-aware SELECT would be hardening only. Same 23a residual, unchanged.

## INCR-24 · Sickbay medication layer · Kofi R141–R170 build spec (2026-07-25)
**Migration is `0060`** (0059 = student_subject_enrolment). **Canonical rounds = THREE: 06:30/12:30/21:00 (R13)** — the surfaces' 17:00 round + 13:00 column are demo drift and LOSE. **SPLIT 24a/24b, ONE migration 0060 (R166).**

**A · The append-only MAR (the actual, vs the chronic-med PLAN)**
- **R141** PLAN (`sickbay_chronic_med`/standing order) vs ACTUAL (the MAR = one row per administration EVENT); a "due dose" is DERIVED (schedule × civil day × round), never stored (R101/R32 derived-state doctrine).
- **R142 `sickbay_med_admin` (0060)** — `id`·`school_id`; `student_id` composite FK (always present); `visit_id` nullable composite FK (NULL for a routine round dose); `slot_id` nullable composite FK **RESTRICT** (the round it's attributed to; NULL for PRN/ad-hoc); the R143 source pointers; **R144 SNAPSHOTS** `drug_name`/`dose_label`/`route`/`is_controlled`/`dispensed_qty` copied at administration (never read live from the plan — the `clinician_name` doctrine); `status` enum; `administered_at`+`administered_by_user_id` (SET NULL + app `assertSchoolClinician`); `witness_user_id`+`witness_override_reason`; `notes`; `corrects_admin_id` nullable composite self-FK RESTRICT + `amendment_note`; `created_at`. **NO `updated_at`/`voided_at`/delete — absence IS the append-only constraint.** `tenant_uk (school_id,id)` INLINE (self-FK + 0033 order). Indexes `(school_id,student_id,administered_at)`, `(school_id,slot_id,administered_at)`, `(school_id,visit_id)`.
- **R143 source = PROVENANCE never permission (R60 built).** enum `sickbay_med_source=[CHRONIC,STANDING_ORDER,DOCTOR_ORDERED,AD_HOC]` + the matching composite RESTRICT pointer (`chronic_med_id`/`standing_order_id`/`consult_id`; Wells ADDs `sickbay_doctor_consult_tenant_uk` in 0060). A DOCTOR_ORDERED row is attribution only — the visiting doctor (not a system user, R21) is never a co-sign/gate. CHECK: the non-null pointer matches `source`.
- **R144** `is_controlled` PINNED at administration (else de-listing a drug next term retroactively makes a past un-witnessed controlled dose look compliant). CHECK `is_controlled=false OR dispensed_qty IS NOT NULL`.
- **R145** `status sickbay_med_status=[GIVEN,REFUSED,HELD,OMITTED]` — a refused/held/omitted dose is a recorded EVENT. SELF_ADMIN deliberately NOT a status (→ O1).
- **R146 append-only; a correction is a NEW row (R36).** No update path; the correcting row sets `corrects_admin_id`+`amendment_note`, the original is byte-unchanged; the reader renders a footnoted amendment. *A MAR that can be edited is a falsifiable clinical record.*

**B · Rounds firing (`today` §2)**
- **R147** a round-dose ties to its round by `slot_id`. **R148 the due list is a DERIVED READ, not a table:** live `chronic_med` where `slot_id=R.slot_id` AND entry active + `on_site_treatable` AND today's Accra civil weekday ∈ `slot.days_of_week` AND (runs-on-holidays OR not a holiday); "done" once a terminal MAR row exists for (student, med, civil-day). Statement-count guard stays flat. **R149 overdue is derived, NO scheduler** (the `medical-hold.ts` "there is NO SCHEDULER" doctrine) — nothing auto-writes OMITTED; the matron closes an overdue dose by writing a MAR row. **R150 PRN is not round-driven** — a PRN med appears in no round list; a PRN dose enters the MAR directly (`is_prn`, `slot_id NULL`, criteria in `notes`).

**C · Controlled-substance register (D5.2 "brief")**
- **R151** controlled = `sickbay_stock_item.is_controlled` boolean the school flags (do NOT seed a Ghana narcotics schedule → O3). **R152 the register is a DERIVED balance over an append-only movement ledger — no stored balance (R10):** balance = Σ RECEIPT − Σ(controlled GIVEN MAR `dispensed_qty`) − Σ WASTAGE ± Σ ADJUSTMENT. `sickbay_controlled_movement` (0060, append-only, LEAF, FORCE RLS): `stock_item_id` composite RESTRICT · `movement_type sickbay_stock_movement_type=[RECEIPT,WASTAGE,ADJUSTMENT]` (administrations are NOT a movement row — read from the MAR, one source of truth) · `quantity`·`occurred_at`·`actor_user_id`(SET NULL+guard)·`witness_user_id`(controlled WASTAGE requires a witness — the diversion point)·`batch_ref`·`reason`. **"Brief" EXCLUDES** batch/lot lifecycle, expiry alerting, multi-store, procurement/PO, cyclic double-count, cabinet-key logs. **R153** `dispensed_qty` on the MAR is the deduction (CHECK-required for controlled GIVEN).

**D · N&MC witness (D5.3 — the sharpest rule)**
- **R154 MANDATORY for a controlled GIVEN administration, BOTH layers.** DB CHECK: `NOT (is_controlled AND status='GIVEN') OR witness_user_id IS NOT NULL OR witness_override_reason IS NOT NULL` — a controlled dose reaches the table only with a witness OR a recorded override, never silently. **R155 the witness IDENTITY is app-layer, never free text, never a student:** a real `ref_user` in THIS school with an N&MC licence via `assertSchoolClinician(schoolId, witnessUserId, {requireNmc:true})` (the DB can't — `ref_user` global; `staff_profile` is tenant `(school_id,user_id)` + `nmc_licence_number`, a real tenant join). A student Sick Bay Prefect can NEVER be witness-of-record; the surface's "witnessed F. Tetteh" is a free-text `note` ("prefect assisted with check-off"). **R156 the override is documented, single-signature** (no second approver — a co-signed override defeats the "no witness available" premise); renders in the register (HM notification → O5). **R157 witness on a non-controlled dose OPTIONAL but must still pass `assertSchoolClinician`; self-witness FORBIDDEN** (CHECK `witness_user_id IS NULL OR <> administered_by_user_id`). **R158 `assertSchoolClinician` — EXTRACT (Sarah advisory 2), don't copy-paste `holdsMatronRole`** — asserts a clinical (MATRON) role in this school; `{requireNmc:true}` adds a non-null in-school `nmc_licence_number`; applied to every clinical actor pointer (administered_by, witness, movement actor/witness).

**E · Standing orders**
- **R159 `sickbay_standing_order` (0060, §3):** `complaint`·`treatment`·`escalation`·`ordered_by_doctor_name` (copied text, NOT a `ref_user` — R21)·`active`·`created_by_user_id`·`tenant_uk (school_id,id)` INLINE. Created/edited by `[ADMIN,MATRON]` (matron GAINS §3 write). **R160 provenance not a gate (R60)** — `ordered_by_doctor_name` is attribution; the matron administers under her own authority; a MAR row references it via `source=STANDING_ORDER`.

**F · Stock + Risk 4**
- **R161 stock is PER-DRUG, school-level, never per-student.** `sickbay_stock_item` (0060): `drug_name`·`form_label`·`unit`·`qty_on_hand` (a stored manually-maintained **reorder aid**, a deliberate corner NOT an audit record — `ponytail:` upgrade to a movement ledger only if non-controlled audit is ever required)·`reorder_point`·`last_restocked_at`·`is_controlled`·`active`·`tenant_uk` INLINE. **No auto-decrement from the MAR for non-controlled stock**; only the controlled balance is derived (R152). **R162 🔴 Risk 4 — no student beside a drug on the SHARED stock/register screen.** The stock row carries NO `student_id`/student text; the surface's `Hydroxyurea 500mg — for Adwoa Mensa` and `Calamine (Adwoa's chronic supply)` are REFUSED (drug + student = a sickle-cell re-identification on a screen the ADMIN can read). Drug-beside-student is correct INSIDE the MAR (it IS the clinical record), forbidden on the shared stock/register. **R163 a patient's own surrendered supply is not school stock** — a MAR `source=CHRONIC` note "patient's own bottle · NHIS-supplied"; no stock deduction, not in the controlled balance.

**G · Gates**
- **R164 the MAR is gated like the VISIT, not the chronic register.** Read=`SICKBAY_CLINICAL_READ_ROLES` (HEADMASTER+MATRON, NOT ADMIN — D2); write=`SICKBAY_CLINICAL_WRITE_ROLES` (MATRON). The MAR is the acute/round clinical graph (`visit_id`/`slot_id`), gated by the app-layer clinical pair — NOT `staff_grant_scope`. A housemaster with a chronic FULL_PLAN grant sees the PLAN, not the admin log (grantee MAR access → O2). **R165 stock/standing-orders/controlled register = `[ADMIN,MATRON]` (§3, new `SICKBAY_STOCK_WRITE_ROLES`)** — shows drug·type·qty·date·actor·witness, NEVER a student name (R162).

**H · Split & migration**
- **R166 SPLIT 24a/24b, ONE migration 0060.** All 4 tables + 3 enums + the `sickbay_doctor_consult_tenant_uk` ADD-UNIQUE ship in 0060 in **24a** (one prod paste). Standard tenant tables — `ENABLE+FORCE+tenant_isolation+parent_deny` catalog-driven; **NO staff_grant_scope** (clinical narrowing is app-layer, the 22a posture). **24a** = spine + config (`assertSchoolClinician`; standing-order/stock/controlled-register writes; setup §3; the carried Wells fixes; no visit dependency). **24b** = the clinical MAR (append-only write + witness gate + controlled deduction; derived rounds read; today §2 + visit-record §3; Doctor-ordered provenance) — the witness/append-only crux with its own full gate.
- **R167 carried obligations into 24a. (a) `resetScheduleSlots` is DONE (R100) — do not reopen.** Still open: **(b)** `validateRoundOrdering` TOCTOU (`updateScheduleSlot`/`toggleScheduleSlot` read the slot set OUTSIDE `withSchool` → read `FOR UPDATE` in-tx + re-validate); **(c)** a slot-create action must refuse a second anchor before the DB unique violation; **(d)** `admitPatient` reads `getSickbayConfig` outside the tx (~555/558) → move the bed/isolation read inside `withSchool` `FOR UPDATE`; **(e)** `medical-hold.ts` non-sargable `::date` casts → half-open timestamp ranges + add index `(school_id, student_id, admitted_at)` (runs on every register save at every Senior school forever).

**AC for Quinn (1–21):** append-only MAR (1 correction=new row, no update/void/delete; 2 footnoted amendment) · witness (3 DB CHECK rejects controlled GIVEN with neither witness nor override; 4 override accepted+rendered; 5 `assertSchoolClinician{requireNmc}` rejects non-clinical/foreign/no-NMC witness app-layer; 6 self-witness CHECK; 7 controlled non-GIVEN needs none; 8 non-controlled witness optional but clinician-only, never a student prefect) · 9 clinician tenancy on every actor pointer (app-layer, DB can't) · derived rounds (10 due appears/leaves on a terminal MAR row; 11 PRN in no round list; 12 retired/not-on-site excluded; 13 no due-dose table, statement count flat; 14 overdue derived, nothing auto-writes OMITTED) · controlled register (15 balance derived not stored; 16 controlled GIVEN needs `dispensed_qty`; 17 no student name on the register — ADMIN sees drug names only) · carried fixes (18 concurrent slot-edit → exactly one fails via FOR UPDATE; 19 second anchor refused; 20 admitPatient read in-tx; 21 no `::date` cast + the index exists).

**🔶 OWNER-ESCALATE (all defaulted, none block the build):** **O1** self-admin status → recommend GIVEN+note, not a distinct status (a SELF_ADMIN status attests to something the clinician didn't do). **O2** grantee MAR visibility → recommend no grantee (housemaster) MAR access at 24 (grants reach the PLAN, not the admin log; widening is a new boundary). **O3** controlled-drug list → recommend the school flags `is_controlled` per item; don't seed a Ghana narcotics schedule (a regulatory claim the product shouldn't make). **O4** retention (D5.1 extension) → the MAR + controlled ledger are TWO more clinical/regulatory record stores the owner wasn't asked about; recommend the same 7-yr setting, no purge at 24, no false UI claim. ⚠️ **RESTRICT FKs mean a student with controlled-administration history cannot be hard-erased within the window** (regulatorily correct, but confirm since D5.1 covered only three objects). **O5** witness-override escalation → recommend recorded+surfaced in the register only, no blocking co-sign, no HM notification at 24.

**Surface ⟂ spec (SPEC wins on logic, HTML on visuals):** (1) witness = CONTROLLED substances + a second N&MC clinician, not "any drug by a non-matron" and not a student prefect; Lucy adds the override-reason affordance. (2) rounds = THREE not four; no 13:00. (3) stock `for Adwoa Mensa`/`Adwoa's chronic supply` REFUSED (Risk 4). (4) "witnessed F. Tetteh" (prefect) → a note; the demo rows are the OLD prefect-supervision model, not the D5.3 controlled-witness rule.

**INCR-24 OWNER DECISIONS (2026-07-25):** **O4 → EXTEND D5.1.** The 7-year-post-exit retention setting governs the MAR + controlled ledger too; no purge machinery at 24; no UI claim of a retention the repo can't perform. Owner acknowledged the RESTRICT-FK consequence: a student with controlled-administration history cannot be hard-erased within the window (regulatorily correct for a controlled-drug record). **O2 → NO GRANTEE MAR (plan only).** The MAR is gated Matron+Headmaster (like the visit record, R164); a grant reaches the care PLAN, not the administration log. Widening is a later boundary decision. **O1/O3/O5 → Kofi defaults** (self-admin = GIVEN+note; the school flags `is_controlled` per item, no seeded national schedule; the witness-override is recorded+surfaced in the register only, no blocking co-sign or HM notification at 24).

A passive advisory on that input ("Administrators can read this — do not enter a student's name"); NOT a hard reject, NOT a roster scanner. The structural R162 guarantee (no `student_id` column reaches §3) is untouched.

**Part 2 · the MAR write (`recordMedAdmin`)**
- **R170** MATRON-only (`authorizeMedAdminWrite`, first statement, the `[MATRON]` seam) · **`withSchool` NOT `withStaffScope`** (the MAR is gated like the VISIT, R164, not grant-scoped) · `administered_by` = the **session actor** (`resolveActor`, no client-supplied administrator — closes a falsifiability path; a matron can't attribute her dose to another) · **APPEND-ONLY, ONE write** — the module exports only `recordMedAdmin`, whose only verb on the MAR is INSERT; no edit/void/delete symbol. A correction is `recordMedAdmin` again with `correctsAdminId` (re-resolved `(school_id,id)`) + required `amendmentNote`; the corrected row is byte-unchanged; chains allowed.
- **R171** source↔pointer contract: re-resolve the pointer server-side, refuse a mismatch FIRST (the 0060 `med_admin_source_pointer_match` is a backstop). CHRONIC→`chronic_med_id` OPTIONAL (R163 patient-own-bottle); STANDING_ORDER→`standing_order_id` NOT NULL; DOCTOR_ORDERED→`consult_id` NOT NULL (attribution only, R160); AD_HOC→none.
- **🔴 R172** snapshots pinned at write; **`is_controlled`/`stock_item_id`/`dispensed_qty` are SERVER-resolved, NOT client-trusted** — `is_controlled` copied from the resolved `sickbay_stock_item.is_controlled` (else a client forges `is_controlled=false` on a controlled drug to skip the witness). A round dose re-resolves `chronic_med_id → entry → student_id` (never a client studentId).
- **R173** status GIVEN/REFUSED/HELD/OMITTED (SELF_ADMIN → GIVEN+note, O1); a controlled non-GIVEN sets `dispensed_qty=0` + `stock_item_id` (the all-controlled CHECKs) — the witness CHECK bites only on controlled GIVEN.
- **🔴 R174 the witness/override (D5.3 crux):** witness field surfaces when `is_controlled && status==='GIVEN'`. Witness via `assertSchoolClinician{requireNmc}` — a second in-school N&MC clinician, dropdown filtered to N&MC ≠ actor (Dex polish); self-witness forbidden (app + `med_admin_witness_not_self`). Override `witness_override_reason` = documented single-signature (R156). App refuses first ("A controlled dose needs a witness or a recorded override reason."); the DB CHECK `med_admin_controlled_given_witness` is the backstop. Non-controlled witness OPTIONAL (still a clinician, no requireNmc). **The controlled GIVEN deducts via the READER (`dispensed_qty`), never a `sickbay_controlled_movement` row** (R152 one source of truth — a movement row would double-count).

**Part 3 · derived rounds (`getMedicationRounds`, today §2, R175)** — slots (MEDICATION_ROUND active, anchor first) × due (`chronic_med JOIN entry` WHERE `slot_id=round`, `is_prn=false`, `entry.active`, `on_site_treatable`, Accra civil weekday ∈ `days_of_week`, holidays rule) minus done (a terminal MAR row for `(chronic_med_id, civil-day)`, any of the 4 statuses). **OVERDUE derived at read (past `starts_at`, ≥1 non-terminal); NO scheduler, nothing auto-writes OMITTED.** PRN never in a round (the `is_prn=false` filter on `chronic_med`). **FIXED statement count — 3 queries, partitioned in memory, no per-student N+1 (Dex A3).** The matron opens a round → Record each due dose (MATRON only; HEADMASTER sees none) → the dose leaves the list next render.

**Part 4 · visit §3 + gate + split**

Original retained."; chains render each link; nothing greyed to "voided"). Doctor-ordered hyperlinks the consult.
