## INCR-26 · Three-tier notification chain + comms thread (console-only SMS) · Kofi R206–R214 build spec (2026-07-26) — CLOSES LANE B
**Migration: NONE** (0062 authored `sickbay_notification` + enums + `notification_log` reuse; R213 confirmed no gap — if Claude Code hits one, STOP). Surfaces: `referral-log §02-thread` (622-698) + `§03 notify-timeline` (750-922) + `visit-record §05` (930-1139). Lucy map: `docs/senior/sickbay-notifications-surface-map.md`. Homes: pure `lib/sickbay/notify.ts` · action `lib/actions/sickbay-notify.ts` · reads `lib/sickbay/notify-reads.ts`.
- **🔴 R206 — console-only SMS boundary (D7, headline).** The notify action does 3 writes then STOPS: INSERT `sickbay_notification`; for SMS-channel INSERT `notification_log` (`status='QUEUED'`, `provider='console'`, phone/message/studentId/sentBy); link `notification_log_id`. **STRICTER than the boarding precedent (structural, not config):** the notify path does NOT import/call `getSmsProvider()`/`sendSms()`, reads no `HUBTEL_*`, touches no secret (grep-clean). **QUEUED is TERMINAL** (nothing dispatches → nothing advances to SENT/FAILED). Future real-dispatch = one seam (route step 2 through `sendSms()`).
- **R207/R214a — tier = event SEVERITY (derived), recipient = fan-out (separate axis).** tier1 = routine visit (**no auto row**; manual pastoral/discharge SMS only), tier2 = admission, tier3 = consult/referral. Derived from the event (visit/admitted/referral). `recipient` fans out: tier2→PARENT (SMS), tier3→PARENT (call+SMS confirm) **and** HOUSEMASTER **in parallel** (policy, one confirm writes both). **HEADMASTER digest + DISTRICT_HEALTH → INCR-27** (enum authored, never fired here). Lucy's "intensity gold/warn/terra" = the colour of severity; `channel` carries SMS-vs-CALL per row.
- **🔴 R214 conflict-1 — NO "escalate" control anywhere.** tier-3 HM row is policy-offered with the parent send; tier-2 HM fallback ("phone by HM if no ack" — undetectable in console-only) uses the **existing generic "Send manual"** affordance, not a bespoke button. Nothing new built.
- **R208 — write semantics (R197): append-only EVENTS + ONE in-place stamp.** Every send/inbound/attempt = a NEW row; no row's `body`/`private_note`/`recipient`/`tier`/`direction`/`channel` is ever mutated, none deleted. **The ONE permitted update: a plan row's `scheduled_for → sent_at` + attach `notification_log_id`** (fulfillment, not history-mutation; the schema has no status enum, so the timestamp IS the lifecycle). RETRY = new row citing the failed one via `retry_of_id` (shape-only in 26 — console-only yields no organic FAILED).
- **R209 — comms thread.** OUTBOUND (staff→parent, SMS/CALL) + INBOUND (parent call/SMS **manually RECORDED** by staff — no webhook; `answered`/`call_duration_seconds`; `notification_log_id` NULL). `notification_log` link = **SMS-OUTBOUND only** (a no-answer CALL = `answered=false`, no FAILED log row). Anchor: `referral_id` (+visit_id) → §02 referral thread; `visit_id` only → §05 visit log; `student_id` always set (durable per-student anchor).
- **R210 — scheduled send (B9): derived-due-at-read + manual send, NO cron.** A plan row (`scheduled_for` set, `sent_at` NULL) renders DUE when `scheduled_for <= now` (inject `now`), reduced-opacity `.future`; matron sends manually (stamps `sent_at`). Nothing auto-fires.
- **🔴 R211/R214-conflict-2 — parent-facing boundary (F4).** ONLY `body` is parent-facing (the SMS text / call summary). **`private_note` = MATRON-ONLY** (trimmed server-side from the HEADMASTER projection — ONE reader, one writer; F-H); `trigger_label` + all metadata = staff-only. **R214b: the `recipient=HOUSEMASTER` body is a FIXED medical-detail-light TEMPLATE** ("student under sickbay care, no medical detail to share") — never free-typed clinical text (A10 hardened; a matron can't leak a diagnosis into an HM message).
- **R212 — gate.** WRITE=[MATRON] (first statement); READ full thread=[HEADMASTER,MATRON] (minus `private_note` for HEADMASTER); HOUSEMASTER = only `recipient=HOUSEMASTER` body (no surface built here — gate ruled so a future one can't leak); ADMIN+BURSAR = nothing (zero-SQL gate). **D8: `sickbay_notification` stays `parent_deny`, NO `parent_scope`, no RLS change, no new prod-paste.**
- **AC for Quinn (NF1–NF13):** NF1 console boundary (no provider/HUBTEL symbol in the notify diff; QUEUED/console rows) · NF2 QUEUED terminal · NF3 tier derived, illegal tier refused · NF4 tier-3 writes PARENT+HM from one action, no HEADMASTER/DISTRICT row, **no "escalate" control in the diff** · NF5 append-only (no update/delete) · NF6 the one `scheduled_for→sent_at` stamp, re-click no-op · NF7 retry shape (`retry_of_id`) · NF8 inbound (`direction=INBOUND`, no log link) · NF9 log-link SMS-outbound only · NF10 due-at-read, no cron symbol · NF11 F4 (`private_note`/`trigger_label` staff-only; parent-scope reader gets `body`+`sent_at`+`channel` ONLY; **private_note absent from HEADMASTER shape**) · NF12 gate matrix + parent_deny · **NF13 HM body = fixed template, a clinical HM body refused**.
- **🔶 OWNER-FLAGS (defaulted, none block):** F-A console-only STRICT-structural (vs boarding's `sendSms`-console — confirm strict or relax). F-B tier=severity (not recipient). F-C no auto-fire (manual send). F-D pastoral tier-1 auto-elevation deferred. F-E HEADMASTER digest + DISTRICT_HEALTH → 27. F-F surface delivery telemetry (85%/retry/Failed) is seed-only demo of the future dispatch — live console renders "Queued · console (not dispatched)", omit-not-fake. ## INCR-39 · L2a offline-scores session re-key (Sarah's L2a backlog) · building 2026-07-27
The delicate one — touches the Score-Ledger PWA shared-tablet PII defence (R3). Sarah's L2a finding: `changeOwnPassword`'s re-auth (`signInWithPassword`, R264) ROTATES the Supabase `session_id`, which is the offline-buffer partition key (`pwa-store.ts recordKey = sessionId::subject::period`); so a teacher with pending offline scores who changes their password → `PwaSession`→`purgeSnapshots(newSessionId)` deletes the old-keyed buffer → lost.
- **Design = Sarah's recommended SELF-CHANGE-SCOPED re-key** (NOT a partition-key swap, NOT touching the purge logic): because the trigger IS the user changing their OWN password, every `oldSessionId::*` record belongs to them → re-keying `old→new` is inherently same-identity (no shared-tablet regression; a different teacher's records sit under a different session prefix, untouched).
  1. `lib/score-ledger/pwa-store.ts` — new `rekeySnapshots(oldSessionId, newSessionId)`: rename each `oldSessionId::*` record to `newSessionId::*` (update `key` + `sessionId`), best-effort, no-op if equal/empty.
  2. `lib/actions/auth.ts` `changeOwnPassword` — return the NEW `session_id` (via `getSessionId()` after the re-auth rotates it): `{ ok, error?, newSessionId? }`.
  3. `components/auth/change-password-form.tsx` — accept a `sessionId` prop (the pre-change session); on success, if `res.newSessionId && res.newSessionId !== sessionId` → `await rekeySnapshots(sessionId, res.newSessionId)` BEFORE the success UI (so it beats the next `PwaSession` purge). Parent path: harmless no-op (no ledger buffer).
  4. `/settings/security` + `/(parent)/account` pass `sessionId={await getSessionId()}`.
- **NOT changing** `recordKey`/`purgeSnapshots`/`PwaSession` partition logic (R3 mechanism unchanged) — lowest-risk. `session_id` isn't a secret (partition key, non-authz) so returning it is fine. NO migration/prod-paste.
- **✅ Quinn GREEN · Dex APPROVE · Sarah PASS (2026-07-27). Stack: `e223e91`.**
  - **Quinn GREEN** — 1107 tests, build ok; behavioral test (fake-indexeddb) proves both properties non-vacuously; 3 mutations bite incl. the load-bearing one (drop the `${old}::` prefix filter → B collides onto A's re-keyed record → cross-teacher assertion RED); the `old===new` guard is load-bearing (else put+delete on the same key self-destroys) and covered.
  - **Dex APPROVE** — self-scoping correct (`::` blocks `"A"` vs `"AB::"`), R3 mechanism byte-identical (`recordKey`/`purgeSnapshots`/`PwaSession`/layout untouched), `pwa-store` has zero runtime imports beyond `indexedDB` (no server leak). 3 non-blocking nits (optional callback seam; harmless empty parent ledger DB; best-effort error path).
  - **🔴 Sarah PASS (R3)** — the re-key CANNOT cross teachers: touches only the acting user's own `${oldSessionId}::` prefix, both args server-derived (not attacker-supplied), destination is the same user's new session; `purgeSnapshots` purge-on-identify unweakened; fail-open = availability-only (worst case = the original bug, recoverable, never a leak); `newSessionId` is the non-secret partition key. Insight: client-side same-origin IndexedDB grants a devtools-equipped local attacker no new capability; R3's real defence is the non-technical-handoff purge, unchanged.
- **🏁 INCR-39 MERGED (PR #204) 2026-07-27. senior-feat ↔ main synced (`f560745`). 🎉 ACTIONABLE BACKLOG CLEARED.**

Staff CPD (the Friday counterpart to VLC's Wednesday). **Attendees are STAFF, not students → NO confidential pastoral layer, NO parent involvement, NO student PII; OPERATIONAL / SHOWN throughout.** Reuses VLC's operational precedents heavily (INCR-40 F0 spine, 42a register, 41 present-by-default) and NONE of the confidential ones (42b/43 REDACTED, read-matrix, HM-exclusion) → materially lighter than VLC. **Scope (owner-resolved 2026-07-30):** the 2 mapped surfaces (`plc-programme-setup`, `plc-session-register`) PLUS a **persisted CPD-points ledger** (OC5 — owner pulled it forward; points ACCRUE to a real auditable table, not just derived at read-time). Still DEFERRED of the "06": NTC portal-sync (external gov integration = stop-and-ask) + SMS nudge (Hubtel = paid external = console-degrade) + the polished teacher-reflection surface + dashboards.
- **INCR-47 · PLC programme-setup spine** (surface 01) — config: cadence + the CPD-points contract (accrual rates that feed the INCR-49 ledger) + N school-authored PLC groups + facilitator/membership/term-focus per PLC. **Gate = the NEW `PD_COORDINATOR` role** (OC1 — double-hattable: an additive `KnownAppRole` grantable on top of VHA/FM/Dean/any base role; a user holds it *alongside* their base role) + ADMIN/HEADMASTER god-tier. ~3–4 SHOWN tenant tables. No split. **Kofi must rule on:** the role's assignment model (admin-assigns-later vs an onboarding wizard input — per [[onboarding-inputs-cascade]], if onboarding collects it, it must auto-configure).
- **INCR-48 · PLC session register** (surface 02) — the facilitator's weekly register: P/L/A present-by-default attendance + per-session facilitator-authored agenda (JSON) + derived lifecycle clock + CPD-points panel + a MINIMAL in-app `plc_reflection` 3-question submit. **Write-gate `canFacilitatePlcSession` = own-PLC facilitator IDENTITY match (userId === plc.facilitator_staff_id) ∥ a MANAGEMENT break-glass arm** (OC2 — set TBD by Kofi; candidate = `[PD_COORDINATOR, HEADMASTER]` and/or VHA, to cover an absent facilitator). Branches off main AFTER 47 merges (no stacking).
- **INCR-49 · PLC persisted CPD ledger** (OC5 pulled forward) — points ACCRUE to a real `plc_cpd_*` ledger (per teacher, per term, auditable) from attended/facilitated sessions + reflections, per the INCR-47 accrual contract; a teacher CPD-total/statement read. **No dedicated surface mapped** (the ledger surface is one of the deferred 4) → surfaces as a total on the session-register + a lightweight statement; Lucy shapes minimal. **Deferred+fenced:** NTC portal-sync (stop-and-ask) + SMS nudge (console-degrade). Branches off main AFTER 48 merges.
- **Reuse map:** `lib/vlc/defaults.ts` coalesce → `lib/plc/defaults.ts` `coalescePlcProgramme` (Friday 15:30/60min/12wk defaults); `lib/vlc/session-clock.ts` → **extract pure time helpers to a shared `lib/senior/time.ts`** (the `lib/senior/form.ts` 4th-copy precedent, Dex-directed) rather than a 2nd copy; `prep_attendance`/`vlc_session_attendance` present-by-default → `plc_attendance`; `canWriteSession` own-identity gate → `canFacilitatePlcSession`; `VLC_CONFIG_*` shape + `WASSCE_SETUP_ROLES` naming → `PLC_CONFIG_*`; `chrome.tsx`; canonical `attendanceStatusEnum` (capture P/L/A, store-not-reject E/M — no new enum); add every `plc_*` to `SHOWN_AUDIT_ENTITIES` (none prefixed → must be listed or build fails).

# MODULE 4.7 — PTA (Parent-Teacher Association) — 6 surfaces, INCR-50..56 · Pence decomposition (2026-07-31)
The FIRST senior module where **PARENTS return as first-class actors** (officers, meeting attendance, dues) → **`parent_scope` RLS RETURNS** (unlike VLC/PLC's parent_deny-throughout). **DUES = MONEY → REUSE the EXISTING Basic-tier billing engine** (`fee_category`/`invoice`/`invoice_line_item`/`payment`/`payment_allocation`/`receipt` + forward-only discount audit, in `db/schema/fees.ts`+`billing.ts`; the dues surface says "same billing engine, separate accountability"), NOT a parallel `pta_dues_*` ledger; **NEVER provision a payment gateway (MoMo/Hubtel = stop-and-ask, standing rule)** — cash/manual recording only. SMS everywhere = queued/gated (Hubtel). Surface 06 (`parent-teacher-meetings`) is **NOT PTA** (self-declares — 1:1 conferences + visiting-day, notes→student record, ~80% value is the SMS-blocked booking) → INCR-56, recommend DEFER.
- **INCR-50 · PTA structure-setup spine** [SPINE — blocks all; NOT blocked by any OC] — surface 01. Four-tier config (Form/House/General/Emergency: active, frequency, officer-role SET, quorum, dues config + forward-only history) + **idempotent generation** of `ptas` (N Form from classes, M House from Houses, 1 General, Emergency on-demand). New: `pta_tiers_config`, `ptas` (tier_type, `scope_ref`=class_id|house_id|null), `pta_dues_config_history` (append-only). FORCE+tenant_isolation+parent_deny, SHOWN, composite FKs. Admin-only. Gate: generation idempotency + forward-only history.
- **INCR-51 · Officer matrix** [off main after 50] — surface 02. `pta_officer` (tier×role×person: parent|staff|external, multi-hat, term, election_ref, ex-officio auto-derived). **Officer = DATA position, NOT a KnownAppRole** (OC3). Gate: `canActAsPtaOfficer` identity-gate ([[vlc-pastoral-confidential-model]] canAccessPastoralFlag pattern — a parent-officer gains PTA-write for their OWN PTA without a widened app-role). parent_deny (full matrix officer-only; parent own-hats → 55).
- **INCR-52 · Meeting lifecycle + DUAL attendance register** [off main after 50] — surface 03. **REUSES PLC/VLC INCR-48 session-register VERBATIM** (open/hold, present-by-default P/L/A, derived clock/KPIs, write-gate IDOR). **TWO parallel registers (teachers + parents — GES compliance)**. Quorum derivation gates binding-decision UI; unmarked→absent on close. `pta_meeting`, `pta_meeting_attendance` (register=TEACHER|PARENT). parent_deny. Gate: Secretary/officer write-gate IDOR + quorum. **⚠ creates the on-demand Emergency `ptas` instance at convene AND must EXCLUDE EMERGENCY from `reconcilePtas`'s close-sweep** (INCR-50 Quinn note — else a later `generatePtas` closes the live Emergency PTA).
- **INCR-53 · Minutes + resolutions + action items** [off main AFTER 52→main — [[stacked-pr-orphan-risk]]] — surface 04. `pta_minutes` (1:1 meeting), `pta_agenda_item` (Discussion/Action/Resolution), `pta_action_item`, `pta_resolution`. Two-stage draft→Chair-review→**adopted=IMMUTABLE** (INCR-49 immutability + readiness-adopt). Gate: immutability-after-adoption (mutation-proven). parent_deny (adopted-to-parents opened in 55 if OC1 rules visible).
- **INCR-54 · PTA dues** [off main after 50; MONEY; SPLIT 54a/54b] — surface 05. **REUSE billing engine** + thin `pta_id` link (nullable on `invoice_line_item` or a `pta_dues_charge` bridge), NOT parallel `pta_dues_*` (OC2). **54a** = category-link + invoice generation + cash/manual recording + Treasurer read report (expected/collected/outstanding, per-tier, aged). **54b** = instalment plans + SMS reminders → DEFERRED (plans ≈ existing partial `payment_allocation`; SMS=Hubtel). Gate: money forward-only correctness + Treasurer-vs-bursar boundary + **NO gateway** (Sarah confirms). [[reports-data-is-server-only]] for the report.
- **INCR-55 · Parent PTA read — the `parent_scope` RETURN** [off main after 52+53+54a] — the module's defining authz work. **NO new tables** — adds `parent_scope` RESTRICTIVE policies (`app.current_parent_user`) to the 51/52/53/54 tables via `withParentScope`. Parent reads OWN dues+membership/hats+attendance + adopted minutes for own PTAs (OC1); denies full matrix/other-family-dues/ledger/drafts. REUSE INCR-46 first-VLC-parent-read + `lib/db/rls.ts` `withParentScope`. Gate (Sarah-heavy): **0 cross-family rows on every PTA table**.
- **INCR-56 · Parent-teacher 1:1 conferences + visiting-day scheduler** [SEPARATE, independent] — surface 06 (NOT PTA). In-app scheduler/queue/notes→student record; SMS booking = Hubtel stop-and-ask. **Recommend DEFER** until SMS provisioned.
- **Critical path:** 50 (spine, merge FIRST) → {51, 52, 54a} parallel off main → 53 (after 52→main) → 55 (after 52+53+54a→main). 56 independent/deferred.
- **✅ OWNER-CALLS RESOLVED (2026-07-31):** **OC3 → officer = DATA position + identity-gate** (`canActAsPtaOfficer`, NO new KnownAppRoles). **OC2 → dues = REUSE the billing engine** (fee_category/invoice/payment + thin pta_id link), cash/manual recording only, **NO payment gateway** (MoMo = separate stop-and-ask). **OC1 → parent-visibility = OWN SLICE *+ PUBLIC TRANSPARENCY*** (wider than rec): a parent sees own dues + own membership/hats + own attendance + own-PTA adopted minutes (parent_scope, own-child), **PLUS the officer matrix AND adopted GENERAL-meeting minutes are visible to ALL parents of the school** (association transparency). ⇒ **INCR-55 has TWO read models:** (a) `parent_scope` own-child (dues/membership/attendance/own-PTA-adopted-minutes) + (b) a **school-wide-parent-readable** classification for the officer matrix + adopted general minutes (any parent of the tenant reads them — NOT student-linked). This shifts INCR-51 (matrix gains a public-read dimension) + INCR-53 (adopted general minutes gain a public-read dimension) — still ship parent_deny at build; 55 opens both reads. Kofi rules the exact school-wide-parent-read RLS shape at 55. Defaultable (flag at PR): SMS gated everywhere; Treasurer cross-category existence-only (never other-category amounts); Emergency tier no standing officers/dues.

## INCR-54a · PTA dues — billing-engine link + generation + cash recording + Treasurer report — SPEC (Kofi · R459–R473 · AC PTA54-1..30) · 2026-08-01
MONEY increment. OC2: REUSE the Basic billing engine (`fee_category`/`invoice`/`invoice_line_item`/`payment`/`payment_allocation`/`receipt`), NOT a parallel ledger. **🔴 Decisive finding: the billing engine is parent_DENY today** (NO `parent_scope` — only the `/r/{token}` receipt link is parent-facing) → the task's "parents already see invoices" premise is FALSE. Adds ONE table (a bridge). Surface 05's `pta_dues_invoices/payments/reminders/plans` = SUPERSEDED (pre-OC2 guess). Key rulings:
- **R459** ONE "PTA dues" `fee_category` per school (tier DERIVED via bridge, NOT per-tier); dues on a **DEDICATED dues invoice** (NOT a line on tuition — per-invoice balance can't express "dues outstanding, tuition paid"; a dedicated invoice makes paid/balance = collected/outstanding by construction).
- **R460 the thin link = a `pta_dues_charge` BRIDGE** (1:1 the dues line item): `line_item_id` (composite FK, UNIQUE), `pta_id` (composite FK), `tier_type` (denorm), `academic_year`+`academic_period_id`, `basis`+`cadence` (CHECK, snapshots), `subject_student_id` (always — billed student / rep sibling), `household_id` (nullable, PER_FAMILY), `rate_snapshot` (money). LEAF. NOT a column on `invoice_line_item` (a bare pta_id can't carry period/basis/family, nor be parent-scoped independent of tuition in 55).
- **R461** Form=PER_STUDENT (one invoice/charge per active student), General=PER_FAMILY (one per household → rank-1 sibling; household-less = family-of-one); House/Emergency dues-disabled → nothing.
- **R462 generation IDEMPOTENT** — one charge per (school,pta,subject,billed-period) via **3 partial-unique indexes** on the bridge; re-run onConflictDoNothing = 0 new; VOIDED re-issuable.
- **R463 FORWARD-ONLY rate** — read `pta_dues_config_history` (greatest `effective_from ≤ period start`), **snapshot** onto `line_item.amount` + `rate_snapshot`; a later history row NEVER re-rates an issued invoice; no rate in force → nothing generated.
- **R464** generation authority = the existing issuance authority `[ADMIN,HEADMASTER]`, explicit (tier×period) NOT cron; the parent Treasurer does NOT generate.
- **R465** payment cash/manual ONLY via existing `recordPayment` (`aggregator` NULL; CASH/BANK_TRANSFER/CHEQUE/manual-MoMo-receipt) → payment/payment_allocation(INVOICE→dues invoice)/receipt(public_token); **NO gateway** (MoMo/Hubtel stop-and-ask). MoMo-float/Hubtel-reconcile/SMS-all DEFERRED (no 54a data source — omit/"not configured", never fabricate).
- **🔴 R466 Treasurer-vs-Bursar (OWNER CALL):** 54a ships bursar/admin records (existing authority). The parent-Treasurer WRITE arm (`canActAsPtaOfficer(Treasurer)` records OWN-PTA dues) = a parent write into the money engine → owner call [rec APPROVE with a hard own-PTA fence: `pta_id ∈ held Treasurer offices`, NEVER tuition, NEVER another PTA]. Until confirmed, bursar records on the Treasurer's behalf — 54a complete either way.
- **R467** Treasurer report = server-only read over the engine via the bridge: Expected=Σ`rate_snapshot`, Collected=Σ dues-invoice `paid_amount`, Outstanding=Σ `balance_amount`, per-tier + per-PTA-instance drill + aged escalation. Honest (no fabricated collected).
- **R468 cross-category = EXISTENCE-ONLY** boolean (`has_other_arrears` via `EXISTS(non-dues invoice balance>0)`), NEVER other-category amounts (the surface's "GHS 800" SUPERSEDED — data-exposure, spec wins). **R469** report read = `canActAsPtaOfficer(Treasurer)` own-PTA ∪ management `[ADMIN,HEADMASTER]` school-wide; no bare role.
- **🔴 R470 parent-visibility reconciliation:** dues NOT parent-visible in 54a (billing all parent_deny; only the `/r/{token}` receipt link, unchanged); held to 55. **55 must NOT blanket-`parent_scope` the `invoice` table** (leaks TUITION — RLS is row-level) → scope the **bridge** (own family) + read amount/status from `rate_snapshot` (+ derived paid flag) WITHOUT touching `invoice`, OR a dues-only `EXISTS(pta_dues_charge reaching this family)` carve-out. The bridge's `rate_snapshot`+family identity exist FOR this. Reconciles OC1 (own-child parent_scope, delivered in 55).
- **R471** bridge isolation: FORCE+tenant_isolation+parent_deny (NO parent_scope — 55 adds), composite FKs, LEAF, SHOWN → `SHOWN_AUDIT_ENTITIES`. Honesty: `dues_enabled=false` → 0 charges/invoices, empty report.
- **🔴 R472 the ONE non-additive edit:** the tuition skip-existence query in `lib/actions/billing.ts` (`issueAllInvoices` + `generateInvoicesForClass`) must EXCLUDE PTA-dues-only invoices (`NOT EXISTS(pta_dues_charge …)`) — else a dues invoice makes tuition issuance skip the student (UNDER-BILL). Single predicate, both callers. Quinn mutation-tests (dues→tuition still billed; removing guard reds).
- **R473 scope fence:** 54a = category+dedicated-invoice+bridge+idempotent/forward-only generation+cash recording+Treasurer report+existence-only+the R472 guard+isolation. DEFERS: plans (54b ≈ existing partial `payment_allocation`), SMS reminders (Hubtel), MoMo float/Hubtel reconcile (gateway), the parent own-dues read (55), the Treasurer write-arm (R466 owner call), Emergency levies (R458), Excel export.
- **Wells:** ONE table `pta_dues_charge` + the 3 partial-uniques + **the R472 `billing.ts` edit (flag — non-additive)**; ~migration **0078** + prod-paste **0081** (confirm); RLS counters +1. Ensure the "PTA dues" `fee_category` idempotent upsert; reuse the existing sibling-rank logic for PER_FAMILY.

### SPEC (Kofi · R444–R458 · AC PTA53-1..27) · 2026-08-01
Post-meeting record; 4 new tables; WIRES `canActAsPtaOfficer` a 2nd time (Secretary drafts / Chair adopts). Builds on the CLOSED INCR-52 meeting + `quorum_met`. Surface 04 (Secretary drafting view + validator; distribution channels Hubtel/PDF-DEFERRED). Reuses INCR-49 immutability + the readiness-freeze + `meeting-clock`. Key rulings:
- **R444** 4 SEPARATE tables (`pta_minutes`, `pta_agenda_item`, `pta_action_item`, `pta_resolution`); composite `(school_id,…)` FK CHAIN CASCADE; inline tenant_uk on minutes + agenda_item (FK targets); action + resolution LEAF. Fixed domains = CHECK not enum.
- **R445 `pta_minutes` 1:1 meeting:** `status` CHECK (DRAFT|CHAIR_REVIEW|ADOPTED, default DRAFT), `secretary_id`/`adopted_by_user_id` SET NULL, `adopted_at`/`distributed_at` nullable; **`UNIQUE(school_id,meeting_id)`**; NO stored preamble/period (derive).
- **R446 `pta_agenda_item`:** `classification` CHECK (DISCUSSION|ACTION|RESOLUTION, **NULLABLE while draft**), `narrative`, `seq_no`; **SEED one row per meeting `agenda_json` item on draft-create**; editable-until-adoption.
- **R447 `pta_action_item`** (child of agenda_item): `description`, owner `person_user_id` XOR `external_name` (at-most-one CHECK), `deadline` nullable(=Ongoing), `status` CHECK (PENDING|DONE), `completed_at`. LEAF.
- **R448 `pta_resolution`** (child of agenda_item): `resolution_no` **NULLABLE-until-adopt**, `resolution_text`, `votes_for`/`_against`/`_abstain` NOT NULL CHECK≥0, `binding` NOT NULL (default TRUE for GENERAL tier); **`UNIQUE(school_id,resolution_no)`**; outcome (PASSED⟺for>against) DERIVED not stored. LEAF.
- **R449** classification = single value, **NO NLP** — the Secretary classifies + fills the structured fields by hand; reclassify-away-from-ACTION/RESOLUTION removes spawned children.
- **R450 lifecycle DRAFT→CHAIR_REVIEW→ADOPTED** (+ reversible CHAIR_REVIEW→DRAFT). **draft-create requires the meeting ENDED (`now≥endMs`)** + `canActAsPtaOfficer(Secretary)` ∥ break-glass; **adopt requires the meeting WRITE-LOCKED (`now≥lockMs`)** + `canActAsPtaOfficer(Chair)` ∥ break-glass (stamps adopted_at/by + assigns resolution numbers). Server-loaded offices, no bare role. Adopt-after-lock ⇒ preamble permanently stable (no snapshot column).
- **🔴 R451 ADOPTED = TOTAL IMMUTABLE** (the INCR-53 fence): NO UPDATE/DELETE/child-INSERT on an adopted minute + its subtree, app-enforced (no trigger — the INCR-49 ledger + readiness-freeze pattern); corrections by a FUTURE amending minute (no `amends_` column). Action-completion tracker DEFERRED (keeps the fence total). **Sarah mutation-tests** (a forged UPDATE/DELETE/child-INSERT against an adopted subtree is refused).
- **R452 QUORUM→RESOLUTION gate:** a `pta_resolution` exists only when `pta_meeting.quorum_met = TRUE` (strictly; NULL/false refused); classifying an item RESOLUTION below quorum is refused; **drafting is quorum-INDEPENDENT** (an inquorate meeting is still minuted). App-enforced (no cross-table CHECK).
- **R453** `resolution_no` assigned **AT ADOPTION** (not draft — a discarded draft burns no number), per `(pta_id × academic_period_id)`, frozen `{scope}-{period}-{NNN}` (NNN=MAX+1 under the UNIQUE guard); provisional preview while drafting.
- **R454 preamble DERIVED** from register+officers (no stored columns): PTA/meeting name, date/times/location, Chair, Secretary, parent present/late/absent aggregates, teacher present-count, `quorum_met`+rule. Stable because adopt-after-lock.
- **R455 submit-for-review (DRAFT→CHAIR_REVIEW) validation:** (a) EVERY agenda item classified; (b) EVERY ACTION has an owner; (c) EVERY RESOLUTION has all 3 votes + `quorum_met=true`. Deadline ADVISORY (Ongoing is legal).
- **R456 isolation:** all 4 FORCE+tenant_isolation+parent_deny, SHOWN → `SHOWN_AUDIT_ENTITIES` (omit fails build); NO triggers; migration **0077** + prod-paste **0080** (confirm); RLS counters +4; parent 0 at build.
- **R457 INCR-55 parent-read flag (OC1 resolved, ADOPTED-only, TWO models):** (a) own-child own-PTA adopted minutes via `pta_minutes→pta_meeting→ptas→scope→students→student_guardian.user_id=app.current_parent_user`; (b) school-wide **GENERAL-tier** adopted minutes to EVERY tenant parent (R429 transparency). PUBLIC projection = preamble aggregates + narratives + resolutions (incl. vote tallies) + action items; OFFICER-ONLY = any draft/chair_review + per-parent attendance rows. Un-adopted → 0 parent rows.
- **R458** distribution = `distributed_at` column only (SMS/PDF/email/GES channels DEFERRED — Hubtel/PDF); amendment = R451; resolution→fee-category DEFERRED (INCR-54).
- **Wells:** 4 tables; migration **0077** + prod-paste **0080** (confirm). **Owner edges (flag at PR):** R450 adopt-waits-for-write-lock (rec-keep; alt = snapshot preamble at adopt for instant adoption); drafter≠adopter not hard-blocked (rec-keep — distinct Secretary/Chair offices provide it); action-tracker + typed amendment deferred.

Dual teacher/parent register; **REUSES PLC/VLC INCR-48 session-register machinery** (clock/write-lock/present-by-default) + **WIRES `canActAsPtaOfficer` LIVE** (first live use — the module's IDOR fence). Surface 03 (State-3 live register + close; State-1 SMS-scheduling DEFERRED). Key rulings:
- **R431 `pta_meeting`:** manual-convene (NO cron); `pta_id` composite FK, `academic_period_id` (resolved from `meeting_date`), `meeting_type` (display label, NO logic branch), date/times, location, `agenda_json` (reuse INCR-48 shape), `invited_teacher_user_ids` jsonb, `quorum_met` bool **NULLABLE**, `convened_by_user_id` SET NULL, inline `tenant_uk` (FK target of attendance). NO stored status/counts.
- **R432 lifecycle DERIVED** (scheduled/held/closed) via `lib/pta/meeting-clock.ts` (grace = `tier_settings.register_lock_grace_hours` coalesce 24h); write-lock reused (refuse-after-close). Early-close (`closed_at`) DEFERRED.
- **R433 REGULAR register writer = the PTA's Secretary** (stored ∥ derived ex-officio) via `canActAsPtaOfficer` ∥ **`PTA_MEETING_BREAKGLASS_ROLES=[ADMIN,HEADMASTER]`**. Chair/any-officer write DEFERRED (flag, rec-no).
- **R434 `pta_meeting_attendance` DUAL:** `register` CHECK (TEACHER|PARENT); `user_id` SET NULL (teacher) XOR `student_guardian_id` SET NULL (parent — best-effort, SMS-only guardians have no user_id); `register_identity` CHECK; `attendanceStatusEnum` (P/L/A capture, E/M store-not-reject); TWO partial uniques (per-register identity); LEAF.
- **R435 PER-REGISTER DEFAULT POLARITY (the honesty crux):** TEACHER = present-by-default (no row=present, PLC-verbatim); **PARENT = absent-by-default** (no row = awaiting-while-live / absent-once-closed; a row = PRESENT/LATE arrival). "unmarked→absent on close" = a pure DERIVATION flip, ZERO writes / no bulk absent rows. Quorum present-count = PRESENT+LATE. **Deliberate surface-grounded deviation from INCR-48's single polarity** (stores the live 17-present/13-awaiting distinction present-by-default can't). Flag, rec-confirm.
- **R436 per-tier roster DERIVED:** teacher = ex-officio Secretary ∪ `invited_teacher_user_ids` ∪ walk-ins; parent = PRIMARY guardians of the scope's students (Form→class / House→house / General→all).
- **R437 COUNT-ONCE:** a person = exactly one register row; officer identity = display tag, NOT a 2nd row (parent-officer→1 PARENT row+tag; staff-officer→1 TEACHER row+tag); twin-parent deduped by user_id; **staff-who-is-also-a-class-guardian = teacher-wins-when-invited** (POLICY EDGE, flag rec-confirm).
- **R438 QUORUM** = display `quorum_rule` text + DERIVED present-count/% ; **`quorum_met` = SECRETARY JUDGMENT** (nullable bool, NOT auto-derived — free text has non-countable clauses); gates the binding-resolution/minutes UI (**INCR-53 enabled only when `quorum_met=true`**). Structured quorum DEFERRED.
- **R439 write-gate `authorizePtaMeetingWrite`** mirrors INCR-48 `authorizeFacilitatorWrite`: the **meeting→ptas join SERVER-loads `pta_id`/tier/`school_id`**; offices SERVER-loaded; `canActAsPtaOfficer(secretaryOffice)` ∥ break-glass; member-in-register + refuse-after-lock. **NO bare role satisfies the officer arm; a Secretary of PTA-A cannot write PTA-B's register.** Sarah mutation-tests (no-role-alone; Chair-parent refused; cross-PTA refused).
- **R440 EMERGENCY convene:** `conveneEmergencyMeeting` inserts a NEW `ptas` EMERGENCY instance (status ACTIVE, class/house NULL) + the meeting in ONE tx; convener = `[ADMIN,HEADMASTER]` ∥ General-Chair-by-identity; multiple Emergencies allowed; auto-archive DEFERRED.
- **🔴 R441 `reconcilePtas` CLOSE-SWEEP EXCLUSION** (the INCR-50 Quinn forward-note): add `&& e.tierType !== "EMERGENCY"` to the close condition — a **ONE-LINE change to the pure `reconcilePtas` in `lib/pta/defaults.ts`** (the increment's single non-additive edit; done by Claude Code, not Wells). Emergency is NEVER machine-closed. Sarah/Quinn mutation-test (create ACTIVE Emergency → `generatePtas` → stays ACTIVE; removing the predicate reds).

One new table `pta_officer` + one identity-gate `canActAsPtaOfficer`. Officer = DATA (OC3, NO KnownAppRole). Builds on the spine (`ptas`, `officer_roles`, `tier_settings` — **COALESCE the keys, spine seeds `{}`**). Surface 02. Key rulings:
- **R419 `pta_officer`:** `pta_id` composite FK `(school_id,pta_id)`→ptas CASCADE; holder = `person_user_id` nullable SET NULL→ref_user **XOR** `external_name` (rare external, e.g. BOG member); **`person_type` DERIVED** (guardian-link=parent / staff-role=staff / external_name=external — display tag, authz NEVER keys on it); `office`, `assignment_basis`, `election_ref`, `term_start`/`term_end`, `ended_at`/`end_reason`; at-most-one holder CHECK (exactly-one app-side); **LEAF, NO tenant_uk**; index `(school_id, person_user_id)`.
- **R420 `office`** = string validated app-side ∈ the tier's `officer_roles` (NO CHECK/FK — per-school config data); drift-tolerant (a config edit removing an office doesn't cascade onto live rows; write-time validation only).

Flag: child-departure auto-termination (rec warn-not-end).
- **R423** `assignment_basis` CHECK `(ELECTED, APPOINTED)` (the meaningful binary — drives term auto-calc); `election_ref` **NOT NULL mandatory** (free-text audit of how appointed; the appointer = election_ref + audit actor, not a 3rd stored axis).
- **R424 ex-officio DERIVED, NEVER stored** (auto-updates on staff change): General → Headmaster (`tier_settings.headmaster_role`, coalesce HEADMASTER — an APPENDED member, does NOT occupy an elected slot); Form Secretary = the class's `class_teacher_user_id`; House Secretary = the house's `hm_user_id`; the `ex_officio_office` (coalesce "Secretary") OCCUPIES its `officer_roles` slot + is NOT electable (guard rejects a stored officer for it).

General-Chair/Form-Master self-service officer-write **DEFERRED** (flag, rec-no — an identity write-arm on a write gate needs an owner call; the appointer is recorded as data on-behalf).
- **R428 isolation:** FORCE+tenant_isolation+parent_deny, composite FK + SET NULL person, SHOWN → `SHOWN_AUDIT_ENTITIES`; **parent 0 at build** (public read = 55).

Coexists with OC1 own-child `parent_scope`.

### SPEC (Kofi · R410–R418 · AC PTA50-1..24) · 2026-07-31
Config trunk (VLC-40/PLC-47 analogue); admin-only, SHOWN, parent_deny (parent_scope RETURNS in 55, NOT here). Unblocked by all 3 OCs (stores config+instances only — not permissions/meetings/invoices). Surface 01 (`schoolup-pta-structure-setup.html`, 779 lines). Key rulings:
- **R410 · 4 FIXED tiers** FORM/HOUSE/GENERAL/EMERGENCY (CHECK, not enum, not extensible). Per-tier flat cols (active, `frequency_norm`=string label, **`officer_roles`=JSON array of office-name strings — a data list, NO permissions** (OC3 boundary), `quorum_rule` free text, dues config) + a **`tier_settings` JSONB bag** (heterogeneous per-tier scalars round-tripped OPAQUE — NOT EAV; spine never branches on it). One `pta_tiers_config` per (school×tier), `UNIQUE(school_id,tier_type)`.
- **R411 · generation = EXPLICIT idempotent scope-keyed upsert** (NOT cron — "tomorrow morning" is presentation). `ptas` scope = **two nullable typed composite-FK cols** (`class_id`→class, `house_id`→house), NOT polymorphic (preserves [[composite-tenant-fks]]). **Partial unique indexes**: Form `(school_id,class_id) WHERE FORM`; House `(school_id,house_id) WHERE HOUSE`; **General singleton `(school_id) WHERE GENERAL`** (partial, NOT plain UNIQUE — NULL-distinctness trap allows 2 Generals; this is PTA50-8, the idempotency crux). - **R412 · archived scope** class/House → PTA `status=CLOSED` (soft, preserved, read-only), NEVER orphan/hard-delete; re-activate → SAME row re-opens; tier toggled off → its instances CLOSED (preserved), re-on re-opens. Scope FK `onDelete RESTRICT`.
- **R413 · dues** rates live on `pta_tiers_config` + **every change appends an immutable `pta_dues_config_history` row** (append-only-hard, `reason` NOT NULL mandatory, `effective_from` forward-only — **backdating REJECTED**, future allowed; never re-rates existing invoices). Spine stores rates+history ONLY; invoices = INCR-54 (reads history by `effective_from`).
- **R414 · Emergency** = no standing officers (`officer_roles=[]`), no standing dues (`dues_enabled=false`), **ZERO instances generated** (on-demand at convene in 52). Validations reject non-empty Emergency officers/dues.
- **R415 · `PTA_CONFIG_WRITE_ROLES=[ADMIN, HEADMASTER]`**, NO new KnownAppRole (OC3 — officers are data). Gates config write + Generate. (PROPRIETOR flagged rec-no.)

- **Wells:** `pta_tiers_config` / `ptas` / `pta_dues_config_history`; the **General-singleton PARTIAL unique is the idempotency crux**; ~migration **0074** + prod-paste **0077** (confirm at build). **Defaultable (flag at PR):** Form-PTA in COMBINED = one per active class school-wide (rec); PROPRIETOR out of write set; backdating rejected.

### SPEC (Kofi · R397–R409 · AC PLC49-1..34) · 2026-07-31
Module 4.6 FINAL increment (OC5 pulled the ledger forward; owner added the dashboard). **PLC 8-pt arm ONLY** (NOT the 20-pt multi-source CPD ledger). Reuses `lib/plc/points.ts` VERBATIM (the anti-double-count seam — ledger == the INCR-48 display by construction). Scope = `plc_cpd_ledger` (1 new table) + `lib/plc/ledger.ts` (accrual) + teacher PLC-CPD statement (surface 03 PLC arm) + school CPD dashboard (surface 04 PLC arm). Key rulings:
- **R398 accrual = LAZY MATERIALISE-ON-SETTLE, idempotent.** A session accrues only AFTER it settles (now ≥ write-lock = scheduled-close + `reflection_window_hours`); triggered by the READ path via `lib/plc/ledger.ts` (upsert one frozen row per attended member of each settled-unaccrued session, via `points.ts` UNCHANGED); idempotent (UNIQUE key + deterministic re-derivation + on-conflict-nothing). **NO cron, NO manual "post", INCR-48 mark/confirm actions NOT modified** (rejected event-triggered — last event precedes settle; rejected manual-post — a forgettable button = silent missing points).
- **R399 settle** = now ≥ write-lock; accrual counts events ≤ settle (`submitted_at ≤ settle` AND `confirmed_at ≤ settle`); a confirm stamped AFTER settle does NOT retro-add (deterministic, terminates). Store `settled_at` (view-independent award instant).
- **R400 `plc_cpd_ledger`** — one frozen row per (school, session, member) with total>0 (attended members only): `school_id`, `session_id` (composite FK → `plc_session.tenant_uk` CASCADE), `user_id` (SET NULL → `ref_user`), `attended_pts`/`reflection_pts` numeric (stored; total=sum, not stored), `settled_at`, `created_at`; **UNIQUE(school_id,session_id,user_id)**; LEAF (no tenant_uk). `plc_id`/`period`/`date` DERIVE via the session join (not columns).
- **R401 POINT-IN-TIME FREEZE** — row written for members active at settle, pts from settle-time rates; later membership/rate edits DON'T rewrite accrued history. Supersedes (for settled sessions) the `session-data.ts:402` live-roster approximation.
- **R402 teacher STATEMENT** — DERIVE totals (`SUM(attended+reflection)` over rows), NO rollup table, PLC-only, **own-identity read** (`WHERE user_id = viewer`), X/8 vs `annual_plc_target` (NOT clamped at 8 — target≠cap), honest 0/8 empty (no fabricated total/forecast/sample rows).
- **R403 isolation** — FORCE+tenant_isolation+parent_deny (NO parent_scope), SHOWN → `"plc_cpd_ledger"` in `SHOWN_AUDIT_ENTITIES`; the accrual write is a derived materialization of already-audited events → **NO new user-audit entry**.
- **R404 DEFERRED fence** — NTC portal-sync (no `ntc_synced` col/chips/"Sync to NTC now"), SMS, the 20-pt multi-source ledger, 3-yr licence cycle/calendar/`ntc_licences`, evidence uploads, forecast/pace, gap-analysis — all OMITTED (dropped, not faked).
- **R405 dashboard READ-authz** — NEW `PLC_DASHBOARD_READ_ROLES=[PD_COORDINATOR, HEADMASTER, VICE_HEADMASTER_ACADEMIC, ADMIN]` (= config-write set **+ VHA**: read/oversight ⊋ write/author — VHA monitors CPD but doesn't configure, stays OUT of `PLC_CONFIG_WRITE_ROLES`/`PLC_SESSION_BREAKGLASS_ROLES`). Gated `hasAnyRole` server-side on route + reader. **HoD department-scoping DEFERRED** (school-wide to the 4 management roles only; HoD-alone/plain-teacher/student/parent get 0). The module's 2nd app-gate — a READ-gate, not the IDOR write-fence.
- **R406 dashboard DATA** — pure derived read (no new table): per-staff PLC X/8 from ledger SUM (point-in-time), school summary (avg, on-track vs below-pace counts), at-risk = **below-PRO-RATA** (default: accrued < 50% of points made available by held sessions to date, held-only — NOT a dishonest below-8; TUNABLE constant). Numerator from the ledger; denominator reuses the `deriveTermProgress` held-only derivation.
- **R407/R408/R409** — dashboard omit-not-fake (drop Sync-to-NTC, licence calendar/column/expiry, forecast/pace, 20-pt "Annual progress"→PLC X/8, evidence, dept/special-category filters, licence-non-renewal framing); dashboard is READ-only, app-gated (role gate scopes management — RLS alone would let any same-school staff SELECT the SHOWN ledger), shares the R398 lazy-accrual seam, adds NO schema beyond `plc_cpd_ledger`.
- **Owner-notes (flag at PR):** OC#3 confirm-cutoff accepted (late confirm earns 0); the at-risk 50%-pro-rata threshold is a tunable display default. **Wells: ~migration 0073 + prod-paste-0076 (confirm at build).** Sarah: mutation-test the NEW read-gate (management see the rollup; teacher/HoD-alone/student/parent → 0) + R398 idempotency/no-double-count + R401 point-in-time.

### SPEC (Kofi · R381–R396 · AC PLC48-1..30) · 2026-07-30
The Friday register (VLC-42a analogue), OPERATIONAL/SHOWN, materially lighter than VLC. Captures 3 event streams (attendance / agenda / reflection) + DISPLAYS a derived CPD-points panel; writes NO ledger (that's INCR-49). Surface `schoolup-plc-session-register.html` (620 lines, goofy-poitras). Key rulings:
- **R381 lifecycle = manual-open, derived-state.** A `plc_session` row exists ONLY when a facilitator/break-glass OPENS it (the VLC `openSession` upsert; one row per PLC×date). States DERIVED not stored: SCHEDULED=cadence date no row · HELD=row exists · MISSED=past cadence date no row · auto-lock (read-only) once write-window elapsed (`isSessionWriteLocked`). **NO cron, NO pre-materialised rows, NO stored status/started_at/closed_at.** Break-glass `[PD_COORDINATOR,HEADMASTER]` may open/hold/mark/edit-agenda/confirm (full authorship, R377).
- **R382 `plc_session`** minimal: `plc_id` (composite FK), `academic_period_id` (composite FK, resolved from date), `session_date`, `topic` (nullable sub-topic), `agenda_json` (jsonb), `opened_by_user_id` (SET NULL); **UNIQUE(school_id,plc_id,session_date)** = open-upsert target; inline `tenant_uk UNIQUE(school_id,id)` (FK target of the other 2). NO stored status/counts/points. Headline = the PLC's R375 term-focus; `topic` beneath.
- **R383 present-by-default attendance** — roster = live active `plc_membership`; P/L/A canonical `attendanceStatusEnum` (no new enum); **PRESENT = ABSENCE of a row** (mark-present deletes the row); Late==Present for CPD; store-not-reject E/M (earn 0). `plc_session_attendance` UNIQUE(school_id,session_id,user_id).
- **R384 write-gate** — every mark re-checks `canFacilitatePlcSession(roles, actorId, session.plc.facilitator_user_id)` **server-loaded facilitator id via session→plc join (NEVER request-supplied)**, session-derived school_id, member-in-PLC check, refuse-after-lock. The module's ONE IDOR fence — Sarah mutation-tests no-role-alone.
- **R385 agenda** = editable `agenda_json` (`{items:[{text,durationMin?,done}]}`), facilitator-authored, editable-until-lock, **NOT append-only**; no agenda-item table (YAGNI). Action-item→task-list extraction DEFERRED.
- **R386 reflection = a SEPARATE `plc_session_reflection` table** (NOT columns on attendance — present-by-default means attendees have no attendance row, yet they're exactly who reflects). UNIQUE(school_id,session_id,user_id); `q1/q2/q3` (or `answers_json`), `submitted_at`, `confirmed_at`+`confirmed_by_user_id`. **Corrects the surface's schema sketch** (which put reflection on `plc_attendance` + used `staff_id`).
- **R387** 3 fixed school-generic questions (takeaway / commitment / next-session-question), in `lib/plc/`, not stored/configurable. **R388** submit = member OWN-identity (server-derived, never request-supplied), window-bound [scheduled close, close+`reflection_window_hours`], **append-only-hard** (never UPDATE/DELETE answers). **R389** confirmation = facilitator one-way stamp (`confirmed_at`+`confirmed_by`), NOT window-bound, distinct from the append-only answer. **R390** window DERIVED from scheduled close (no stored `held_at`/`closed_at`; Ghana UTC+0).
- **R391 the 48/49 boundary (crux):** 48 PERSISTS events only (session/attendance/reflection+confirm), DERIVES+DISPLAYS the points panel + KPIs via a NEW pure `lib/plc/points.ts`, and writes **NO `plc_cpd_*` ledger row**. INCR-49 is the SOLE ledger writer, importing the SAME `points.ts` → display==accrual by construction; UNIQUE(attendance)+UNIQUE(reflection) guarantee ≤1 attended-event + ≤1 confirmed-reflection-event per member×session → no double-count.
- **R392 SUPERSEDE the surface's "+1.0 fixed facilitator" & "5.0 total"** — per R372 the facilitator is an ordinary attendee (0.5 attended, NO bonus, NO reflection pt); the surface's card is internally double-counted. Honest derived total for the mock's 8 attendees = 4.0 attended + reflections as each non-facilitator's is confirmed-in-window.
- **R393 the exact derivation contract** (shared 48-display/49-accrual): `attended_pts` if Present(no row)∨Late; `reflection_pts` if attended ∧ ≠facilitator ∧ submitted≤window-close ∧ confirmed; cap `max_pts_per_session` (1.0; facilitator 0.5); % denominator = held-sessions-only; 75%/`annual_plc_target` never cap a per-session value.
- **R394 omit-not-fake** — SMS arm ENTIRELY deferred (in-app prompt only, never Hubtel — the "SMS reminder"/"nudge SMS"/"trigger reflection nudges" copy dropped); NTC-sync/`ntc_synced`/NEW-induction-badge/action-item-extraction/"Sessions cancelled" tile all OMITTED; un-held session shows no register/points; lifecycle shows a derived points *preview* ("will award"), not "auto-posted to ledger" (that's 49).
- **R395 isolation** — `plc_session`/`plc_session_attendance`/`plc_session_reflection` = FORCE+tenant_isolation+parent_deny (no parent_scope), composite FKs, all SHOWN → each in `SHOWN_AUDIT_ENTITIES` (reflection answers are SHOWN — staff CPD ≠ pastoral; audit = metadata only, never answer body). **R396 scope fence:** 48 = open/hold + attendance + agenda + reflection submit/confirm + derived display ONLY; INCR-47 spine consumed read-only byte-unchanged.

- **OC-1 session-creation model** DEFAULTED to Kofi R381 (manual-open/derived-scheduled — mirrors VLC, same visible schedule, no cron/orphan non-events; surface says "auto-generated weekly" but that's a LOGIC fork the precedent wins) — flag at PR. **OC-2 explicit cancellation** DEFERRED per Kofi (a called-off week = not-held/MISSED; no CPD consequence since % counts held-only) — flag at PR. ### SPEC (Kofi · R366–R380 · AC PLC47-1..27) · 2026-07-30
Staff-CPD config trunk (the VLC-F0 analogue), OPERATIONAL/SHOWN throughout — attendees are STAFF so NO confidential layer, NO student PII, NO parent path. Anchor = NTC licence renewal (20 CPD pts/yr, 8 from school-based PLCs). Key rulings:
- **R366 `PD_COORDINATOR`** — catalog/seam add (`KNOWN_APP_ROLES` + `STAFF_ROLES` picker + label + `ref_role` seed), **free-text `ref_role.code` → NO enum, NO migration for the role**; **rank-1** in `rankOf` (inert/escalation-safe — cannot grant ADMIN/HM/PROPRIETOR); **admin-assigns-later** via `/staff` (`STAFF_ADMIN_ROLES`-gated); NO onboarding cascade (post-onboarding settings surface, nothing to seed). Additive/double-hattable by construction (a user holds it *alongside* a base role).
- **R367 `PLC_CONFIG_WRITE_ROLES=[PD_COORDINATOR, ADMIN, HEADMASTER]`** (`as const satisfies`) — gates EVERY config write. **VHA deliberately NOT in the write set** (an academic leader who configures PLC *wears the hat*).
- **R368 config READ = `isStaff`** (no `PLC_CONFIG_READ_ROLES` array) — a bare TEACHER who facilitates must read cadence/focus/contract for INCR-48.
- **R369** no mandatory coordinator; ADMIN/HM are the bootstrap fallback + grant the first hat; NO last-coordinator guard.
- **R370 `plc_programme` singleton** + `coalescePlcProgramme` (Friday ISO-5 / 15:30 / 60min / 12wk defaults, never null/throw) + `configured_at` honesty. `session_end` & `max_pts_per_session` **DERIVED, never stored**.
- **R371 CPD contract = 4 editable scalars** on the singleton: `pts_per_attended_session` 0.5, `pts_per_reflection` 0.5, `reflection_window_hours` 48, `annual_plc_target` 8. `max_pts_per_session` (1.0) DERIVED. **SUPERSEDES the surface's `cpd_points_rules · condition_json` EAV note** (OC4 — flat scalars, no rules-engine).
- **R372 accrual rules** (INCR-49 runs on these): Present/Late earns attended pts (**Late==Present**), Absent 0; reflection pts require attendance + within-window + facilitator-confirmed; **facilitator = ordinary attendee (no bonus scalar)**; **per-session accrual, NOT aggregate-gated** (75% is a display KPI only); `annual_plc_target` is a target not a cap; track only the 8 PLC pts.
- **R373 `plc` group** — `type` **3-value CHECK `('subject','cross-cutting','new-teacher')` NO enum**; **`facilitator_user_id` nullable SET NULL** (store the user id, not a staff_id — the R377 identity hot path); nullable `cadence_override` (null=inherit); **`archived_at` soft-archive, NEVER hard-delete** (48/49 rows hang off it); NO room field, NO HoD role (facilitator is a manual staff assignment).
- **R374 `plc_membership`** open-row (`left_at IS NULL`=active), **M2M**, `UNIQUE(school_id,plc_id,user_id)`, **NO `role` column** (SUPERSEDES the surface's `plc_memberships.role` note — facilitator authority lives ONLY on `plc.facilitator_user_id`); assigning a facilitator auto-ensures their active membership.
- **R375 term focus** free text, `UNIQUE(school_id,plc_id,academic_period_id)`, **NO frozen canon, NO seed — school starts blank** (the sharp difference from VLC's 11 frozen values); no history table (OC7). ⇒ `lib/plc/defaults.ts` has NO value/canon library (only `coalescePlcProgramme` + type map + time helpers).
- **R376** mandatoriness/induction **DERIVE from `type`** (pure map, NO `mandatory`/`voluntary`/`induction` columns — OC6); CPD rate uniform across types; new-teacher NTC induction-milestone = omit-not-fake (deferred).
- **R377 `canFacilitatePlcSession`** (defined now, wired in 48) = own-PLC facilitator IDENTITY match (server-loaded `plc.facilitator_user_id`, NEVER from request) ∥ **`PLC_SESSION_BREAKGLASS_ROLES=[PD_COORDINATOR, HEADMASTER]`**. **VHA & ADMIN deliberately EXCLUDED** from break-glass (VHA wears the hat; ADMIN self-grants = audit-visible; the sickbay "ADMIN configures, never authors clinical content" boundary). The module's ONE IDOR fence — [[builds-widen-ratified-authz-and-self-bless]]; Sarah MUST mutation-test that no role alone satisfies the bare-facilitator arm.
- **R378/R379/R380** — all `plc_*` = FORCE+tenant_isolation+parent_deny (catalog, **NO parent_scope**), composite `(school_id,id)` FKs, **all SHOWN → must be listed in `SHOWN_AUDIT_ENTITIES`** (no prefix → omitted entity fails the classify-at-creation build guard); the ABSENCE of parent_scope/redaction/pastoral-gate is CORRECT, not a gap; omit-not-fake for an unconfigured school (NTC-sync badge, session/attendance tiles, "Generate next week's sessions", "of Y/gap" indicator all OMITTED, not faked); scope fence = config spine ONLY (no session/attendance/reflection/ledger/NTC).
- **Defaulted per Kofi rec (flag at PR):** 75% floor = display KPI (not an accrual gate); "staff in ≥1 PLC" denominator = teaching-staff (TEACHER/FORM_MASTER) base, not all-staff. **Wells: ~migration 0071 + prod-paste-0074 — confirm next free numbers at build (counters drifted across VLC).**

Append-only (no `updated_at`, create-only). **`open` is NOT a column — it DERIVES** from 42b flags (`resolved_at IS NULL`); the surface "2 open · 4 total" = count(notes) + count(unresolved flags). Index `(school_id, student_id)`. LEAF.
- **R327 · `vlc_pastoral_observation` — PG observation, FM-recorded, APPEND-ONLY, PG = DATA not a principal.** student composite FK, `observed_by` text NOT NULL ≤80 (the PG named as free-text DATA, `surfaced_by` precedent — NO `peer_guide_id`), `recorded_by_user_id` SET NULL (the FM), `body` NOT NULL ≤4000, `created_at`. Append-only. **PG reads NOTHING — structural** (no PG login/principal/read path). LEAF.
- **R328 · `vlc_pastoral_case` — ONE editable running summary per flag (1:1), NOT append-only.** `flag_id` composite FK → `vlc_pastoral_flag` (needs R331 tenant_uk), `summary` NOT NULL ≤8000, `opened_at`, `last_revised_at`, `last_revised_by_user_id` SET NULL. **`UNIQUE(school_id, flag_id)` → 1:1, lazily created.** EDITABLE by FM(own)+Dean (bump summary+last_revised); the SOLE non-append-only table. NO delete. LEAF.
- **R329 · READ gate + SOLE reader (extend `pastoral-data.ts`, STUDENT-fenced).** All 4 read through ONE extended reader (the sole content path). The journal page is per-student → fence by **`studentId`**: resolve student→class→`class_teacher_user_id`, role-gate `VLC_PASTORAL_READ_ROLES` + own-class narrowing (DEAN→any; FM→own-class students, server-loaded). A SINGLE aggregate `getStudentCasework` resolves the fence ONCE for all 4 projections. Display views only (abbreviated name, pre-formatted time, derived word count). Confirm no other query projects the 4 bodies (repo walk).
- **R330 · WRITE gate + the class-match assertion RETIRING the 42b deferral.** WRITE = `canWritePastoralFlag` re-checked server-side on EVERY create + case edit (Dean OR own-class FM on the target student's class; ADMIN/HM/other-class-FM/PG/student/parent refused). NO student/PG writer. **Retires 42b's deferred nit:** in `raisePastoralFlag`, when `sessionId` set, assert `vlc_session.class_id === flagged student's class_id`, reject on mismatch (single choke-point; the case inherits it via flag→session).
- **R331 · parent_deny + composite FKs + the flag `tenant_uk` (Wells).** All 4: ENABLE+FORCE+tenant_isolation+parent_deny (catalog, NO parent_scope). 🔴 **`vlc_pastoral_case.flag_id` composite FK → `vlc_pastoral_flag`, but 42b built the flag LEAF (no tenant_uk)** → the migration must FIRST `ADD vlc_pastoral_flag_tenant_uk UNIQUE(school_id, id)` (composite-FK target before the case FK ALTER — 0033/0057 ordering), THEN the case FK. **The flag tenant_uk ALTER must ALSO be in the prod-paste** (prod's flag came from hand-paste-0070, not a replayed migration — idempotent `ADD CONSTRAINT IF NOT EXISTS`). Migration 0069 + ONE prod-paste 0071. No triggers.
- **R332 · audit metadata-only, classify-guard GREEN.** Each create + case edit = ONE audit row (actionType/entityType `vlc_pastoral_*`/entityId/actor; NO body/summary/observed_by/student). All 4 `vlc_pastoral_*` → `isRedactedAuditEntity` true, guard green, ZERO SHOWN/REDACTED edits.

Surface reconciliations (owner wins): student "own reflections read-only" OMITTED (no login); PG "log own" OVERRIDDEN (FM records, observed_by data); "Dean cannot edit" = the 3 append-only tables have no editor, the case IS Dean-editable per the verbatim gate; the char-card auto-draft OMIT-NOT-FAKE.

## INCR-43 · Module 4.5 / VLC Student journal — the pastoral READ boundary (5 tables) — Pence decomposition · owner-calls RESOLVED (2-way split, staff-facing) → 43a building (2026-07-28)
- **✅ Owner decisions (2026-07-28):** **authorship = STAFF-FACING** — FM records journal entries + PG observations; the named PG is attributed via an `observed_by` DATA field (the 42b `surfaced_by` precedent); **NO student/PG logins** → the clean **2-way split**, module stays 100% staff-facing, `canAccessPastoralFlag` reused verbatim (→ no student self-read in 43, d2 moot). **Character paragraph = FM-AUTHORED free text, NO AI / NO auto-summary** (#6 — child-safety; the surface §01 "auto-generated" framing is OMIT-NOT-FAKED). Defaulted (Pence rec): (e1) case_file = one editable summary per flag; (e3) classify the non-prefixed tables REDACTED + redact the paragraph audit diff; (e4) paragraph editable-until-lock.
Pence: the split DEPTH is a function of ONE owner-call — **journal/PG authorship**. Default (FM-records, no student/PG logins → 100% staff-facing, reuse 42b's cleared gate verbatim) → **2-way split**; owner wants real student/PG authoring → **3-way split + a STOP-AND-ASK on SHS student/PG account provisioning** (new auth architecture, not in BUILD_STACK). Surface 04 `schoolup-vlc-student-journal.html`.
- **🔴 Pence finding — the REDACTED prefix does NOT auto-cover 4 of the 5 names.** `isRedactedAuditEntity` branches `startsWith("vlc_pastoral_")`; only `vlc_pastoral_note` matches. `vlc_journal_entry`/`vlc_pg_observation`/`vlc_case_file`/`vlc_character_paragraph` fall through → the classify-at-creation guard FAILS the build unless each is deliberately classified (name them `vlc_pastoral_*` OR add to `REDACTED_AUDIT_ENTITIES`). Kofi/Wells ruling (e3).
- **PRIMARY: 2-way split (default authorship = FM-recorded):**
  - **43a · VLC casework** — `vlc_journal_entry` + `vlc_pastoral_note` + `vlc_pg_observation` + `vlc_case_file`. ONE gate: `canAccessPastoralFlag` reused VERBATIM (own-class-FM-identity OR Dean); extend `lib/vlc/pastoral-data.ts` as the sole content reader over all 4 (same own-class join). All REDACTED + parent_deny. journal/note/pg-obs **append-only (hard — no edit/delete/backdate, no updated_at)**; case_file = one editable summary per flag. **Retires the 42b deferred class-match assertion** (`raisePastoralFlag`: session's class == student's class — lands here as the case-file hangs off the flag→session link). Security novelty ~1× (gate/reader/prefix/parent_deny all built + cleared).
  - **43b · VLC character paragraph** — `vlc_character_paragraph`, the ONE new gate shape: read = FM(own)+Dean **+ HEADMASTER (paragraph-only)**; write = FM authors/finalises; single row editable until `locked_at`, then frozen. Provenance (#6): FM-authored free text, NOT AI/auto-summary. The INCR-45 leaver-reference output. Isolated so Sarah gets a focused gate on the one table that widens the read set (failure mode = HM reaching journal content, an owner-#2 violation). **Redaction ≠ read-gate:** the paragraph's audit diff is REDACTED even though its app-read adds HM.
- **CONTINGENCY: 3-way split** (only if owner mandates student/PG logins): 43a journal+pg-obs (the write-only-no-read seam isolated), 43b note+case-file, 43c paragraph — plus the account-provisioning stop-and-ask.
- **Per-entity gate matrix (default):** journal_entry (CREATE FM-records / READ FM+Dean / REDACT-explicit / append-only-hard); pastoral_note (FM+Dean / FM+Dean / prefix✓ / append-only); pg_observation (FM-records, PG named via `observed_by` data / FM+Dean, **PG reads NOTHING** / REDACT-explicit / append-only); case_file (FM+Dean / FM+Dean / REDACT-explicit / editable-per-flag); character_paragraph (FM-authors / FM+Dean **+HM** / REDACT-diff-explicit / editable-until-lock). All parent_deny.
- **Critical path:** owner-calls → Kofi (AC + naming/classification rulings + case-file shape) → Lucy (map `docs/senior/vlc-student-journal-surface-map.md`, gate each section, omit-not-fake the "auto-generated paragraph" framing) → Wells (schema, migrations, one leak-critical prod-paste per table, composite FKs, the deferred assertion, the redaction classification) → build → Quinn/Dex/Sarah per sub-increment. 43a → 43b sequential (43b decorates 43a's `/senior/vlc/journal/<student>` page); independent branches off main, no stacked PRs.
- **Highest risk:** (1) the 42b deferred class-match assertion is a LIVE gap — must land in 43a; (2) [contingency] the PG-write-only-no-read seam; (3) the 43b HM-read exception (must be scoped to the paragraph ONLY — Quinn: HM sees paragraph AND HM=0 on the other 4); (4) the naming/redaction gotcha; (5) journal immutability (edit/delete/backdate all rejected).
- **🔴 OWNER-CALLS (batch):** **(d/b) authorship** [rec staff-facing: FM records journal + PG obs, PGs attributed via `observed_by` data, NO student/PG logins → 2-way split; contingency = student/PG logins → stop-and-ask + 3-way]; **(#6) character paragraph** [rec FM-authored free text, NO AI/auto-summary — child-safety; surface §01 "auto-generated" is overridden, omit-not-fake]. Defaulted (Pence rec, confirm at PR): (e1) case_file = one editable summary per flag; (e3) classify the 4 non-prefixed tables REDACTED + redact the paragraph's audit diff; (e4) paragraph editable-until-lock; (d2) no student self-read in 43 (no student login to read from). LOCKED (implement, don't ask): #2 (FM+Dean content, HM paragraph-only, ADMIN barred), #4 (parents none), #1 (DEAN role).

Cols: `id`, `school_id`, `class_id` (composite FK), `session_template_id` (composite FK → `vlc_session_template.tenant_uk` — value + slot A/B DERIVE through it, NO `value_id`), `session_date` (stored date), `held_by_user_id` (SET NULL), `created_at`/`updated_at`. `tenant_uk UNIQUE(school_id, id)` INLINE (FK target for attendance). `UNIQUE(school_id, class_id, session_date)` (natural key + upsert). **NO `programme_id`** (school singleton), **NO `academic_period_id`** (derives from date), **NO phase/duration/`started_at`** (R311), **NO `status`/`locked`/`closed`** (R312).
- **R311 · phase timings DERIVE — never re-store.** Lifecycle bar + agenda windows compute from `coalesceVlcProgramme(programme).session_start` + the 5 frozen `VLC_PHASES` durations via `addMinutes` (exactly the F0 `endTime` derivation). The live "12 min in / phases 2/5" clock is client-side ephemeral. Agenda "what" = frozen `VLC_PHASES.description` + `vlc_session_template.prompt` (F0 editorial), NOT per-session stored text.
- **R312 · lifecycle/status DERIVED, no stored enum.** "Held" = the row exists. "Auto-locked at 3:33" = DERIVED from `session_date` + programme window vs now (app-layer late-edit guard, NOT a stored `locked`).
- **R313 · `vlc_session_attendance` — present-by-default P/L/A (prep_attendance idiom).** A row exists ONLY for a non-present student; **PRESENT is never a row** (the parent `vlc_session` confirms the register was taken). Cols: `id`, `school_id`, `session_id` (composite FK → `vlc_session.tenant_uk`), `student_id` (composite FK → students), `status` (**reuses canonical `attendanceStatusEnum`** — capture shows P/L/A per surface; E/M storable-not-rejected so the M-not-A seam + future Sickbay hook stay open, but 42a builds NO such control), `minutes_late` (nullable), `note` (nullable), `recorded_by_user_id` (SET NULL), `created_at`. `UNIQUE(school_id, session_id, student_id)` (upsert). LEAF (no tenant_uk). **FM-only write** (owner d) via shared form-resolver (own-class). The "PG-gold marked first" highlight DERIVES from the INCR-41 roster — NO `marked_by_pg` column, NO PG on the row.
- **R314 · small groups = DERIVED / EPHEMERAL — NO table in 42a.** 2 PGs → 2 groups is structural (derives from roster). Per-student membership has no 42a reader (client-side split); 42b's flag carries group context as free text, not an FK. The "idea brought back" brief has no 42a reader → NOT built (INCR-43 candidate). YAGNI.
- **R315 · no derived scalars.** NOWHERE store present_count/attendance_rate/late_count/phase_current/summary-status. present = enrolled − ABSENT rows (LATE is a present sub-state); rate/counts/phases all derive. (Surface "36 PRESENT · 90% · 2 late · 4 absent" reproduces from 40 enrolled + 2 LATE + 4 ABSENT rows.)
- **R316 · SHOWN + scope fence.** Add `vlc_session` + `vlc_session_attendance` to `SHOWN_AUDIT_ENTITIES` (operational, like `attendance`/`prep_attendance`); neither is `vlc_pastoral_` → both MUST be listed explicitly or the classify-at-creation guard fails the build. 🔴 **Hard scope fence — 42a builds NONE of:** `vlc_pastoral_flag` (42b), any `VLC_PASTORAL_*` gate (42b), the `startsWith("vlc_pastoral_")` REDACTED branch (42b), a reflection/journal table (INCR-43), a PG-write path (owner c → INCR-43), a facilitation-points/`vlc_character_paragraph` table (INCR-43), a small-group/project-brief table (R314), a Sickbay→VLC hook. The pastoral-flag callout, reflection meter, and PG points are OMITTED-not-faked (Lucy).
- **Read/write gates (REUSE, no new group).** WRITE = the session's-class **Form Master** (own-class, owner d). READ = `VLC_CONFIG_READ_ROLES` (operational/SHOWN — school-wide staff read fine; own-class scoping is a 42b/flag concern). No `VLC_SESSION_*` alias. **Build-directed DRY (roadmap):** extract the shared senior form-resolver (4th copy) + `lib/vlc/authz.ts`, fix the redundant page `getCurrentUser()`.
- **Wells:** 2 tenant tables, migration ~0069 + prod-paste; ENABLE+FORCE+`tenant_isolation`+`parent_deny` (catalog) + composite `(school_id,X)` FKs; `vlc_session.tenant_uk` INLINE; the two UNIQUEs; no derived-duplicate/status/started_at columns; no triggers. Fix the STALE `vlc.ts` "for INCR-41's vlc_session" comments (vlc_session lands HERE).
- **Lucy:** re-route under `/senior/vlc` (3rd tab); head-row + facilitator strip (FM + 2 PGs derived) + lifecycle bar (derived windows) + focus banner (F0 value/template) + attendance grid (P/L/A, present-by-default, PG-gold derived) + agenda timeline (F0 phase copy). Omit-not-fake the pastoral-flag callout, reflection meter, PG points.
- **AC VLC42a-1..17 (Quinn):** 1 one session per (class,date) upsert; 2 refs template not value, no programme_id/academic_period_id (grep); 3 phase timings derive, no phase/started_at col; 4 no stored status; 5 auto-lock derives (write after window refused); 6 present-by-default (40 class, 2 late+4 absent = 6 rows); 7 canonical enum reused not forked, capture P/L/A only; 8 attendance composite FKs + UNIQUE(session,student) upsert; 9 FM-only write (student/PG/other-class-FM/non-FM refused); 10 PG-gold derives, no marked_by_pg col; 11 no small-group/project table (grep); 12 rate derives (36/90%/2late/4absent from rows); 13 no derived scalars (grep); 14 SHOWN both + classify guard green; 15 RLS wrong-school 0 + cross-school INSERT rejected + no-GUC 0; 16 parent_deny 0 both; 17 scope fence (no pastoral_flag/VLC_PASTORAL_/redaction-branch/journal/PG-write/points/small-group).
- **🔶 Owner-calls (both NON-blocking, DEFAULTED):** (minor) live phase windows render from the *scheduled* start (demo "started 3 min late" shows 2:30-based windows) — rec DERIVE, add a nullable `started_at` later only if a truthful live clock matters (no migration pain). (note) FM-only write = no substitute/ADMIN break-glass co-writer in 42a — rec keep FM-only (loosening later is safe). Flag at PR.

Surface 02 `schoolup-vlc-peer-guides.html` (`/pastoral/vlc/peer-guides` on the surface; Lucy re-routes under `/senior/vlc`). **OPERATIONAL, SHOWN audit, NO pastoral PII.** Peer Guides = senior students (2/class, Forms F2–F3, one boy + one girl by default, one-semester tenure) elected by classmates (FM approval + Dean sign-off) to facilitate VLC small-groups — a visible, prefect-like student-leadership role, NOT confidential counselling. The confidential pastoral graph (`vlc_pastoral_flag`, journal, PG-first *session* attendance) is **INCR-42/43 — OUT OF SCOPE.** Builds `vlc_peer_guide` (append-only appointment roster) + `vlc_training` (Dean-authored training events) + `vlc_training_absence` (present-by-default PG training attendance). Reuses the F0 `DEAN_OF_STUDENTS` role + `VLC_CONFIG_*_ROLES` gates — NO new role, NO new access group. Next migration (Wells; ~0068) + hand-run `prod-paste-00xx-vlc-peer-guides.sql` (verify `verify-prod-rls.sql`). No triggers — all cross-row validation in `lib/vlc/`.
- **R301 · "class" + eligibility + cap + gender.** A "class" = a `classes` row (form-class/stream: "Form 2 Eunyam", "Form 3 GA" — the same unit attendance/gradebook use), NOT a form-level. Eligible = `classes` whose resolved SHS form is **Form 2 or Form 3**; **Form 1 excluded** ("students receive, they don't lead in their first year" — surface `.no-pg-note`), and any non-F2/F3 form is ineligible. **"2 per class" = a hard cap of 2 *active* PGs per (class × current period) on the write path AND the target for vacancy flagging** (vacancy = <2). Over-appointment (3rd active) is refused. **1 boy + 1 girl is ADVISORY, not enforced**: rep-gender DERIVES from `students.sex`, the 1+1 target is a read-time flag, appointing two same-sex PGs is NOT refused. No stored slot-gender column.
- **R302 · the `vlc_peer_guide` record (append-only, bunk_allocation idiom).** Columns: `student_id` (composite FK → students, CASCADE), `class_id` (composite FK → classes — the *constituency* class), `academic_period_id` (composite FK → academic_period — tenure scope), `appointed_at`, `appointed_by_user_id` (single-col SET NULL → users), `ended_at` (nullable — **NULL = currently serving**, bunk_allocation open-row idiom), `ended_reason` (nullable, optional operational note). `tenant_uk UNIQUE(school_id, id)` **inline**. Partial `UNIQUE(school_id, student_id, academic_period_id) WHERE ended_at IS NULL`. **NO derived-duplicate scalars** (no count, no gender-balance, no `status`, no `training_completed_at`). Never DELETE; vacate = set `ended_at`; fill = INSERT a new row.
- **R303 · tenure = one academic_period** (SHS semester), NOT the VLC 3-arc block. No expiry job — the appointment scopes out of the next period by its `academic_period_id`. `ended_at` set ONLY on early departure. Re-selection in a later period allowed (partial unique is period-scoped). "Rotating after T2" is a DERIVED forward state.
- **R304 · who manages + gates (REUSE F0, no new group).** WRITE = `VLC_CONFIG_WRITE_ROLES=[DEAN_OF_STUDENTS,ADMIN]` (the Dean owns the roster; the FM vote is OFFLINE, so FM gets NO roster-write). READ = `VLC_CONFIG_READ_ROLES=[DEAN,ADMIN,HM,FM]` (operational/SHOWN, school-wide FM read fine). Do NOT mint `VLC_ROSTER_*` aliases.
- **R305 · training records (two entities, minimal).** `vlc_training` = Dean-authored monthly event: `academic_year` (text), `scheduled_date`, `title`, `description?`, `duration_min`. NO stored attendance/status. `tenant_uk` inline. `vlc_training_absence` = **present-by-default** (prep_attendance idiom): one row per (training × PG) ONLY for a non-present PG, composite FKs, optional `excused` (default false), optional `note`, `recorded_by_user_id` (SET NULL), `UNIQUE(school_id, training_id, peer_guide_id)`. Attendance % DERIVED.
- **R306 · "attendance" boundary (hard scope fence).** INCR-41 attendance = **PG TRAINING attendance ONLY.** NOT the VLC class-*session* register — `vlc_session` + student P/L/A is **INCR-42, OUT OF SCOPE.** INCR-41 introduces NO `vlc_session` table. (Doc-hygiene: the `vlc.ts` "authored-ahead … for INCR-41's vlc_session" comments are a STALE label — vlc_session lands at INCR-42; fix the comment when Wells is in the file.)
- **R307 · vacancy protocol (DERIVED, append-only — no vacancy row).** Vacancy DERIVED: eligible class with <2 active PGs in the current period. Into-vacancy = a step-aside sets `ended_at`. Out-of-vacancy = INSERT a replacement row scoped to the SAME current `academic_period_id` (winner serves the remainder, automatic). The candidate-nomination + class-vote workflow (≤4 candidates, ballot, tally) is **NOT built** — see OC2.
- **R308 · audit = SHOWN.** Add `vlc_peer_guide`, `vlc_training`, `vlc_training_absence` to `SHOWN_AUDIT_ENTITIES` (operational, no PII). NONE uses the reserved `vlc_pastoral_` prefix → they MUST be explicitly listed or the INCR-30/31 classify-at-creation guard fails the build. `ended_reason` stays operational (confidential welfare detail belongs in INCR-43).
- **R309 · parent/tenant visibility.** Owner decision #4 LOCKED — parents see NOTHING VLC-wide. Each table gets ENABLE + FORCE + `tenant_isolation` + `parent_deny` via the catalog loop. Composite `(school_id, …)` FKs.

(2) **Signup:** owned schools (ownership ≠ PUBLIC → PRIVATE/MISSION/INTERNATIONAL) → creator = **PROPRIETOR + ADMIN** (founder can delegate admin or keep it); PUBLIC/GES → ADMIN only. - **R280 escalation guard (the crux):** one shared pure `canGrantRole(actorRoles, code)` = `rankOf([code]) ≤ rankOf(actorRoles)`, enforced SERVER-SIDE at ALL 4 grant sites — `addStaff`, `assignStaffRole`, `importStaff` (per row), `createInvite` (+ `acceptInvite` re-check via the inviter's rank for a directly-inserted row). An ADMIN(2) refused on PROPRIETOR(3); ADMIN→ADMIN(2≤2) preserved; only a PROPRIETOR grants PROPRIETOR.
- **R281 signup:** conditional `roleValues.push(PROPRIETOR)` gated `ownership !== "PUBLIC"` in `onboarding.ts` + seed the `ref_role` PROPRIETOR label.
- **R282 picker:** do NOT add PROPRIETOR to the static `STAFF_ROLES` array (would be visible-but-forbidden); optional `STAFF_ROLE_LABEL` "Proprietor" entry. **R285:** no last-proprietor guard (a school may validly have zero).
- **AC P1–P10** (Kofi); the escalation guard P2 (all 4 paths, incl. hand-crafted/CSV "PROPRIETOR") is the highest-value test. L1/L2b invariants (rankOf/canManageTarget) stay byte-identical (P9).
- **Wells: none** (empty schema diff, P10). **Lucy: none** (no new surface). **Spec locked → building.**
Owner-directed: give the PROPRIETOR role (registered inert in L1, top-rank in L2b's user-mgmt) its complete cross-module permission model + assignment path. Owner's framing (2026-07-26): "the owner role can play dual or tri roles such as owner + headmaster/admin" → PROPRIETOR is an OWNERSHIP/governance role, composable with operational roles, NOT necessarily an operational superset.
- **What exists today:** `PROPRIETOR` ∈ `KNOWN_APP_ROLES` (inert — in NO `lib/access.ts` group); top rank in `rankOf`/`canManageTarget` + `USER_ADMIN_ROLES` (can block/reset/activate everyone, un-blockable by admin/headmaster); NOT in `STAFF_ROLES` picker; NOT assigned at signup.
- **The design questions (Kofi to ground + surface as OWNER CALLs):** (1) **permission model** — governance/oversight-only (owns the school, sees roll-ups, manages users + role grants; operational writes need a held ADMIN/HEADMASTER) vs operational SUPERSET (does everything admin/headmaster can + governance). (2) **assignment path** — who can grant PROPRIETOR? (only another PROPRIETOR, to prevent an ADMIN minting a super-role = escalation; or set at signup). (3) **signup** — private schools' creator = PROPRIETOR (+ ADMIN/HEADMASTER)? public/GES schools have NO proprietor (govt-owned, headmaster appointed) → gate on school type/ownership. (4) which `lib/access.ts` groups PROPRIETOR joins (if superset). (5) a governance/oversight SURFACE (a proprietor dashboard) or none. (6) schema — likely seam/catalog-only (roles are free-text `ref_role.code`); confirm no migration.
- **Ghana context:** proprietor/proprietress = the owner/founder of a PRIVATE school; public/GES schools are government-owned (no proprietor).
- **Spec (Kofi) in progress → owner calls back to me.**
Kofi R271–R278 + Lucy map `docs/senior/l3-forgot-password-surface-map.md`. **SEAM-ONLY — no schema/migration/prod-paste** (Supabase's `resetPasswordForEmail` owns the recovery token). Built by claude-code (`1810e51`) + my route-handler fix (`7ff6a72`).
- **Seam** (`lib/auth`): `sendPasswordResetEmail`, `establishRecoverySession` (PKCE `exchangeCodeForSession`), `sessionAuthMethods` (`amr` decode, mirrors `sessionIdFromJwt`); reuses `updatePassword`. All dev-bypass no-op.
- **Actions** (`lib/actions/auth.ts`): `requestPasswordReset` (NEUTRAL-ALWAYS → enumeration-safe, R273), `verifyResetOtp` (no redirect, unlike `verifyLogin`), `completePasswordReset` (newPassword only — NO target id R274; min-8; **R276 walk-up guard** — refuses a password-only-`amr` session, FAIL-OPEN on unreadable `amr`).
- **Phone path** (works, dev-testable): `/reset` → phone → OTP → verifyResetOtp → set-new-password → `/dashboard`. **Email path**: reset email → **`/auth/reset-callback` Route Handler exchanges the PKCE code (cookie persists — the SC-render cookie-drop bug claude-code caught + I fixed)** → `/reset-password` reads the session → set-new-password → `/login?reset=1`.
- **Surface** (Lucy, net-new — no authored surface): "Forgot password?" on the login Password tab; `/reset` (`ResetForm`); `/reset-password`; shared `SetNewPassword` (New+Confirm, no current-password).
- **Verified:** tsc clean, 1089 tests + `lib/auth-l3.test.ts` (11, incl. the route-handler lock), build exit 0, `/reset` + `/reset-password` render.
- **🔶 Flags for the gates / Sarah:** (1) **R276 amr guard is FAIL-OPEN** on empty/unreadable `amr` (avoids locking out legit resets) — Sarah to require-verify vs accept-ceiling; (2) the exact GoTrue `amr` method strings couldn't be runtime-confirmed (no live Supabase) — coded to the documented shape; (3) the **email path is structurally-verified only** (no real recovery link in dev) — live verification needs a real Supabase env; (4) `/login?reset=1` banner not wired (inert-harmless, per "don't change the login card").
- **Gates pending: Quinn + Dex → Sarah.**
The last piece of Module L. Owner call (2026-07-26): reset via **BOTH** phone OTP and email link. A "Forgot password?" affordance at login (`components/auth/login-form.tsx`) → a reset flow that proves identity then lets the user set a new password (reusing L2a's `updatePassword`).
- **Phone OTP path:** enter phone → `signInWithPhone` (OTP) → `verifyPhoneOtp` (establishes session) → set a new password (`updatePassword`). Reuses existing OTP infra + the L2a self primitive — likely NO new schema.
- **Email link path:** enter email → a reset link. **Open (Kofi/Wells): Supabase's built-in `resetPasswordForEmail` (portable via the `lib/auth` seam, no token table, sends a link to a redirect page where `updateUser` sets the password) vs a custom invite-style token** (needs a table + email send). Prefer the built-in if it fits the seam/portability posture.
- **Security (Kofi/Sarah):** enumeration resistance (don't reveal whether a phone/email exists — respond the same either way), rate-limiting posture (note the ceiling; Supabase throttles OTP), console-degrade SMS (never provision Hubtel), the reset completion sets the CURRENT session's password only (no target id — same structural guarantee as L2a).
- Surfaces (Lucy): the "Forgot password?" link on the login card + the reset page(s). NOT `schoolup-archive-password-recovery` (that's data-export archive) / `schoolup-2fa-enrolment` (2FA) — likely net-new auth screens or an extension of the login surface.
- **Spec (Kofi) + surface (Lucy) in progress.**
